**Gates, CI and process (binding on every Phase 9 agent)**

31. **The orchestrator's process decisions after Workflows 8A and 8B bind Phase 9.** (a) A commit ends with the `Claude-Session` line its own session's system reminder gives, when one does, never another session's. (b) Agents that run beside other agents (Stage 2 and 3 builders, 9C's Stage 1 builders, 9D's `fix-flow` and `fix-console`) run only their own packages' gates and no e2e gate; contracts, foundation and integration agents (and 9D's `a11y-audit` and `fix-api`, which run alone) keep the whole-repo gates, integration alone on the final tree. (c) A console builder runs its own full-stack spec files as one call that holds the e2e lock for the whole sequence: `flock -w 60 /tmp/openverify-e2e.lock timeout 520 sh -c "<stack down> && <turbo build> && <stack up> && OPENVERIFY_E2E_REUSE_STACK=1 <its specs>; rc=$?; <stack down>; exit $rc"`, one call per spec file when they do not fit, never a raised limit. (d) `pnpm test:integration` runs as `pnpm --filter @openverify/db test:integration`, then from `apps/api` `--project graph`, then `--project api --shard=i/3`, every call part of the gate. (e) A spec that adds an OTP send presents an address of its own (`presentAddress`, `withForwardedFor`). (f) Every call fits the 10-minute cap, lock waits included.
32. **The unit gate's recipe** (Phase 8 amendment 125). The API suite runs as blob shards in `<dir>/blobs`, each coverage run writing to its own directory outside it, merged with `--merge-reports=<dir>/blobs`; a lone shard exits 1 on its partial coverage and the merge is the gate. Four shards beside other agents, two alone.
33. **Numbers at `f0a32761`.** API 8,253 tests in 584 files (branches 85.87%); integration db 78 tests, graph 13 (its template built in 16.4 s of its 180 s guard), api shards 75, 91 and 75; the mocked flow suite 79 tests; the full-stack suite 137 tests in 66 files, three shards of 46, 46 and 45 in 4.2, 5.3 and 4.6 minutes plus 1.3 for the stack start, 9 OTP sends from `127.0.0.1` a full pass (a used stack holds one clean pass); vision 103 test files, split at 58; SDK host tests 1,334.
34. **CI.** 13 jobs: `lint` 30 minutes (one package at a time under a 4 GB heap), `node` 25 (since `1f0510b9`; typecheck at `--concurrency=2` under the same heap), `unit-api` two shards of 40 each (since `a68b4279`), `unit-api-coverage` 20, `unit-dashboard` 20, `format` 10, `integration` 30, `infra` 10 (it runs `code-runner.test.sh`, which builds an image), `vision` 30, `android` 45 (floor 1,334), `android-instrumented` 60, `e2e` 70 with Playwright's 45-minute `globalTimeout`, `secrets` 10. Phase 8 adopted no Gradle lock, so Phase 10's form stays.
35. **The Validation order** gains Phase 8's lines: `workflow-docs:write` with `git diff --exit-code docs/workflows` beside `openapi:write`, `docker build -f infra/docker/code-runner.Dockerfile .` beside the apps and vision images, and the workflow benchmark (`apps/api/scripts/workflow-benchmark.ts`, reported, not gated, under the e2e lock).
36. **Release age.** Neither `pnpm-workspace.yaml` nor `services/vision/pyproject.toml` sets a release age at `f0a32761`, so 9A Stage 1a still adds both. Phase 8's newest pins are each at least 14 days old by 9A's date gate (2026-11-03T02:25Z): `bullmq` 6.3.14 (2026-10-13), `@axe-core/playwright` 4.14.0 (2026-10-15), `axe-core` 4.14.0, `@xyflow/react` 12.12.0, `@dagrejs/dagre` 3.1.1, `@panzoom/panzoom` 4.6.2, `temporal-polyfill` 1.0.5, `quickjs-emscripten-core` 0.32.0.
37. **The format gate's trap** (an owner item since 8B). `.prettierignore`'s bare `services/` and `data/` patterns match at any depth, so `pnpm format:check` skips `apps/api/src/services/` and `packages/*/src/data/`. No agent edits `.prettierignore` (a config-protection hook refuses it); a file written there is checked with `prettier --check --ignore-path=.gitignore <file>`, and lines of an existing file there are not reformatted beyond the change.

**Phase 7 as built after 7C**

38. **7C's facts the flow and platform read.** `flowHandoffSchema` has `handed-off` (K7A-5) and `flowSessionSignalsSchema` `next-sequence` (K7A-7); `bot_score` and `behavior_threat_level` are at version 2 (K7A-12), so a change of `IDENTITY_FIELD_STEP_TYPES` takes `bot_score` to 3; today's SDK finalises at `FlowCoordinator.kt:668`, `:671`, `:714`, `:717` and `:868` (after K7A-6), not the `:598-600` of C151.6; `transactions` and `transaction_files` are in `SUBJECT_PII_TABLES` (K7B-1); the transaction type and graph query template version routes (K7B-3), status changes and labels (K7B-4) and relations (K7C-2) are served, with `@openverify/node`'s `transactionTypeVersions` and `graphQueryTemplateVersions`; `explorer-url` is nullable (K7B-11); `filter[identifier]` is still served without a PII check (the 7B refresh's 9B default closes it), and the console never sends it.

## Workflow 9A Stage 1a amendments (contracts-9a)

Recorded by `contracts-9a` on 2026-11-04 at `e56e4e9c`, the Phase 9 base, whose code equals `f0a32761`. The full inventory is `docs/research/phase9-inventory.md`, and every claim below carries its `path:line` there. **Workflow 9A stopped at Stage 1a on amendment 1**, as C151.6 and the Stage 1a bullet require for a red projection test. Nothing of C147-C179 is built, and every 9A checkpoint is recorded NOT BUILT in `docs/tdd/phase9-contracts-9a.tdd.md`. Amendments 2 onwards are the drift a rerun of Stage 1a builds on.

1. **PROJECTION RULES WRONG (C151.6, V02, V03; owner decision needed before 9A can run).**
   - **What the plan assumed.** C151.6 leaves a verification step exited through `exhausted` out of the projection, assuming that today's SDK then walks from the projection's list and reaches the fallback step.
   - **What the SDK does.** It does not walk from the list. After every processed attempt, `FlowCoordinator` re-reads the session and calls `continueFrom(work.step)`, which resolves from **the step object it worked on** (`FlowCoordinator.kt:206-207`, `:410`, `:792`, `:920`, `:940`, `:1013`, `:1065-1066`, `:1150`, `:557-588`). A government ID step whose attempts are used up therefore resolves to `Resolution.Finalize` through `verificationState` (`:668`, or `:671` for a failure without hints), whatever the new projection lists. `finalize` then polls `pollAttempts` times and shows `Error(STILL_PROCESSING)` (`:1210-1220`).
   - **The evidence.** A host test over the unchanged SDK and the existing `FakeFlowApi` was run with `./gradlew :openverify-sdk:testAndroidHostTest --tests ...FlowCoordinatorProjectionTest`. It served the successive projections exactly as C151.6 writes them and gave these results:
     - (a) consent → government ID → selfie → completion: passed, with no `Completed` before the completion step;
     - (b) consent → government ID → a branch into a v2-only step: passed, and the SDK stops on `Unsupported` at the `server-continuation` placeholder;
     - (c) consent → government ID exhausted → document → completion: **failed**, with the states `Processing(id_card) → Finalizing(id_card) → Error(STILL_PROCESSING, canRetry=true)`.

     The source and the report are in `docs/tdd/phase9-contracts-9a.tdd.md`. No projection-only rule can avoid this while the person is on the step: the finalisation depends only on the step object already held and the session's `verifications`, and the server must report a failed verification as failed.
   - **The options.** Each keeps the test's assertions as they are, except (iii).
     - (i) **The candidate rule, proved green in the same run.** A verification step that has an `exhausted` edge has no v1 form. It is served to spec-1 clients as a `server-continuation` placeholder, wherever it would appear: as the current step, or in the unconditional run after it. Today's SDK therefore stops on its unsupported screen before such a step, as it already does before a `screen`, `branch` or `action`. The test's case (c) then asserts that the SDK stops on `Unsupported` at the government ID's position, with no `Finalizing` and no `Completed`, and that no verification is created. The consequence: a version that uses a fallback does not run on today's Android SDK until Phase 10 (C175.1 already lists `exhausted` routes for Phase 10), and C151.7's publish warning names fallbacks beside screens, actions, waits and custom consent copy.
     - (ii) The server takes the `exhausted` port only for clients that declared spec 2. This is refused here, because it contradicts C151.7 ("negotiation changes only what is sent, never the path").
     - (iii) Accept today's behaviour: `Finalizing`, then `STILL_PROCESSING`, then the person's retry (`load`) reaches the fallback step, because `FlowNavigator.next` falls back to the list's first step for an id it does not hold (`FlowNavigator.kt:34-35`). This changes the test's assertion, which the plan forbids an agent to do; it is the owner's to take.
   - **Default proposed:** (i). Until the owner decides, Workflow 9A does not start Stage 1b or Stage 2.
2. **Stale references.**
   - The SDK finalises at `FlowCoordinator.kt:668`, `:671`, `:714`, `:717` and `:868`, as Phase 8 outcome 38 says; the plan's `:598-600` is stale.
   - The steps the SDK passes as done are a recorded consent (`:560`), and a verification whose latest attempt is `PASSED`, `SKIPPED` or `NEEDS_REVIEW` (`:652-653`, `:701`, `:866`), and a `device_handoff` whose state is done (`:587`).
   - Other stale lines: compose's `flow` service is at `infra/docker-compose.yml:533-553`; the flow configuration is at `apps/api/src/config.ts:550-551`, `:1022`, `:1148` and `:1158`; the `require-mfa` default is at `packages/db/src/tables/tenancy.ts:31` and `packages/contracts/src/dashboard-api.ts:373`.
   - Permissions now count 58 API scopes and 9 operator permissions.
3. **`trigger-flow` and actions' `triggers` (C152.2, review F05).** `ActionRegistryEntry.triggers: null` serves **every** trigger type (`packages/workflow-engine/src/actions/registry.ts:164-165`). The graph check refuses a misfit only for a non-null list (`graph/check.ts:474`), and the console's palette does the same (`palette-model.ts:161`). So adding `trigger-flow` to `WORKFLOW_TRIGGER_TYPES` would let every `null` action serve flows, against "none until listed". The (S) deliverable must make `null` mean "every trigger type but `trigger-flow`" in the registry's documented rule, the graph check, the catalogue served at `GET /workflow-actions` (`apps/api/src/workflows/runs/catalogue.ts:64`) and the palette, test-first, with Phase 8's suites green. The alternative, an explicit list on every action, touches every catalogue entry.
4. **The decision record's flow-failure form (outcome 5).**
   - `DECISION_EVIDENCE_KINDS` lives in the `evidence` jsonb with no CHECK (`packages/db/src/tables/decisions.ts:88`), so the new evidence kinds need no migration.
   - The actor's id format is checked by `deciderIdFormat` (`tables/decisions.ts:137`), so `foundation-9a` (S) must recreate both `inquiry_decisions_actor_type_valid` and `inquiry_decisions_actor_id_format` for the actor `inquiry-flow` with an `inq_` id.
5. **v2 flows fail verifications until the reader lands.** `jobs/process-verification.ts:189` parses with `flowSchema.safeParse` and throws `PermanentJobError('invalid_template')` on any other shape, so every verification of an inquiry pinned to a v2 version would fail. `foundation-9a`'s v2-safe reader must cover it (outcome 10) before any v2 version can be published, with a test.
6. **Publish contexts on a v2 flow.** `PublishContext.flow` (`services/publish/index.ts:37`), `signal-config.ts:40` and `handoff.ts:24` are typed on the strict v1 `Flow`. v2 steps of v1 types carry additive optional members that the v1 step schemas refuse, and `screen`, `branch` and `action` are new. So the v2-safe reader hands v1 consumers the v2 steps of v1 types without those members (or the consumers move to a shared step view), and treats `screen`, `branch` and `action` as unknown to them.
7. **The handoff step's position.** `currentHandoffStep` (`services/handoff/steps.ts`, called at `services/handoff/flow-session.ts:50`) finds the current `device_handoff` step by walking the v1 list. On a v2 flow it reads the server's position. The exits C151.2 calls "Phase 7's handoff resolved" are `continueHandoffHere`, `redeemHandoff`, `rejectCurrentHandoff` and `declineHandoff` (`services/handoff/flow-handoffs.ts:567`, `:1009`, `:641`, `:939`), all in `services/handoff/`.
8. **Verification decision sites (C151.2).** `advanceFlowInTx` belongs in four places:
   - `processVerification`'s decision transaction (`jobs/process-verification.ts:335-409`, after the decision hooks at `:399-407`);
   - `failVerificationAfterError`'s transaction (`:506-547`);
   - the silent AAMVA skip, which runs inside the first site (`jobs/types/aamva.ts:232`);
   - and, for exclusion only, Phase 8's sandbox `run_verification` with `debug-forced-status` (`workflows/actions/verifications/run-verification.ts:125`), which decides a verification created outside the flow and is never the current step's attempt (C151.4).

   `requiredVerifications` has one call site (`services/inquiry-settlement.ts:73`).
9. **Patterns in v1.** `flowV1Schema` is today's `flowSchema` unchanged, and its form field `pattern` is validated with `new RegExp(pattern, 'u')` (`packages/flow-spec/src/flow.ts:69-76`). Rule 18's "no `new RegExp` on operator text" therefore applies to v2 only; v1 drafts keep their check until migrated.
10. **`IDENTITY_FIELD_STEP_TYPES`** (`packages/checks/src/signals/bot-score.ts:67-74`) gains no `screen`. A paste into a v2 screen's identity field counts as no evidence, `bot_score` stays at version 2, and its digest is unchanged. Adding `screen` is a version-3 change for a later owner decision.
11. **Flow-provisional signals (C150.9, decision 68).** True for every computed signal whose inputs are network, device or session facts. False for `inquiry/country_comparison` (it reads `government-id-extraction`) and for `inquiry/list_match_count` (it reads `lists`, is not in `COMPUTED_SIGNAL_NAMES`, and is merged when served). The client-collected ones are the three with collection tier `session-integrity`: `bot_score`, `behavior_threat_level` and `automation_artefact_count`.
12. **Directional icons.** `apps/flow/src` holds none: the only SVG is the QR code. `flow-i18n`'s `mirrored` flag is therefore a helper or a rule for the icons `flow-renderer` adds; there is no icon file to borrow.
13. **Release age (decision 62).** Neither `pnpm-workspace.yaml` nor `services/vision/pyproject.toml` sets one at the base, and `services/vision/uv.lock` has no `[options]` table. The date gate held (2026-11-04T09:14Z). The two settings and their tests were **not** committed, because the Workflow stopped. A rerun of Stage 1a adds them first, exactly as its bullet says.
14. **`eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y`.** 6.10.2 still peers ESLint up to 9, so decision 54 stands.

**Rerun of Stage 1a (2026-11-04, after the owner's decision on amendment 1).** Items 15 onwards are the rerun's, recorded as each family lands. Commits from `e9bca8cf`; the checkpoint shas are in `docs/tdd/phase9-contracts-9a.tdd.md`.

15. **Release age, both package managers (decision 62).** `pnpm-workspace.yaml` sets `minimumReleaseAge: 20160` (strict when explicit) and nothing else of the kind; `packages/config/src/workspace-conventions.test.ts` fails a different value, a `minimumReleaseAgeExclude` key, `minimumReleaseAgeStrict: false` or `trustLockfile: true`, and `turbo.json` adds `$TURBO_ROOT$/pnpm-workspace.yaml` to `@openverify/config#test`'s inputs. `services/vision/pyproject.toml` sets `[tool.uv] exclude-newer = "14 days"`; `uv lock` (uv 0.13.3, no flag) wrote exactly `[options] exclude-newer = "0001-01-01T00:00:00Z"` (uv's no-op, with its own comment) and `exclude-newer-span = "P14D"`, and no version or artifact moved; `services/vision/tests/test_dependencies.py::test_uv_holds_every_resolution_to_a_relative_14_day_release_age` fails an absolute cutoff or an `exclude-newer-package`. The frozen install and `uv lock --check`, `uv sync --locked` pass. The dated record is `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md`'s new "Version age" paragraph. **CLAUDE.md was not edited** (the harness this Workflow ran in allows no agent to change it): the owner replaces its "Dependency versions" bullet "pnpm 12 enforces a minimum release age. If it adds `minimumReleaseAgeExclude` entries, remove them and pin an older version instead." with: "Every release must be at least 14 days old, and both package managers are configured to enforce it: `minimumReleaseAge: 20160` in `pnpm-workspace.yaml` (strict, so a young pick fails the install, and every install re-checks the lockfile) and `[tool.uv] exclude-newer = "14 days"` in `services/vision/pyproject.toml` (recorded in `uv.lock` as `exclude-newer-span = "P14D"`; never pass `--exclude-newer` on a command line). A young pick is never excluded (no `minimumReleaseAgeExclude`, no `exclude-newer-package`): pin an older version, or wait."
16. **Common, as built.** Ids `theset`, `dply`, `tsa`, `ssp` in both prefix tables (`themeSet`, `deployment`, `themeAsset`, `simulationPreset`). Scopes `theme-sets:read`, `theme-sets:write`, `deployments:read` at the end of `API_SCOPES`; developer holds all three, viewer the two reads (none is in `NOT_FOR_VIEWERS`), reviewer none; `PERMISSION_INFO` puts the three in the existing "Templates" area, so the area order is unchanged. Problems: `FLOW_ENGINE_PROBLEM_STATUSES` (`flow_step_not_current` 409, `field_unknown`, `field_write_once`, `fields_too_large` with `size` and `limit`, `consent_text_mismatch` 422, `selfie_capture_not_extendable` 409; fixtures `contracts/fixtures/problems/flow-engine/`) and `TEMPLATE_PROBLEM_STATUSES` (`definition_invalid`, `precondition_failed` 412, `draft_exists`, `promote_unresolved`, `field_type_changed`, `consent_copy_incomplete`, `consent_copy_missing_placeholder`, `locale_unsupported`, `embed_origin_invalid`, `embed_wildcard_unacknowledged`, `redirect_uri_not_allowed`, `publish_acknowledgement_missing`; fixtures `problems/templates/`), and `FLOW_DEFINITION_PROBLEM_CODES`, the C174.8 codes a v2 flow's `definition_invalid` carries in `errors[].code` (the graph checks add their own beside them). The four Phase 8 codes keep Phase 8's statuses, by test. A v2 flow's problems use location `body` and pointers under `/data/attributes/flow/`. Neither new folder is read by the Android SDK (`Fixtures.list` reads `problems/` and `problems/flow/` only). The 412's current ETag travels in the response's `ETag` header, not the body. The theme problem codes (`theme_contrast_insufficient`, `theme_set_default`, `asset_type_refused`) are frozen with the theme contracts in (H); the 9B codes are `contracts-9b`'s. `@axe-core/playwright` is a dev dependency of `apps/flow`, and `e2e/src/axe.test.ts` allows the importers `apps/flow` and `e2e` and no other. ADR 0008 is `docs/adr/0008-flows-server-decided-branching-embedding-and-preview-origins.md`.

Beyond the plan's list, three stub files give `flow-spec-logic`'s other deliverables an export line: `v2/device-kind.ts` (`deviceKindOf`, C150.2's mapping), `v2/scope-schema.ts` (`scopeJsonSchema`, C150.10) and `v2/json-schema.ts` (`flowV2JsonSchema`, `FLOW_V2_SCHEMA_ID`, the v2 export of C147.1). Every stub throws `FlowSpecNotBuiltError` through `notBuilt(name, owner, ...inputs)` (`v2/schemas/problems.ts`), so nothing passes a flow unchecked; `v2/stubs.test.ts` holds each until its owner replaces the test. 18. **The v2 schemas as frozen** (`v2/schemas/`): the schema checks shapes and the uniqueness of step ids, edge ids, component ids per screen (nested included) and route keys; the entry, endpoints, ports and every C147.6 rule are `checkFlowGraph`'s. Decisions the plan left open: (a) `prefillFrom` is one field key on `phone_otp` and `email_otp`, and a map from input to field key on `database` (its `DATABASE_FIELDS`) and on an AAMVA `form` step (its form inputs), since those collect several values; (b) the failure step's members are camelCase, `decisionBasis`, `contestRoute { url?, contact?, caseTemplateId? }` (C128.3's rule: a URL or a contact) and `biometricBasis`; (c) `captureMethodsByDevice`'s keys are the `device.kind` values (`desktop-web`, `mobile-web`, `native`), at least one; (d) a form component carries `field`, `labelKey`, optional `hintKey` and `required`; `address` binds `fields { street1, street2?, city, subdivision?, postalCode, country }`; a `button` has `action` (`submit`, `cancel`, `link`), `port` (a submit's `button:<key>`) and `link` (`help-url`, `privacy-url`); (e) a pattern is plain text of at most 500 characters, never compiled by the schema (v1's `RegExp` check stays v1's alone, amendment 9); (f) `onNull` matches `else`, `skip-route`, `review` or `route:<key>`, optional in the schema (`on_null_missing` is a typing rule); (g) the counts of C147.5 are `FLOW_V2_CAPS` (`v2/schemas/common.ts`) and bound the arrays; byte caps and container depth are graph rules; (h) `FlowIssue` gains `pointer` (RFC 6901, through `toJsonPointer` of `flow.ts`), and `safeParseAnyFlow`/`parseAnyFlow` (`FlowSpecError`) dispatch on `metadata.specVersion`.

SDK host tests 1,334 → 1,360.
21. **The owner's option (i), as tested.** A verification step with an `exhausted` edge is a placeholder wherever it would appear (`exhausted-1`, `exhausted-2`): the SDK stops on `Unsupported("step server-continuation")` after consent, with no `Finalizing`, no `Completed` and no verification; a fresh start at the government ID's position does the same. After the fallback was taken on another client, a fresh start at the document step runs it and completes only at the server's completion (`exhausted-3`, `exhausted-4`). A consent step with custom copy stops the SDK at the first step and records no consent (V11).

Extractions are `R/H` `beta` (`document-type` `H` `beta`), so a route on one needs `heuristic:`; `birthdate`, `age-years` and `expiration-date` are PII, `country-code` and `id-class` are not. `inquiry.fields.<key>` is described as `person` (fail-safe for `asserted-fact:`; the engine may mark a prefilled value `operator`).

Amendment 3's rule is `actionServesTrigger(triggers, trigger)` in `@openverify/core` (with `LISTED_ONLY_TRIGGER_TYPES` and `isWorkflowTriggerType`): the graph check and the console's palette call it; `GET /workflow-actions` still serves `triggers: null`, which now documents "every type but trigger-flow" (no Phase 8 answer changed). 30. **(T): the routes (C154.4, C156; `template-routes.ts`).** `validateInquiryTemplateVersionBodySchema` (an optional unsaved body and `meta.mode`) and `templateVersionValidationDocumentSchema` (`inquiry-template-version-validation`: `validation`, `flow-sha256`, `acknowledgements-required`); `diffInquiryTemplateVersionQuerySchema` (`against`) and `templateVersionDiffDocumentSchema` (`inquiry-template-version-diff`, id `<a>..<b>`, the C154.6 sections, `layout-only`, `consequences[]` of `TEMPLATE_DIFF_CONSEQUENCES`); `previewInquiryTemplateVersionBodySchema` (unsaved `flow`, `copy`, `field-schemas`, `theme-set-id`, `styles`, and `meta.locale`, a base locale or `en-XA`, `ar-XB`) and `templatePreviewDocumentSchema` (`inquiry-template-preview`: `steps` of `clientStepViewSchema`, `theme` of `flowThemeSchema`, `copy`, synthetic `guidance`); `simulatedScopeSchema` (PII in logs), `simulateInquiryTemplateVersionBodySchema` (a scope or a preset id, never both) and `templateSimulationDocumentSchema` (`inquiry-template-simulation`: `simulated: true`, each step with its port, route result, `on-null` and the facts' tier, stage and source, the outcome with its `flow-outcome`, `halted`); `restoreInquiryTemplateBodySchema`, `copyInquiryTemplateBodySchema`, `promoteInquiryTemplateBodySchema` with `promoteMetaSchema`; `templateUsageDocumentSchema` (`inquiry-template-usage`: named references and `*-hidden` counts, P34). 31. **(T): translations, deployments, presets, events.** `inquiry-template-translations.ts` (C162.7: `inquiry-template-translations` with `inquiry-template-version-id` and `translations[] { step, step-display-name, component, attribute-name, locale-values[] }`, `importTranslationsBodySchema`, `TRANSLATIONS_CSV_LIMITS`, `TRANSLATIONS_CSV_FIXED_COLUMNS`); `deployments.ts` (C154.7: `deployment` with `resource-type`, `creator`, one `routes[]` entry and an `inquiry-template` relationship, `listDeploymentsQuerySchema` with `filter[inquiry-template-id]`, `filter[environment-id]`, `filter[changes-only]`, `buildDeploymentResource`); `simulation-presets.ts` (C156.4: `simulation-preset`, ids `ssp_…` or `builtin:<name>`, `label: "synthetic values only"`, `PII_SIMULATION_PRESET_ATTRIBUTES`); `template-version-events.ts` (the version summary an event carries: no flow, configuration or copy). Events `inquiry-template-version.created`, `.published` (`INQUIRY_TEMPLATE_VERSION_EVENT_NAMES`) and `deployment.completed` (`DEPLOYMENT_EVENT_NAMES`), subjects `inquiry-template-version` and `deployment`, resolved through the payload's `inquiry-template` link and **strict** in `apps/api/src/events/subject.ts`; their samples are in `apps/api/src/webhooks/samples.ts`; the console words them and groups them as "Inquiry templates" and "Deployments". The eight new resource types join `RESOURCE_TYPES`. `@openverify/node` gains `deployments` (`list`, `listAll`, `retrieve`) and `inquiryTemplateTranslations` (`retrieve`, `import`). The OpenAPI registry names `FlowV2` and `FlowComponent`, so the recursive component's references resolve (K8-15's test); the schema keeps a global id `FlowComponent`.

32. **(F): the origin module and its subpath (C158.1, C157.2; V04), as built.** `packages/contracts/src/origin-pattern.ts` imports nothing and exports `MAX_ORIGIN_ENTRIES` (50), `SHARED_HOSTING_SUFFIXES` (29 entries: the hosting platforms of C158.1 plus `firebaseapp.com`, `appspot.com`, `onrender.com`, `fly.dev`, and the public second-level country suffixes `co.uk`, `org.uk`, `ac.uk`, `gov.uk`, `com.au`, `net.au`, `org.au`, `co.nz`, `co.jp`, `com.br`, `com.cn`, `co.in`, `co.za`, `com.mx`, `com.tr`, `com.sg`, `co.kr`; incomplete by design), `OriginEnvironmentKind` (`sandbox`, `production`), `checkOriginEntry(entry, environment)` (a refusal code or `null`), `isOriginEntry`, `isWildcardOriginEntry`, `matchesOriginPattern(origin, entry)` and `parseEmbedPolicyAnswer(value)` with `EmbedPolicyAnswer` (camelCase members). The refusal codes, which the API's `embed_origin_invalid` may carry per entry: `not_an_origin`, `scheme`, `userinfo`, `path`, `ip_literal`, `bare_wildcard`, `wildcard_port`, `wildcard_too_short`, `shared_hosting_suffix`, `not_lowercase_ascii`, `localhost_in_production`. Decisions the plan left open: `https://127.0.0.1` is refused as an IP literal (only `http://127.0.0.1[:port]` is the sandbox's loopback entry); a leading-zero or out-of-range port is `not_an_origin`; an entry is at most 300 characters; the default port is filled in on both sides, so `https://a.example.com:443` and `https://a.example.com` are one origin. Every pattern in the module is a fixed literal; nothing a caller passes reaches `new RegExp`. `parseEmbedPolicyAnswer` re-checks every `frame-ancestors` entry with the sandbox grammar (the API has applied the environment's) and refuses any extra or missing key. The subpath `@openverify/contracts/origin-pattern` maps `@openverify/source` to `./src/origin-pattern.ts` and `types`/`default` to `dist`. The golden vectors are `contracts/fixtures/origin-pattern/vectors.json` (`entries` with `environment` and `refusal`, `matches` with `origin`, `entry`, `matches`, `answers` with `valid`); a contracts test holds `embedPolicyAnswerSchema` and `parseEmbedPolicyAnswer` to the same answers.
33. **(F): the flow API (C148.5, C151.5 to C151.8, C153.2, C160.6, C162.9, C171.4).** `flow-embed.ts`: `FLOW_SPEC_HEADER` (`OpenVerify-Flow-Spec`) and `parseFlowSpecHeader(value)` (the declared versions in order, unknown ones ignored, `[1]` when none), `flowScreenParamsSchema` and `submitFlowScreenBodySchema` (`values` by field key, PII in logs, at most 1,000, and an optional `button` key) with `PII_FLOW_SCREEN_ATTRIBUTES`, `extendSelfieCaptureParamsSchema` (`POST /flow/selfie-captures/:id/extend`, answering the selfie capture), `flowThemeAssetParamsSchema` (`GET /flow/theme-assets/:id`; (H) adds the operator's asset routes), `embedPolicyParamsSchema` and `embedPolicyAnswerSchema` (C157.2, kept out of the public OpenAPI document). The session (`flow-api.ts`) gains, all optional so older answers parse: `position` (`flowSessionPositionSchema`: `step-id`, `state` of `FLOW_POSITION_STATES` `active`, `waiting`, `halted`, `ended`, `previous-step-id`, `retry-after-ms` up to 30,000), `step` (`clientStepViewSchema`), `values` (`flowSessionValueSchema`: a PII value the client may not read back is `{ set: true }`), `theme`, `copy`, `locales { enabled, default }`, `embed { allowed-origins }`, `completion { redirect-url }` (an absolute URL whose scheme is not `javascript:`, `data:`, `vbscript:`, `file:`, `blob:` or `about:`; null when there is none) and `spec-version`. Consent's body gains `text-sha256[]`, `copy-source` (`custom`, `built-in`) and `catalog-version` (C162.9); a file upload's body gains `capture-method` of `CAPTURE_METHODS` (C153.2). Today's Android SDK decodes `flow-session-v1-projection.json` and `flow-session-v2.json` (ContractFixturesTest).
34. **(F): the session's `flow` is a union until the projection is served.** `flowSessionFlowSchema` is `z.union([flowProjectionSchema, flowSchema])`: Phase 9 sends the projection to every client (amendment 19), and the v1 flow with transitions stays valid so today's API (which serves the template's v1 flow) and its tests pass while `api-flow-engine` builds the projection. **`integration-9a` narrows it to `flowProjectionSchema`** once the session serves the projection, with the OpenAPI snapshot. The web flow (`apps/flow/src/flow/flow-position.ts`) reads either: its `SessionFlowStep` is the union's step, and a `server-continuation` placeholder resolves to `{ kind: 'unsupported', reason: 'step' }`, never skipped (owner option (i); a test in `flow-position.test.ts`, which fails with the case removed). `flow-renderer` removes that walk when it renders the server's `position` and `step` (C151.5).
35. **(F): settings and the inquiry (C158.1, C158.4, C160.7, C174.6).** `dashboardSettingsSchema` and `dashboardUpdateSettingsBodySchema` gain `allowed-embed-origins` (at most 50 strings; the grammar is the API's, through the origin module), `allowed-redirect-origins` (nullable: `null` keeps the install-wide rule) and `default-theme-set-id` (`theset_…`, nullable), **optional in the response** until `foundation-9a` serves them; their defaults are the separate `EMBEDDING_SETTINGS_DEFAULTS` (`[]`, `null`, `null`), not part of `DASHBOARD_SETTINGS_DEFAULTS`, so no served answer changed. **`foundation-9a` (F) merges them into `DASHBOARD_SETTINGS_DEFAULTS`** when it serves them, and changes the API's `routes/dashboard/settings.test.ts` and the contracts defaults test then (as Phase 8 did with its workflow settings). The `PATCH` body takes `meta.acknowledge-wildcards` (V17); `meta` alone is "nothing to update". A settings problem points into the flat body, so the golden `embed-origin-invalid.json` names `/allowed-embed-origins/1` (corrected in the RED commit, with `phase9a-problems.test.ts`). The console's `RetentionSetting` type excludes the new `EmbeddingSetting` names; `dashboard-settings` shows and edits them. `POST /inquiries` takes `redirect-uri` (`redirectUriSchema` of the version, amendment 28), and the inquiry resource carries `redirect-uri` (nullable).
36. **(F): the messages (C156.2, C159.4; `flow-messages.ts`).** `FLOW_MESSAGE_MAX_BYTES` and `PREVIEW_ASSETS_MAX_BYTES` (1 MiB each). Console to preview (`source: openverify-console`, `version: 1`): `preview-document` (an unsaved document as a record), `select-step`, `set-outcome` (`step-id`, `passed`, `failed` or `needs_review`), `set-viewport` (320, 375, 768), `set-scheme`, `preview-assets` (at most two, `asset-id`, `kind` `logo` or `font`, `bytes` a transferred `ArrayBuffer`). Preview to console (`openverify-preview`): `ready`, `error` (`malformed-document`, `asset-refused`, `unknown`), `navigated`. Parent to flow (`openverify-parent`): `hello` only. Flow to parent (`openverify-flow`), only after a valid hello and only to its exact origin: `ready`, `event` (`start`, `step-changed` with the step's type, never its id, `complete`, `cancel`, `error`), `resize`, `complete` (`inquiry-id`, `status`, a hint), `cancel`, `error` (`EMBED_ERROR_CODES`). Each is strict, so an unknown member or type is dropped. The `bytes` member is a refinement over `unknown` typed `ArrayBuffer`, because `z.instanceof` cannot be written as JSON Schema, which every exported schema must be (`json-schema.test.ts`); its JSON Schema is empty.
37. **(F): existing tests changed:** the golden `embed-origin-invalid.json`'s pointer and `phase9a-problems.test.ts` (amendment 35); the contracts fixture list (`fixtures.test.ts`) and the Android list (`ContractFixturesTest.kt`) gain the two session fixtures. No served answer changed: the OpenAPI snapshot grows by the session's new members and the projection, and `openapi:write` leaves its sha256 unchanged on a second run.

38. **(H): theme sets, revisions and restore (C160.1, C160.2, C160.5; `packages/contracts/src/theme-sets.ts`).** `theme-set` (`theset_`): `name` (1 to 100, trimmed), `status` (`THEME_SET_STATUSES`: `active`, `archived`), `styles` (`themeStylesSchema` of amendment 30), `revision`, `is-default`, `created-at`, `updated-at`, `archived-at` (set exactly when archived; an archived set is never the default). `listThemeSetsQuerySchema` (`filter[status]`, a comma-separated string as every list filter keeps it), `createThemeSetBodySchema` (`styles` absent is the empty tree), `updateThemeSetBodySchema` (at least one of `name` and `styles`; `styles` replaces the tree), `restoreThemeSetBodySchema` (`meta.revision`). Decisions the plan left open: a revision is the resource `theme-set-revision` with id `<theme set id>:<n>` (`themeSetRevisionId`), attributes `revision`, `name`, `styles`, `creator` (`lastUpdaterSchema`), `restored-from` (the copied revision, `null` for an edit), `created-at`; `GET /theme-sets/:id/revisions` is newest first and pages with `page[before]` (a revision number, at least 2) and `page[size]` (`listThemeSetRevisionsQuerySchema`), not `page[after]`, since a revision has no prefixed id. Builders: `buildThemeSetResource(ThemeSetRecord)`, `buildThemeSetRevisionResource(ThemeSetRevisionRecord)`.
39. **(H): assets (C160.6).** `theme-asset` (`tsa_`): `kind` (`THEME_ASSET_KINDS`: `logo`, `font`), `media-type` of what is stored (`image/png`, `image/jpeg` for a logo's rendition, since a WebP becomes PNG; `font/woff2`), `byte-size`, `width` and `height` (a logo's, `null` for a font), `licence-attestation` (`{ by, at }`, exactly on a font), `unicode-range` (a font's only), `created-at`; `buildThemeAssetResource(ThemeAssetRecord)`. `THEME_ASSET_LIMITS` (`logo-bytes` 262,144, `logo-pixels` 2,048, `font-bytes` 524,288) and `THEME_ASSET_UPLOAD_MEDIA_TYPES` (PNG, JPEG, WebP; WOFF2). The upload `POST /theme-sets/assets` is multipart: the bytes in the part `THEME_ASSET_FILE_FIELD` (`file`), and `uploadThemeAssetFieldsSchema` for `kind`, `unicode-range` and `meta[licence-attested]` (`true` required for a font). Decision the plan left open: `unicode-range` is taken at upload, an operator's value, held to a strict CSS `unicode-range` pattern (`U+` code points, ranges or `?` wildcards, comma-separated), so it reaches a `FontFace` descriptor as it is and can never carry another rule. `themeAssetContentParamsSchema` is `GET /theme-sets/assets/:id/content`; `THEME_ASSET_RESPONSE_HEADERS` freezes both reads' headers (operator: `private, no-store`, `attachment`, `nosniff`; flow: `private, max-age=3600`, `nosniff`).
40. **(H): the theme problems and the contrast gate (C160.4).** `THEME_PROBLEM_STATUSES` (`theme_contrast_insufficient` 422, `theme_set_default` 409, `asset_type_refused` 415) with `THEME_PROBLEM_CODES` and `themeProblemCodeSchema` in `problem.ts`; golden problems in `contracts/fixtures/problems/themes/`, not read by the Android SDK. `themeContrastProblemSchema` carries `pairs[] { pointer, foreground, background, ratio, required }`: decision the plan left open, `foreground` and `background` are **token names**, never colours, `pointer` names the foreground cell (`/data/attributes/styles/<light|dark>/palette/<token>`, also when the cell was absent and took the built-in token), `ratio` unrounded in [1, 21], `required` 4.5 or 3; at most 32 pairs. `packages/core/src/contrast/index.ts` (exported by `@openverify/core`) freezes `CONTRAST_PALETTE_TOKENS` (equal to the contracts' `THEME_PALETTE_TOKENS`, by a contracts test), `CONTRAST_TEXT_MINIMUM` 4.5, `CONTRAST_NON_TEXT_MINIMUM` 3, `CONTRAST_ENHANCED_MINIMUM` 7, the sixteen `CONTRAST_PAIRS` of C160.4 (each with `purpose`, `required` and `enhanced`: 7 for the text, link and button-label pairs, `null` for control and focus; `line` has none), `ContrastFinding` (`foreground`, `background`, `ratio`, `required`, `level` `fail` or `warn`) and the stubs `relativeLuminance(colour)`, `contrastRatio(foreground, background)` and `checkPaletteContrast(palette)`, which throw `ContrastNotBuiltError` (owner `api-themes`) until **`api-themes` builds them in 9B and replaces `contrast.test.ts`'s stub tests** with the vectors.
41. **(H): the inquiry, resource types and the client.** `POST /inquiries` takes `theme-set-id` (`theset_`; the API checks it is an active set of the environment) and the inquiry carries `theme-set-id` (nullable, optional). `RESOURCE_TYPES` gains `theme-set`, `theme-set-revision` and `theme-asset` (`vocabulary.test.ts` changed for that). `@openverify/node` gains `themeSets`: `list`, `listAll`, `create`, `retrieve`, `update`, `archive` (`DELETE`), `revisions(id, { page: { before, size } })`, `restore(id, revision)`, `uploadAsset(kind, file, { licenceAttested, unicodeRange })` and `assetContent(id)` (bytes and type), with `ThemeAssetFile` and `ThemeAssetUploadOptions`. The OpenAPI snapshot changes only by the inquiry's `theme-set-id`; the theme routes appear when `foundation-9a` (H) registers them. Existing tests changed: `vocabulary.test.ts` (the resource list), `fixtures.test.ts` (five fixtures), `phase9a-problems.test.ts` (the `themes` folder), `resources.test.ts` (the group list) and the Android list (five server-only fixtures).

42. **Found by the final e2e gate: a version's settings take `inquiry-expiration-after-start-seconds: null`.** Amendment 29 served the resolved after-start window as `null` when absent but kept the input `number | undefined`, so the console's "New draft", which copies the current version's resolved settings into the new draft, was refused ("Some values are not valid"; `e2e/tests/ui-lifecycle.spec.ts:136`). `templateSettingsSchema` now takes `null` there, meaning none, as absent does; a test in `template-settings.test.ts` holds the round trip (`resolveTemplateSettings(resolveTemplateSettings({}))` equals the defaults). The OpenAPI snapshot changed for it.

## Owner decision after Workflow 9A's stop (2026-11-04)

Binding on Phase 9 and on Phase 10. It resolves the Workflow 9A Stage 1a amendment 1 (PROJECTION RULES WRONG).

1. **A verification step with an `exhausted` edge has no v1 form (option (i); amends C151.6 and C151.7).** It is served to spec-1 clients as a `server-continuation` placeholder wherever it would appear: as the current step, or in the unconditional run after it. Today's Android SDK therefore stops on its unsupported screen before such a step, as it already does before a `screen`, `branch` or `action`, and never reaches `Finalizing` on an exhausted step. The C151.6 projection host test asserts this in its case (c): the SDK stops on `Unsupported` at that step's position, with no `Finalizing`, no `Completed` and no verification created; its other assertions stay as written. A template version that uses a fallback does not run on today's Android SDK until Phase 10's SDK reads spec 2 (C175.1 already lists `exhausted` routes for Phase 10), and C151.7's publish warning names fallbacks beside screens, actions, waits and custom consent copy. Option (ii) stays refused (C151.7: negotiation changes only what is sent, never the path), and option (iii) is not taken.

## Workflow 9A Stage 1b amendments (foundation-9a)

Recorded by `foundation-9a` at `cbb0402f`. Evidence and every handoff: `docs/tdd/phase9-foundation-9a.tdd.md`.

1. **CI split (0).** CI 41802158981 (Phase 8's push) took node 21.2 of 25 minutes, inside the last 25%: `@openverify/db` and `@openverify/watchlist` moved to a new job `unit-db` (25 minutes, coverage uploaded); node keeps the typecheck and every other package. `infra/tests/ci-workflow.test.sh` checks that node, unit-api, unit-dashboard and unit-db test every package exactly once, and (F) that node tests `@openverify/js`. The times after the split are read at the Workflow's push (`integration-9a`).
2. **One migration**, `0030_phase9a`, extended at (S), (T), (F) and (H) by the Stage 1a amendment 17 recipe with no hand edit; the PGlite migration time did not move (4,472 ms before (T), 4,470 after (T), 4,527 after (F), 4,514 after (H), load 1.3 to 1.6).
3. **(S) as built.** `inquiry_decisions.flow` (`{ templateVersionId, failureStepId, routes[] }`, present exactly on `flow-failure`) beside the inquiry columns. The step record's, action rows' and snapshots' repositories were stubs handed to their owners, who built them. `recordConsent` became one transaction under the inquiry lock, so `assertCurrentStepInTx` and `advanceFlowInTx` share it. The AAMVA silent attempt's after-commit work travels as `InquiryDecisionHookResult.flowAfter`. `storedFlow` is gone: `storedAnyFlow`, `storedFlowView` (a v2 flow as its v1-typed steps), `safeStoredFlowView` and `flowViewOf` in `services/stored-flow.ts` serve all 16 readers. The signals input moved to `jobs/signals/input.ts` (`inquirySignalsInputInTx`). The screen route answers the session (`flowSessionResponseSchema`); `flowScreenParamsSchema` is `{ stepId }`.
4. **(T): the session's theme is `null` until 9B.** `resolveThemeForSession` answers `null` (the member is left out and the flow keeps its own stylesheet) rather than a built-in theme, because the built-in tokens are `oklch()` values and C160.3's palette is hex: `api-themes` defines the built-in palette as hex in 9B (review H29).
5. **(T): the simulation preset routes** answer 501 to an operator and 401 without a session (`routes/dashboard/simulation-presets-frozen.test.ts`, which `api-templates` replaces). The redaction coverage test lists `simulation_presets` and `theme_assets` with the tables no subject redaction reaches (`SIMULATION_PRESET_TABLES`, `THEME_ASSET_TABLES` in `services/redaction.test-support.ts`).
6. **(F): the internal route** authenticates in an `onRequest` hook, before its params are read (so a request without the token learns nothing, not even a 400), and counts `internal-embed-policy` per token (`flow-server-current`, `flow-server-previous`) there too. With no token configured it accepts nothing (401). A `null` answer of `embedPolicyFor` is 404.
7. **(F): the origin settings are validated in 9A.** `embeddingSettingsProblems` (`services/embed-origins.ts`, `flow-server-embed`'s file) is built rather than stubbed: each entry of `allowed-embed-origins` and `allowed-redirect-origins` through `checkOriginEntry` for the environment's kind (an unknown kind counts as production), then a production wildcard without its acknowledgement. Leaving it a stub would have stored origins the matcher refuses. `flow-server-embed` may extend it (an audit field for the acknowledgement, the version's subset at publish).
8. **(F): `redirect-uri` on `POST /inquiries` is refused until built.** `checkInquiryRedirectUri` refuses every value with 422 `redirect_uri_not_allowed` (fail closed); `createInquiryInTx` stores the URI once accepted (`inquiries.redirect_uri`, added here: no stage owned the column). The inquiry resource does not serve `redirect-uri` yet (for `flow-server-embed` or `api-flow-engine` with the inquiry attributes).
9. **(F): `files.capture_method` is stored by no path yet.** The column, `NewFileInput.captureMethod` and the CHECK exist; the upload in `services/flow.ts` (`api-flow-engine`'s) passes the body's `capture-method` when C153.2 is built.
10. **(F): `apps/flow/tsconfig.json` includes `server.ts`** with `allowImportingTsExtensions`, besides `tsconfig.server.json` (Node resolution, `erasableSyntaxOnly`, Node types), because typescript-eslint's project service only reads `tsconfig.json` and the root ESLint config is protected; the typecheck runs both. Node strips types through the workspace link, so the V04 fallback (built `dist` with `dependsOn: ["^build"]`) is not needed and `turbo.json` is unchanged.
11. **(F): the slots.** `/embed/:inquiryId` renders `EmbedEntry({ flow, fallback })`, today the not-found page; `main.tsx` calls `mountEmbedBridge(window)` before the session is read; `preview.html` is a `build.rolldownOptions.input` (Vite 8 deprecates `rollupOptions`) whose entry calls `startPreview(document)`.
12. **(F): the mock registries.** `registerFakeFlowExtension` (`src/testing/fake-flow-registry.ts`) and `registerMockFlowExtension` (in `e2e/support/mock-flow-api.ts`), each with `session` and client or route hooks. The fake answers only the client methods of Phase 8 itself; a method added to `FlowApiClient` comes from its owner's extension.
13. **(F): `@openverify/js` lives in `packages/sdk-js`**, so `workspace-conventions.test.ts` names a published-name exception (`PUBLISHED_NAMES`); the package is private until Phase 11. Its Vite library build writes `dist/index.js` and `dist/openverify.iife.js` (`OpenVerify`), `tsc -p tsconfig.build.json` the types.
14. **(F): the logical-properties rule** is exported (`@openverify/config/eslint-logical-properties`), but the root `eslint.config.js` does not spread it: a config-protection hook refuses edits to it (as for `flow-i18n`). An owner adds `...logicalPropertiesConfig()` there.
15. **(F): configuration.** The flow server token needs at least 32 bytes (UTF-8), not 32 random bytes, which cannot be checked; placeholders are refused in production. `.env.example` keeps `OPENVERIFY_FLOW_SERVER_TOKEN_PREVIOUS` commented out (the compose test refuses an empty secret-like value). `OPENVERIFY_DASHBOARD_CSP_MODE` and `OPENVERIFY_FLOW_PUBLIC_URL` reach the dashboard container now; the console reads them in 9C.
16. **(H): `inquiries.theme_set_id`** (FK to the set of its own environment) is added here, for `POST /inquiries`' `theme-set-id`. `checkThemeSetChoice` (`services/themes/settings.ts`) refuses any non-null set until 9B, on the inquiry and on `default-theme-set-id` (served, default `null`).
17. **(H): `themeSetPublishProblems` reads `PublishContext.themeSetId`.** Its call sits first inside `publishHookProblems` (a 422 at `/data/attributes/theme-set-id`); no caller passes the member yet, so `api-templates` passes the version's `theme-set-id` when it builds C154.9.
18. **(H): theme assets have no row-sealed column**, so `SEALED` gains nothing; `theme_assets.wrapped_dek` (aad `file`) is in `ENCRYPTED_COLUMNS` and `REWRAP_TABLES`. No theme repository is declared: `api-themes` adds them in 9B.
19. **(H): the flow slot is a context.** `App` cannot see a session (the renderer reads it), so `app.tsx` provides `SessionThemeContext` with `(session) => applySessionTheme(session, styleVariant)`; `flow-renderer` calls `useApplySessionTheme()(session)` and passes `styleVariant` to `App`.
20. **(A11Y): one tag list in `@openverify/config/axe`.** The pure half moved from `e2e/src/axe.ts` (which re-exports it), so the mocked flow suite and the full-stack suite share `AXE_WCAG_TAGS` without a cross-package import. `expectAccessible` also takes `where` and an `exceptions` override (its own tests); an expired exception fails every call.
21. **Existing tests changed because the plan changes what they check:** `db/testing.test.ts`, `schema-v2.test.ts`, `key-rotation.test.ts` (new tables and columns); `db/migration-v5.integration.test.ts` (Phase 4 files seeded by SQL); `contracts/rate-limits.test.ts`, `dashboard-api.test.ts`, `phase9a-flow.test.ts` (policies and served defaults); `api/plugins/rate-limit.test.ts`, `services/context.test.ts`, `jobs/records.test.ts`, `services/redaction.test.ts`; `config/workspace-conventions.test.ts`; `api/phase9a-flow-plumbing.test.ts` (it leaves what `api-flow-engine` built).
22. **Open at the end of Stage 1b, for `api-flow-engine` and `integration-9a`:** the final gate failed only in `api-flow-engine`'s in-progress work: its RED commit `61443e18` (`routes/flow/session-flow.test.ts`, 18 tests) has no GREEN yet, and its current-step check (`87e507f9`) refuses `createVerification` in three shared tests written before the flow had a position (`api.integration.test.ts`, 2; `services/handoff/handoff-races.integration.test.ts`, 4; `e2e/tests/ui-reviewer.spec.ts`, 1 and 2 not run). ## Workflow 9A Stage 2 amendments

Recorded by `integration-9a` (Workflow 9A Stage 4) from the Stage 2 reports and the code at `0c7eb7ca`. Each agent's evidence is its `docs/tdd/phase9-<agent>.tdd.md`.

### flow-spec-logic

1. **The borrowed edit in `packages/workflow-engine` (C150.12) is wider than one function.** `parseCondition` refused any root outside Phase 8's and any `/` in a path member (every signal name holds one), so the edit adds `expressions/flow-condition.ts` (`typeFlowCondition`, `evaluateFlowCondition`), `parsePath(text, member)` with `FLOW_PATH_MEMBER`, `parseConditionOver(raw, roots)` and `evaluateFlowExpression`, each off unless the new functions set it; Phase 8's 1,984 tests pass.
2. **H03 at run time.** Phase 8's evaluator answers `{"!==": [<null>, "failed"]}` with `true` (only its publish typing stops it), so the flow evaluates routes **only** through `evaluateFlowCondition`, which reads a non-literal null operand of `!==` as unknown. Binding on every caller.
3. **The migration folds what v1 read as falsy (C147.10).** Beyond the literal `null` for a path outside v1's context, every sub-condition that reads no `inquiry.status` is folded to its v1 value (`condition_folded`), because the strict dialect reads a `null` under `!`, `!!`, `and` or `or` as unknown; a `var` of v1's whole context is kept as `{"var": "inquiry"}` (`var_object_context`) so publish refuses it until rewritten. Note codes are `MIGRATION_NOTE_CODES`; routes are keyed `r1`, `r2`, labelled "Transition <n>"; a migrated form's submit button is `continue` with copy key `screen.continue`.
4. **Additive exports beyond the frozen ones** (re-exported through `v2/graph.ts`, the index unchanged): `nextPosition`'s sixth argument `NextPositionOptions { visits, moves, waited }`, `resumeAt`, `RouteTaken.notes`, the halt reason `edge_missing`, `FlowGraphOptions`' `fieldSchemas`, `linkFields`, `customConsentCopy`, `noticeKeys` and `checks`, and `checkFieldSchemaChanges`, `checkSettingsWindows`, `flowScopeAt`, `analyzeFlowForPublish`, `canonicalJson`, `checkScopeStatus`, `FLOW_LOOP_LIMITS`, `FLOW_STEP_NOTES`, `FLOW_GRAPH_PROBLEM_CODES`, `FLOW_GRAPH_WARNING_CODES`, `MIGRATION_NOTE_CODES`, `MIGRATED_SUBMIT_LABEL_KEY`, `PATTERN_PROGRAM_LIMIT`, `PATTERN_SOURCE_LIMIT`, `PATTERN_REFUSAL_REASONS`, `VISIBILITY_RULE_LIMITS`, `VisibilityRuleError`, `REVEALING_WORDS`, `MAX_FIELD_SCHEMAS`, `SHORT_WINDOW_SECONDS`, `ACKNOWLEDGEMENT_KINDS`. `v2/stubs.test.ts` became a guard that no frozen export throws "not built".
5. **The graph codes** sit beside `FLOW_DEFINITION_PROBLEM_CODES` in `definition_invalid`'s `errors[]`: errors (`entry_unknown`, `edge_endpoint_unknown`, `port_invalid`, `port_duplicate`, `port_unwired`, `step_unreachable`, `terminal_unreachable`, `verification_type_repeated`, `step_order_invalid`, `consent_not_dominating`, `step_id_reserved`, `screen_submit_missing`, `button_port_duplicate`, `link_field_unset`, `visibility_rule_invalid`, `screen_components_too_many`, `container_too_deep`, `step_too_large`, `flow_too_large`, `field_binding_mismatch`, `on_null_route_unknown`, `action_source_invalid`, `fields_too_many`, and the analyses' `decision_basis_missing`, `contest_route_missing`, `biometric_basis_missing`, `consent_key_missing`) and warnings (`unverified_path`, `verification_all_beta`, `duplicate_collection`, `step_id_revealing`, `spec1_unsupported`, `client_signal_before_screen`, `tipping_off_route`, `window_below_20_hours`, `asserted_fact_route`, `unverified_path_completion`). The console's words are 9C's (`dashboard-flow-builder`).
6. **C148.3 nested quantifiers (owner decision open).** Taken literally, a quantifier on a group that holds one is refused (`nested_quantifier`), which refuses `\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?`; with a Pike VM nesting costs no time and `PATTERN_PROGRAM_LIMIT` (2,048) bounds the program, so the owner may relax it. Also refused: lazy quantifiers, `\D`, `\W`, `\S`, `\b`, `\n` and other escapes outside V10's list, inline flags, the empty pattern. A pattern applies to the whole value.
7. **C151.6, decisions left open:** an exited `device_handoff`, and an exited consent with custom copy, are left out of the projection; a step id of the placeholder's form (`continue-<n>`) is refused (`step_id_reserved`); the flow's `description` is not sent.
8. **C147.6, decisions left open:** draft and publish modes run the same rules; without `fieldSchemas` every bound field counts as PII (fail closed); a screen showing a legal notice satisfies the consent rule for itself; the spec-1 warning is per step; `email` binds `string` or `email-address`; revealing words match the start of an id segment; the duplicate-collection warning covers a field bound twice, a phone or email field then its OTP step, and a database or AAMVA form whose inputs a screen already binds. The window warning reads settings, so it is `checkSettingsWindows`.
9. **C150.7, C150.10, C151.13, decisions left open in the analyses:** "not required" is by the version's configuration, else the registry default; a check's tier includes its alternate (`A/H` counts as heuristic); a branch that reads a list is adverse; `asserted-fact:` compares the fewest verifications from a route's target and from `else` to any completion; `unverified-path:` is required for every completion a path reaches avoiding all verifications or one verification type another path holds. C128.4: a read of a selfie step's facts, or of `inquiry.status` or any check status when `templateIsBiometric` is not `false` (`null` fails closed).
10. **Signal sources (C150.2, C150.10):** `flowScopeAt` marks a signal `client` when its collection tier is not `server` (the three session-integrity signals); `FLOW_SCOPE_PATHS`' generic `signals.<name>` says `server`. Carried: align the generic entry (owner of `v2/schemas/`, 9C's `dashboard-flow-builder` reads both).
11. **C149.6:** `fieldReferences` pointers are into the version (`/flow/...`, `/copy/...`); a copy `{name}` placeholder counts as a reference only when another member references the field.
12. **Caps with their basis:** `PATTERN_PROGRAM_LIMIT` 2,048 (one match at most 10,000 × 2,048 steps), `VISIBILITY_RULE_LIMITS` depth 16 and 200 nodes, `MAX_FIELD_SCHEMAS` 1,000 (public), `SHORT_WINDOW_SECONDS` 72,000 (WCAG 2.2.1), a 500 ms CPU budget per pathological match (worst measured 74 ms).

Owner item: add `import { logicalPropertiesConfig } from './packages/config/eslint/logical-properties.js';` and `...logicalPropertiesConfig(),` after `...phase5Config(),`, with the root-config test of `flow-i18n`'s RED `4ab55add` (`calculateConfigForFile` gives the rule `[2]` for `apps/flow/src/app.tsx` and `packages/ui/src/button.tsx`, `undefined` for a dashboard file). The export script runs on Node's type stripping and Vite 8's `runnerImport` (no `tsx` in `apps/flow`); the flow's typecheck covers it since Stage 4.
18. **Decisions left open:** the catalog version is content-addressed (`web-` and 16 hex digits of the consent and notice texts' digest), so `consent-texts.json` grows only when such a text changes; the table holds every `consent.*` and `notice.*` leaf of each locale, unfilled, append-only (a test reads every committed revision); `language` before `lang`, each in the query before the fragment; the lint rule lets through a physical utility under an explicit `rtl:` or `ltr:` variant and counts only whole Tailwind utilities; `LocaleReviewStatus` `partially-reviewed` gains optional `by`, `date`, `catalogSha256`, and a review is recorded by archiving the catalog at `contracts/fixtures/i18n/reviewed/<locale>.<catalog-sha256>.json` (without it every key fails closed to unreviewed); `Widen<T>` admits `_zero`, `_two`, `_few`, `_many`; `<html lang>` names the full requested tag; the mirrored flag is `iconDirectionClass` with a source-scan test (no icon file to borrow, amendment 12).

## Workflow 9A Stage 3 amendments

Recorded by `integration-9a` from the Stage 3 reports. "Settled in Stage 4" points to the Stage 4 amendment that closed an item.

### api-flow-engine

20. **A flow-spec 1 version keeps serving its stored v1 flow (C151.6, amendment 34).** The default template's transitions migrate into branches, where the projection stops, so today's Android SDK would stop after the ID step of every v1 template with transitions. The projection is served for flow-spec 2 versions only; C151.4 still holds for v1 inquiries (a client acts only on the step the server names). Consequence, decided in Stage 4: `flowSessionFlowSchema` stays the union `flowProjectionSchema | flowSchema` and is **not** narrowed (S4-10). The alternative (`projection` treating a migrated status-only branch as unconditional) is the owner's to choose, with Phase 10.
21. **Missing hooks reported**, each settled in Stage 4: the session's `OpenVerify-Flow-Spec` and the flow CORS (S4-1), the upload's `capture-method` (S4-1), the inquiry's step names and `flow-outcome` (S4-4), `inquiry.completed`'s meta (S4-4). **Carried** to Workflow 9B (S4-12): the resume expiry window (`extendExpiryOnResumeInTx` in `POST /inquiries/:id/resume` and the one-time link redemption), the per-inquiry `meta.expiration-after-*` keys (two nullable `inquiries` columns), and the comparison source in the checks' evidence (`inquiryFieldSources` beside `inquiryFields` in `@openverify/checks`' references, `comparison-source` in the two comparison checks' metadata).
22. **Handoff exits on the server's position (C96.1, C96.3; left open):** continued-here and redeemed take `next`; a decline moves nothing; the desktop's rejection of a redeemed phone closes the phone's entry with port `rejected` (outcome `handoff-rejected`) and re-enters the handoff step (note `handoff_rejected`); `handoff-resolved` never positions an inquiry the engine never positioned; once the path left the handoff step, a late request is answered by Phase 7's own state machine, never `flow_step_not_current`.
23. **A handed-off session records its own consent after the flow passed its consent step (C94.6), which moves nothing.** The web flow shows that consent first since Stage 4 (S4-3). Carried: on a flow-spec 2 version the projection leaves out a consent the phone's session has not given, and `step` names only the current step, so a v2 phone has no consent step to show (the API should serve the passed consent step's client view to a session without consent); Workflow 9B (`contracts-9b` names the owner) or 9D's `fix-api`.
24. **Screen submission, decisions left open (C148.5):** a value for a field the screen does not bind is discarded; an empty optional value writes nothing; lengths count code points; `email` and `phone` use Phase 5's validators (any region without `allowedCountries`, stored as `+` and digits); `url` must be `https`; a date is `YYYY-MM-DD` within its bounds; the primary button is the submit button without a port (`next`); an unknown button is 422 at `/button`; a resubmission of a step already left answers 200, a redacted inquiry 410; an unpositioned inquiry is positioned first.
25. **Selfie-capture extension (C171.4):** `SELFIE_CAPTURE_EXTENSION_LIMIT` 10 (ours, WCAG 2.2.1's minimum; a capture's life at most 33 minutes), counted from `expires_at - created_at` (no column); only the current step's capture; an expired capture is refused as a submitted one is.
26. **Session members, decisions left open:** `embed.allowed-origins` is `embedPolicyFor`'s `frame-ancestors` (`[]` for an unknown inquiry or an inactive template); `completion` on every read (`redirect-url: null` when none); `values` only with `step`; "custom consent copy" is an override of a consent text key in any locale; `first_platform` is `android` for the Android SDK and `web` otherwise; the session poll ends a wait and settles a waiting `run_workflow`.
27. **Scope, decisions left open (C150.2):** `lists` is empty; `session.resumed` is more than one session; `session.handoff.state` is Phase 7's state of the first handoff step with a row; `device.kind` from the newest session that reported one; `inquiry.fields` stay on the server; a check null whose reasons are an outage or an unconfigured provider is review class, as is a snapshot null whose reason is not `ignore`.
28. **Races:** the timeout sweep racing a run's completion is tested at the engine (two `action-finished` exits leave the step once); the run's cancellation is `api-flow-actions`'.
29. **Existing tests changed by C151.4 and the positioning session:** routes/flow tests seed or reach the steps before their target (`government-id-nfc-submit`, `inputs`, `phase5-create-upload`, `phase5-submit`, `verifications`), `selfie-capture-create` and `-submit` seed the ID attempt, `selfie.test-support` puts the selfie after consent, `kyb-flow.test.ts` and `services/otp/testing.ts` pass the earlier steps, `session-cap-objects.test.ts` positions at the handoff step, `flow-handoffs-cases.test.ts` records consent first, `api.integration.test.ts` decides the ID before the selfie.
30. **For the docs:** a list screening that later finds the inquiry in `needs_review` records `inquiry_already_decided` without a move (written in `docs/flows/engine.md`).

### api-flow-actions

31. **The grant a `run_workflow` target was published against has no column (C152.2).** `run-workflow.ts` reads `flowRunGrants` from the version row (`recordedGrantOf`), which does not exist, so a `latest-published` target is held to no scope and no PII (fail closed) and its egress to the template's `egress:<step>` acknowledgement; a pinned `wfv_` is checked against its own immutable grant. **Carried** to Workflow 9B (S4-12): `flow_run_grants jsonb not null default '[]'` on `inquiry_template_versions` in 9B's migration, `flowRunGrants: FlowRunGrantRecord[]` on the row, and the publish storing `flowActionPublishGrants(...)` and requiring `flowActionAcknowledgements(...)` (both built, neither called).
32. **The flow-failure record (C150.7)** is written through `createInquiryDecision` with Phase 8's own `adverseRefusal` gates, because `recordInquiryDecisionInTx` takes an `AuditActor` (never `inquiry-flow`) and has no `flow` member. An `explicit-consent` basis without the recorded `consent.automated-decision` answers `automated_decision_consent_missing` (review). A fact the scope cannot type is recorded as H/beta, never as more certain; routes count up to the one taken, `else` all of them.
33. **A written but null snapshot (`not_yet_collected`) does not end the wait at once:** the walk waits while any `waitFor` fact is null, so the branch follows its own `onNull` at `maxSeconds`, with no review (ignore class). A snapshot with values ends the wait at once; a named signal that is not flow-provisional is never computed.
34. **The sweep's events:** settled in Stage 4 (S4-5).
35. **`copy_extractions`, decisions left open:** a decided verification is `passed` or `needs_review`; the inquiry must be `created` or `pending` (`inquiry_not_open`); the field rules' codes become the error port's; a `null` extraction is never written; the audit entry is the system's `inquiry.updated` with `field-keys` and no value.
36. **C174.8's `workflow_grant_widened` and the other flow action error codes** joined `reference-docs.ts` in Stage 4 (S4-7).
37. **Publish problem codes (C152.2):** `workflow_unknown`, `workflow_unpublished`, `workflow_trigger_invalid`, `workflow_grant_missing`, as `errors[]` entries of `assertPublishable`'s 422, naming only the missing permission names. The sweep logs a failure under `reason` (the C53.8 lint refuses `code` in a log call).

43. **`If-Match` and `ETag` are not in the OpenAPI document** (read from the headers through `ifMatchHeadersSchema`; the frozen route schemas are unchanged). Carried, optional: `headers: ifMatchHeadersSchema` on PATCH and publish and an `ETag` response header on the four version routes.
44. **Existing tests changed:** `packages/db/src/repositories/inquiry-templates.test.ts` (a template holding only a draft is served as `null`), `phase9a-template-tables.test.ts` (the stub case removed), `phase9a-template-plumbing.test.ts` (the built routes leave the 501 list), `inquiry-template-flow-v2.test.ts` (drafts take v2; template creation stays v1-only).
45. **Decisions left open:** (a) a template holding only a draft (the first promote) is not listed or served until its draft is published; (b) copy makes the new template's version 1 published from a published or superseded version only, carrying its validation, digest and acknowledgements, with a deployment and its events; (c) an acknowledgement is a person's: an API key cannot publish a version that needs one; (d) every publish writes a deployment, but `POST /inquiry-templates` (version 1 published at once) writes none and fires no event (carried, optional: route it through `deployInTx`); (e) `COPY_KEYS` holds 20 keys; a biometric consent override must state `{days}`, every other key and component text takes `{organization}` only, button labels none; any C162.2 attribute is accepted on any component of a screen of the flow, an option only by its key; (f) `locale-unreviewed:<locale>` is required in production when the flow has a consent or screen step and an enabled locale is neither source nor reviewed (sandbox warns `locale_unreviewed`); (g) a Phase 8 workflow output has a name and a PII mark, not a type, so a PII output types as string and a non-PII one as number, and a route comparing a non-PII boolean output with `true` may be refused until outputs carry types; (h) promote re-resolves list-config screen lists (kind and name) and `run_workflow` targets (`latest-published` only; a pinned `wfv_` is `promote_unresolved`), copying report templates and signal sets as they are; (i) the diff keys v1 flows through `migrateFlow`; consequences built: `required_check_relaxed`, `verification_step_removed`, `consent_text_changed`, `unreviewed_locale_enabled` (not built: `ga_check_replaced_by_beta`, `verification_step_skippable`); (j) used-by reports report-template and case-template references as `[]` and 0 and scans only published workflow versions; (k) validate in save mode answers the save rules, in publish mode the publish report plus every async hook's problems, both with `acknowledgements-required`; (l) a CSV import with a parser or header problem refuses the whole file, a cell for a locale the draft does not enable is `locale_unsupported`; (m) the consent check compares every digest in `text-keys` then `optional-text-keys` order, a count mismatch is `consent_text_mismatch`, and the base locale keys `consent-texts.json`.

### flow-renderer

46. **The client header and CORS** (C151.7, HIGH): settled in Stage 4 (S4-1). The mocked suite's preflight list in `apps/flow/e2e/support/mock-flow-api.ts` was left as it is (Playwright's `page.route` does not route preflights).
47. **The version's link fields are not in the session (C162.3):** a `link` button is left out while its URL is unknown, and the help slot holds only the language selector. Carried to Workflow 9B (`contracts-9b`): `links { help-url, privacy-url }` in `flowSessionResponseSchema`, served by the session builder.
48. **No flow route cancels a session (C148.2):** a confirmed `cancel` shows "You stopped for now" and the inquiry stays resumable; the embed bridge tells the page since Stage 4 (S4-9). An API cancel needs a contract and a route (not planned).
49. **`prefillFrom` on `database` and AAMVA `form` steps (C148.7, amendment 18(a)):** the client view leaves the input-to-field map out and the session keys `values` by field key, so the client prefills an input only where the field key equals the input's name. Carried to Workflow 9B (`contracts-9b` names the owner): serve those `values` by input name, or keep the map in the client view.
50. **`intlLocale` missed inside components** (keyed by the instance, while `useTranslation()` answers a copy): settled in Stage 4 (S4-6); `useIntlLocale()` stays.
51. **i18next keeps the module catalogs by reference:** `applySessionCopy` gives the store its own `structuredClone` before its first write; every other writer of i18n resources must do the same.
52. **Startup language:** `bootstrapSession` moves a fragment's `language`, `lang` and `style-variant` into the query it rewrites (never a credential); `App` reads them once, negotiates the language among `locales.enabled`, and binds `styleVariant` (prop, else the URL's) into `applySessionTheme`.
53. **`<bdi>`:** values with a direction of their own (the OTP destination, a file's name and size, the handoff device, the wallet's network and name, the organisation) are isolated; counts and times stay plain text (the bidi algorithm keeps European numbers in order, and `Intl` formats them in the person's digits).
54. **A visibility rule outside the strict subset** shows the screen as unavailable (never a guess) while the server hides such a component; publish refuses these rules.
55. **The client's transition walk is removed (C151.5):** a session with no `position` (a pre-Phase-9 server or a test) is read as the projection in list order, transitions ignored. `apps/flow/src/flow/json-logic.ts` and its test are no longer imported and were left in place (carried, optional: remove them; 9D's `fix-flow`). `UnsupportedReason` is `'step'` only.
56. **Capture method (C153.2):** `capture-method` goes after any selfie capture fields and before `file`; the method is remembered by photo in a `WeakMap`.
57. **The 2.2.1 warning (C171.4)** follows the limit on the client's monotonic clock from the capture's opening, moved by each extension's change to `expires-at`; the client never compares clocks with the server.
58. **The language selector** sits in a fixed `footer[data-slot=help]` after `main` (3.2.6), offering only `locales.enabled` once a session names them.
59. **Borrowed edits:** one import and one `screen` member in each `apps/flow/src/i18n/locales/{en,es,fr,de,ar}.ts`, and the regenerated `contracts/fixtures/i18n/*.json` (consent texts and catalog version unchanged); `src/handoff/qr-code.tsx` names its icon kind.

61. **The contest route's framing words** (`src/steps/contest-route-words.ts`) exist in `en` and `es` only. Carried to 9D's `fix-flow`: move them into the catalogs in all five locales, marked machine-drafted.

### flow-server-embed

62. **Existing tests changed** because the behaviour their stubs asserted was built: `apps/api/src/phase9a-embed-plumbing.test.ts`, `apps/flow/src/phase9a-slots.test.tsx`, and `packages/sdk-js/src/index.test.ts` (deleted, replaced by the client's tests).
63. **The flow server's tests live in `apps/flow/src/server/*.test.ts`** (Vitest collects `src/**`); `server.ts` itself is outside the coverage include. `isInquiryId` copies `isId('inq', ...)`'s fixed pattern (the server imports no package index, V04), held equal by a test over eleven vectors.
64. **Decisions left open:** a traversal spelled into an `/embed/` path is 400 before the entry is chosen, a malformed id proper 404 with `'none'` and no lookup; the bucket counts only well-formed embed loads; 404 and invalid answers are cached as refusals for 60 s, network failures, timeouts and other statuses answer `'none'` uncached; concurrent lookups of one id share one request; extensionless files under `licenses/` are served as text, every other file only by its extension.
65. **The preview reads the console origin from a `<meta>` (`openverify-console-origin`) the flow server injects** from `OPENVERIFY_DASHBOARD_PUBLIC_URL`, the same source as its `frame-ancestors`; without the server the preview accepts nothing.
66. **Missing hook, the embed entry's start:** `EmbedEntryProps` hands the entry only the route's `<Start start={start} />`, so the entry reads `flow.props.start` and wraps its client (`observeFlowClient`); a cleaner seam (a session observer `flow-renderer` calls) is carried, optional, to 9D's `fix-flow`.
67. **The cancel:** settled in Stage 4 (S4-9).
68. **The mocked suite** serves the console and customer pages from loopback servers (Chromium's local network access refuses a page fulfilled by `page.route` that frames the loopback flow server), and installs the shared mock through a page whose `url()` is the flow origin. Carried, optional: the shared mock should answer CORS with the request's `Origin`.
69. **C178.3's font half is not proven to render:** no decodable, permissively licensed WOFF2 exists without committing a font (the only ones under `node_modules` are OFL-1.1), so the spec posts a buffer that sniffs as WOFF2 and the preview answers `asset-refused` because it does not decode, with no request and no CSP violation. The logo half renders. Carried to Workflow 9B (`api-themes`, with an operator font fixture it may commit under its licence decision).
70. **C171.2 embedded coverage:** the embedded axe run covers 10 step types; `selfie`, `completion`, `failure`, `screen`, `device_handoff` and `nfc` are not run embedded. Carried to 9D (`a11y-audit`).
71. **The preview's chrome is English and marked `lang="en"`** (decision 33); steps render in the document's locale.

Recorded by `integration-9a` on 2026-11-05, run alone after every other 9A agent, from `0c7eb7ca`. Evidence: `docs/tdd/phase9-integration-9a.tdd.md`. Items are `S4-<n>`.

S4-1. **The flow routes' hooks (C151.7, C153.2).** `GET /flow/session` reads `OpenVerify-Flow-Spec` and names the version served in the response header; the flow CORS allows and exposes `openverify-flow-spec`; an upload's `capture-method` field (`FILE_CAPTURE_METHODS`, 400 otherwise) reaches `files.capture_method` (`UPLOAD_LIMITS` 6 fields and 7 parts). Without them every cross-origin flow failed its preflight and the web flow never received `step` or `values`. ## Workflow 9B Stage 1a amendments (contracts-9b)

Recorded by `contracts-9b` from 2026-11-06, run alone, on `6a290723` (Workflow 9B's base). The re-verification behind each item is `docs/research/phase9-inventory.md`, sections 12 to 16, with every `path:line`; the evidence is `docs/tdd/phase9-contracts-9b.tdd.md`, with each family's checkpoint commit. Items are `B<n>`; where an item contradicts a contract above, the item wins.

**Re-verification (9A as built, S4-12, Phases 8, 7 and 6)**

B1. **The gitleaks rule covers the handoff token too (C169.4, review P33).** `packages/contracts/src/tokens.ts` holds Phase 7's `ovh_` (`HANDOFF_TOKEN_PREFIX`) beside `ovs_`, `ovu_`, `ovo_`, `ovm_` and `ovi_`, each 43 base64url characters, so the rule is `(ov_(sbx|prod)|ov[suomih])_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}`, bounded on both sides, and not the plan's `ov[suomi]_`. Its entropy floor is 3.5 bits per character, measured (C169.4's "set from the measured fixtures"): the tracked tokens without "synthetic" score 0.30 and 0.59, those with it 2.63 to 4.25, and 20,000 random tokens 4.43 at the lowest. The test is `infra/tests/gitleaks-config.test.sh` (run in CI's `infra` job, held there by `ci-workflow.test.sh`); its tracked-token case lists the two low-entropy values (`ovo_` and `ovs_` of one repeated letter) instead of renaming them, because they predate the rule and fall under the floor; every new token carries "synthetic".

B2. **`updated_at` indexes (C163.4).** The dirty-day scan needs an `(environment_id, updated_at)` index on `inquiries` (it has only the partial retention index `inquiries_retention_updated_at_idx`), `verifications` and `verification_checks` (none). `cases` already has `cases_environment_id_updated_at_id_idx`. `foundation-9b` (A) adds the three and none on `cases`.

B3. **There is no document-level response serializer (C169.3).** `plugins/api-version.ts`'s `onSend` hook (`:118-125`) only sets `OpenVerify-Version` on server-API routes; responses are serialized by the zod serializer compiler (`app.ts:147`) and masked per route. `applyKeyAttributeBlocklist`'s hook site is therefore a new `onSend` hook in `plugins/api-version.ts`, registered after the existing one, for an API-key principal only, on the serialized JSON payload (parsed, stripped recursively, re-serialized): a strip before serialization would fail the strict response schemas whenever a required attribute is blocklisted. The plugin is registered before `idempotency` and `api-logs` (`app.ts:214-216`), whose `onSend` hooks run after it, so a replayed answer and a stored API log hold the stripped body. `foundation-9b` (W) places the call; `api-keys-webhooks` fills the stub.

B4. **The user-session hook site (C167.2, review F29) is confirmed:** `findUserSessionContext` (`operator-auth/session-context.ts:42`) computes `permissionsForRoles(roles)` at `:83`; `permissionsForEnvironment` replaces that call. Sign-in's environment defaults to `sandbox` at `operator-auth/operator-auth.ts:271`, then the other kind (`:299-300`), which `api-access` changes to the first environment with a role.

B5. **Ids (C174.1), as built in common:** `export: 'exp'`, `savedView: 'svw'`, `reportBulkJob: 'rbulk'` in both prefix tables (`packages/contracts/src/ids.ts`, `packages/db/src/ids.ts`).

B6. **Scopes and roles (C167.1), as built in common:** `analytics:read`, `inquiries:export`, `verifications:export`, `accounts:export`, `reports:export`, `lists:export`, `transactions:export`, appended in that order to `API_SCOPES`. Admin holds all; developer and reviewer gain `analytics:read`; viewer reads it through `VIEWER_PERMISSIONS` (it ends in `:read` and is not in `NOT_FOR_VIEWERS`, whose comment now says why); no built-in role but admin holds an `:export`. The console's `PERMISSION_INFO` gains an "Analytics" area (after "Cases"), and each export sits in its object's area: "Export inquiries", "Export verifications", "Export accounts", "Export reports", "Export lists and their items", "Export transactions"; "View analytics". The refusal tests are core's (`permissions.test.ts`: each export refused to developer, reviewer and viewer through `missingPermissions`; `analytics:read` refused to a holder of `inquiries:read` alone). Route-level refusal tests come with the routes (`foundation-9b`'s frozen routes and their owners). Existing tests changed by the plan: contracts `vocabulary.test.ts`, dashboard `permissions.test.ts`, `permission-checklist.test.tsx`, `role-options.test.ts`, and the golden `contracts/fixtures/api/role.json` (the reviewer role).

B7. **Problems (C174.8), as built in common: all nine codes 9A did not add**, so the route schemas of (X), (U) and (C) only reference them:
- `EXPORT_PROBLEM_STATUSES` (`problems/exports/`): `saved_view_pii_filter` 422 (the pointer names the filter key, never its value), `export_too_large` 422 (Phase 8's case export code, with its status, by test);
