## Workflow 9B Stage 2 amendments

Recorded by `integration-9b` from `analytics-logic`'s report (`docs/tdd/phase9-analytics-logic.tdd.md`). Each item binds 9C and 9D.

### analytics-logic

AL1. **Strict rate bounds (C164.7).** An "under X%" bound is `X = floor(100(t-1)/D) + 1` and an "over Y%" bound is `Y = ceil(100(D-t+1)/D) - 1`, so a bound is always passed and never met: 10 of 40 is 25%, which "under 25%" does not describe. A rate whose numerator and complement are both below `t` (a denominator from `t` to `2t-2`) is `{suppressed: true}`: no one-sided bound describes it. A test compares every N/D up to 80 at `t` = 11, 15 and 20 with a bound searched from its definition.

AL2. **When a rate is served (C164.7).** `AnalyticsTable.rate` serves an exact value only when every cell it sums is shown; a bound only when every denominator cell is shown, "under" only when no numerator cell is hidden as complementary, and "over" only when no numerator cell is hidden at all. Otherwise `{suppressed: true}`. An exact rate over a shown denominator would reveal a hidden numerator, and a bound read from a hidden denominator would narrow it.

AL3. **Complementary suppression is exact.** The plan's per-group rule runs first (one hidden cell left in a group hides its smallest shown non-zero part, else the total; zeros are never hidden), then an exact integer check of linear derivability hides, for every hidden cell still determined, the smallest shown non-zero cell its derivation reads, until nothing is determined. The time axis is one lattice (day, week from Monday cut to the window, month cut to the window, total) whatever bucket is asked, so every bucket of a window hides the same cells. `suppressAnalyticsTable` is the one function every answer, CSV and caption is built from.

AL4. **What suppression does not cover** (for `docs/analytics/` and the 9C console): bounds the labels imply. A small-count cell is 1 to `t-1`, and a complementary one is at least `t` because zeros are never hidden, so a total close to its hidden parts' least sum narrows them (protection-interval inference).

AL5. **The additive relations** (`ANALYTICS_RELATIONS`, ours, holding once a window settles; the rollup counts so they hold): inquiries created = started + expired-before-start, started = finished + expired-after-start, finished = completed + failed + approved + declined + needs-review, completed = the three completed splits (counted over inquiries now completed); verifications created = submitted + canceled + skipped, submitted = passed + failed + requires-retry + needs-review, step-entered = the four step outcomes; checks runs = the six statuses. `decided-*`, the funnel and the case counts declare none; a caller serving such a population adds its relation through `AnalyticsTableInput.groups`.

AL6. **Step share and the funnel (C164.2).** `step-share` is absent for a step with no transition into it or whose entries exceed the transitions into it, which is the entry step, because `analytics_transition_daily` records no transition from the start; its overall share is its figure. `funnelFigures` takes the rows of one template version, refusing anything else, because its `started` is that version's denominator. Proposed migration, carried to 9D (S4B-14): a reserved start `from_step_id` in `analytics_transition_daily`.

AL7. **Checks (C164.4).** `failure-share` is absent when a check's failed results exceed the failed verifications of its type (an advisory check fails without failing the verification) or when there are none. Reason counts are `counts['reason:<code>']`. `checkFigures` takes results of one (check, type, requirement); `analytics_check_reason_daily` has no type or requirement column, so a check used both ways has its reasons combined (proposed migration carried to 9D, S4B-14). A check with no counted result, or only rows of count 0, is "not run": empty figures, served `ran: false`.

AL8. **Signal bins (C164.6).** The frozen `SIGNAL_BINS` of B13 stand; each basis is ours and provisional, tuned on nothing: `bot_score` ten equal bins of 0-100 (the last includes 100); `automation_artefact_count` and `list_match_count` 0, 1, 2-4, 5+; `session_count` 0, 1, 2, 3-5, 6+; `handoff_count` 0, 1, 2, 3+; `max_session_distance` 0-49, 50-499, 500-1,999 and 2,000+ km, binned on whole kilometres of the stored metres; enum and boolean signals by value; `country_from_session` by upper-case alpha-2 code, the codes present listed sorted, then `not-evaluated`. `list_match_count` comes through `listMatchCountValue`: a completed screening gives its match count, anything else `null`, never 0. `signalFigures` keys counts by bin name, every fixed bin listed with its zeros.

AL9. **Cases (C164.5).** `caseFigures` includes `assigned` only when assignee rows are passed; the per-reviewer distribution is `caseAssigneeFigures`, former users combined as one `null` row listed last, served only to `users:write` holders and never exported by name.

AL10. **Durations (C163.5).** Edge k is 2,592,000^(k/40), the first bucket starting at 0 and the last holding 30 days and more; percentiles take the nearest rank (the smallest bucket whose running count reaches ceil(p/100 n)); `suppressDuration` hides a duration over fewer than `t` values and answers `null` over none.

AL11. **Composition rules for the answers (C164.8).** A cell served by two answers comes from one table (the funnel's per-bucket `started` is the inquiries table's); the live day is left out of every outcome series; `suppressed-cells` is `table.suppressedAmong(the ids served)`; the table is built over the full lattice of the window after every filter. `filter[tag]` reads the tag rollup's four counts as series without relations (no figures function for it).

AL12. **Exports beside the frozen names** (re-exported through modules the frozen index lists, as B11 allows): `AnalyticsInputError` (a 500 problem, its message naming the input, never the value), `assertCount`, `ANALYTICS_RELATIONS`, `FUNNEL_TOP_PATHS` (20), `checkReasonKey`, `caseAssigneeFigures`, `listMatchCountValue`, `analyticsTimeLattice`, `analyticsBucketNodes`, `suppressDuration`, `analyticsSeriesCellId`, `analyticsRelationGroups`, `suppressAnalyticsTable` and their types. No frozen signature changed.

## Workflow 9B Stage 3 amendments

Recorded by `integration-9b` from the Stage 3 reports, each worded as a binding decision. Where `integration-9b` settled a deviation, the settling commit is named; anything carried names its owner in the Stage 4 section (S4B-14).

### api-themes

TH1. **Built-in tokens (C160.3, C160.4).** The twelve built-in tokens are stored as hex in `packages/ui/src/styles.css`, converted once from the oklch values (method in `docs/themes/README.md`), and held equal to `BUILTIN_PALETTES` (`@openverify/core`, the source Phase 10's C221.8 exports). The built-in light `focus` is `#000000`, not the converted `#155dfc`, which is 1.302:1 on `primary`, below the 3:1 C160.4 asks for focus on primary; so the console's focus rings are black. **Owner to confirm**, or drop the focus-on-primary pair from C160.4.

TH2. **The built-in dark palette** applies only under `:root[data-openverify-variant='dark']`, which only the flow sets; the console keeps its light look. With no theme set, the session carries no `theme` member (9A amendment 4).

TH3. **Theme queries** live in `apps/api/src/services/themes/store.ts`, not in `packages/db/src/repositories/{theme-sets,theme-assets}.ts`, because the repository index was shared in 9B; moving them is optional.

TH4. **Asset rules.** A font upload by an API key answers 403, since `theme_assets.licence_attested_by` holds a `usr_` id (decision 27). Restore re-runs the save rules; PATCH or restore of an archived set answers 422; a second DELETE answers the archived set unchanged; a restore of a revision the set lacks answers 422 at `/meta/revision`. Per-component colour overrides are not built: flow-spec 2 has no per-component colour member.

TH5. **Settled by `integration-9b`:** `ApiDependencies.vision` (the logo rendition reaches vision on the route, `2c9402ce`/`44937cc0`); the flow's asset loader (`FlowApiClient.getThemeAsset`, the renderer applying each theme with a loader, `ThemeBranding` in the layout header reading the loader the theme was applied with, `101a19f8`, `bd423683`, `b1f1a681`); the promote's `mayWriteThemeSets` read in the target environment from stored assignments (`7cb44855`/`600cd219`); 503 declared on `POST /theme-sets/assets` and the frozen 501s dropped (`80ed770f`). **Carried:** the promote does not pass `assets` to `promoteThemeReferenceInTx`, so a set with a logo or font answers `promote_unresolved` rather than being copied (fail closed); the session has no `organization-display-name` outside `handoff-context` and the uploaded font no `unicode-range` member (contract changes); `ProblemMembers` does not admit a list of records (ThemeProblemError casts). The real-font proof stays carried (no WOFF2 generator in either lockfile).

### api-templates-carry

TC1. **Simulate (C156.4) as built:** person steps exit `next`; a simulated `failed` or `canceled` verification takes `exhausted` where the step has it, else the inquiry fails; an unsimulated verification or a `run_workflow` with no simulated status stops the path (pending); a verification's facts enter the scope once its step is entered; waits run to their deadline; an enabled check left out counts passed, `null` is an errored check of the review class, `not_applicable` without a reason blocks the decision (`needs_review`), and a status its checks contradict is a 422; default error types are `timeout`, `run_canceled` and `run_errored`; `copy_extractions` and `tag_inquiry` act on the simulated scope, `update_account_fields` writes nothing. Not simulated: the C150.7 decision-record gate, and a reason for a simulated `null` signal. Refusals are 422 with no new code at `/meta/scope/<pointer>`, or `/meta/preset-id` with the inner path in `detail`.

TC2. **Built-in presets:** 22 ids `builtin:<API verification type>-passed|failed`, served with `scope: null` and `created-at: null`; at simulate every step of the named type passes or fails and every other verification step passes; a passing government ID carries synthetic extractions (US, `dl`, a birthdate 30 years before now, an expiry 5 years after). A user's preset scope is served `null` without `pii:view` (C174.5), the user's own included.

TC3. **Publish checks (C158.2, C158.4):** a version's `embed-origins` entry must appear exactly on the environment's `allowed-embed-origins` (no wildcard cover), matching `allowedEmbedOrigins`; `redirect-uri-schemes` are re-checked against the grammar and the denylist. These run at publish and in validate's publish mode, not on save; problems never echo an origin or a URI.

TC4. **`templateIsBiometric`:** true when the draft's own flow holds a selfie, else Phase 8's `biometricTemplates` for the published version, else `null` (fail closed). `workflows/publish/facts.ts` exports `biometricTemplates` (a borrowed edit).

TC5. **Flow-spec 2 template creation:** version 1 is drafted on a template held back from reads, validated, held to every publish rule with no acknowledgements and published in one transaction; a flow needing an acknowledgement is drafted and published with its acknowledgements instead. No deployment and no event, as for v1.

TC6. **Settled by `integration-9b`:** the 501s of simulate and the preset routes dropped, and 422 declared on `POST /inquiry-templates` (`80ed770f`); the C178.4 replay of 9A's branching inquiries through simulate (`322aefba`, four inquiries, same path port by port, same status and `flow-outcome`). **Carried:** `docs/flows/` text for simulate's semantics and its two known differences (9C's docs or 9D).

### api-templates-finish

TF1. **The preview (C156.1)** answers every renderable step's client view (a v1 flow migrated), the theme from `resolveThemeForVersion` with the contrast answer (a failing palette answered, never refused), the copy for an enabled locale (its own texts, a component key's default-locale text, a built-in key left to the client's catalog) or, for `en-XA` and `ar-XB`, every `en` text built-in ones included through `pseudoLocalizeTree`, and configuration-only guidance (sides, poses, a consent text's `days` and `organization`), never a field value or default. 422 at `/meta/locale` for a locale the version does not enable, at `/data/attributes/theme-set-id` for a set not active here, under `/data/attributes/styles` for contradictions, at `/data/attributes/flow` for a stored flow that cannot be read. Nothing is stored. The golden `template-preview.json` stays illustrative (rounded ratio, no consent guidance), valid against the schema.

TF2. **Run grants (C152.2):** a draft's publish and a flow-spec 2 creation store `flowActionPublishGrants(...)` in `flow_run_grants` after every publish rule; a copy keeps its source's; a non-draft publish passes nothing. The API unit gate ran as 12 shards because 6 passed the call cap at that load. **Settled by `integration-9b`:** the preview's 501 dropped (`80ed770f`).

### api-flow-carry

FC1. **comparison-source (B47):** keyed by compared field as `compared-fields`; a field is `initial` only when every key it read that holds a value was set at creation, `entered` otherwise (a key the sources do not name included, fail closed); left out when no sources or no reference; `{}` when nothing was compared. Check implementation versions unchanged. Borrowed edits: `jobs/government-id.ts` and `jobs/types/database.ts` pass the sources.

FC2. **Session links (C162.3):** both members served whenever the engine positioned the inquiry, each `null` unless an https URL the contract accepts; an inquiry the engine never positioned has no `links`. **Prefill by input name (B48):** a database or AAMVA form step's `values` are keyed by input name, an AAMVA input's hyphens read as underscores (today's web flow reads them so). **The handed-off v2 phone (B48):** a session without its own consent on an active position past the flow's first consent step is served that consent's client view as `step`, its position unchanged; recording that consent moves nothing.

FC3. **Settled by `integration-9b`:** the renderer reports each session's links (`caed6205`/`e2a11b8b`) and the position falls back to the served consent step when the v2 projection leaves it out (`47a17a4e`/`4b4d921c`). **Carried:** `docs/flows/` text for prefill by input name, the phone's consent and the links (FC2's wording), for 9C's docs or 9D.

### api-flow-expiry

FE1. **The expiry windows (C151.10):** creation sets `expires-at` from `meta.expiration-after-create-interval-seconds`, else the version's; the start takes the inquiry's after-start window, else the version's (and may shorten); a resume and a one-time link redemption take the inquiry's after-resume window, else the version's, as `max(expires-at, now + window)`; link creation takes the request's `expires-in-seconds`, else the inquiry's own window, else the version's, capped at `expires-at`. Ours: a decided or redacted inquiry is never moved, and a start or resume never revives an inquiry at or past its `expires-at`. The three windows are served on every inquiry resource, `null` when the version decides; they are set at creation only.

FE2. **Settled by `integration-9b`:** link creation calls `oneTimeLinkLifetimeSeconds` (`6fad7ae7`/`efa1074d`), with the route's description. **Carried to 9D:** the flow paths that start an inquiry outside the engine (`openInquiry`, `getFlowSession`'s fallback for an unwalkable flow) skip the after-start window, and a Phase 7 handoff redemption opens a session without extending the expiry.

### api-analytics

AA1. **Filters the rollups cannot apply are refused, never ignored (C164.8):** the step, transition, path, verification, verification-step, check and check-reason rollups have no `platform` or `device_kind`, so `GET /analytics/{funnel,verifications,checks}` answer 400 to `filter[platform]` or `filter[device-kind]`. With `filter[tag]` the overview serves the tag rollup's four counts only, no rates, `time-to-finish` null.

AA2. **Unsettled windows.** Core's exact check treats each relation as exact, so an unsettled window (a pending inquiry is started but neither finished nor expired) made it throw. The API adds to each relation an `<total>.unsettled` remainder part (never served, counted as known to a reader, the cautious side); a relation whose parts exceed its total on a day is not declared. Carried (S4B-14): build the remainder into `suppressAnalyticsTable` or document it in core.

AA3. **Redaction (C163.4) as built:** on an open stored day the contribution moves from facts to redacted rows under a savepoint (facts plus redacted unchanged); when it cannot (the inquiry changed since the last full recompute) that one day is recomputed at once without the inquiry, an extra outcome visibility point for that day only; closed days stay frozen; the live day gets a redacted row only. The first build counts only inquiries unredacted at the time. Live days run from the local day of the last watermark; before the first pass every day is live; week, month and total buckets holding a live day are `live: false` and sum outcomes over stored days only.

AA4. **Definitions left open, as built:** finished = completed or failed; the completed splits come from `flow_outcome`; marked-for-review = finished and (needs_review, flags, a review decision record or an attempt in needs_review); `decided-*` reads the latest approve, decline or flow-failure record (`solely_automated` first, else by actor type); interacted = the step record's first entry exited; a step's verification outcome is the last sent flow-created attempt of its type; cases resolved, `sla-met`, `sla-missed` and resolution time from the case columns. A not-run check uses the registry's default requirement. A signal value outside its bins is left out and logged by name and count.

AA5. **The settings hook** refuses a zone `pg_timezone_names` does not list (422 at `/analytics-time-zone`); the pass compares the state's zone with the setting to rebuild. **The benchmark** gains `--teardown` beside the frozen `--seed` and `--measure`; `--seed` is resumable and took 3 calls. Answers are not cached (reads at most 0.4 s at 400 days on 1 M inquiries).

AA6. **Settled by `integration-9b`:** 501 dropped from the six routes (`80ed770f`). **Carried (S4B-14):** `analytics_rollup_state.closed_through` so a raised settle window never reopens a closed day; device dimensions on the step, verification and check rollups if 9C needs those filters; a user deletion path calling `forgetAnalyticsAssigneeInTx` first (none exists).

### api-access

AC1. **Borrowed edit recorded:** `packages/db/src/repositories/case-queues.ts` `pullNextCaseId` filters with `templateVisibleTo(reader, environmentId, cases.caseTemplateId)` (`f3a3714f`), passing the environment as a parameter: a correlated column turned the visibility check into a hash join and a sort, failing Phase 8's R008 read budget. Other callers passing a column stay correct but evaluate the subquery per row.

AC2. **Decisions:** a step-up with a wrong factor answers 401 `mfa_code_invalid` and counts against the sign-in limiter; a passkey step-up keeps its challenge in an `mfa_challenges` row whose token is never handed out; the MFA reset on oneself answers 409, its notice is sent after the commit with its outcome audited; reactivation clears the password and every assignment and keeps the factors; the resend needs the superset rule and no step-up; a role edit adding an organisation-level permission to a narrowly assigned role is 422 `role_scope_invalid`; last-seen is `user_sessions.updated_at`, moved at most once a minute; the device summary comes from the sign-in audit entry's sealed user agent; `last-sign-in-at` is the newest `user-session.created`; `mfa-enabled` is served to `users:write` holders only (P20); `filter[action-prefix]` matches the served kebab spelling; `filter[ip]` canonicalises IPv6 and answers 503 without a blind-index key. **Owner to confirm:** `PATCH /dashboard/users/:id` with `status: active` keeps its Phase 2 suspension meaning (roles kept), now under the superset rule and the step-up when the actor has a factor; or refuse it once 9C's console uses `/reactivate`.

AC3. **Settled by `integration-9b`:** the server API's `GET /user-audit-logs` serves the explorer's filters through `listAuditExplorerDocument` with PII visibility (it answered them unfiltered: fail open, `241e4cdd`/`4d0fd563`); an organisation setting's change is audited in every environment (`281c30b6`/`a086e092`); both processes give the audit IP index the configured key (`f240d22e`/`735cf39a`); the inquiry template promote reads the target's rights from stored assignments (`5cc72469`/`433db78e`); the access routes' 501s dropped and 503 declared on the dashboard audit list (`80ed770f`).

### api-keys-webhooks

KW1. **Borrowed edit recorded (W7):** `apps/api/src/services/webhooks.ts` stores, changes, clones and serves `name`, `description`, `failure-alert` and `previous-secret-expires-at` (`780bb7bf`).

KW2. **W3 cannot hold as written:** the `webhooks_set_updated_at` trigger moves `updated_at` whenever the sweep sets `failure_alert_notified_at`, so a webhook's `updated-at` moves when an alert fires. Carried to 9D (a migration): skip rows where only that column changed, or move the alert time to its own table.

KW3. **The sender name (B33):** `formatSender` answers `"<raw name>" <address>` and nodemailer writes the RFC 2047 encoding (an encoded word inside quotes is forbidden by RFC 2047 section 5). **Settled by `integration-9b`:** `mailSenderNameSchema` refuses `\p{Cf}` and `=?` at save (`47ce169c`/`4d384520`). **Carried (S4B-14):** the other per-environment senders (`workflows/actions/messaging/messenger.ts`, `jobs/cases/notification-emails.ts`, `services/otp/send.ts`, `services/handoff/flow-handoffs.ts`) do not pass `mail-sender-name`; Phase 8's case notifications and case emails do not read `notification_preferences`, so those choices are stored and served but not applied (each a read of the preference at the queue site).

KW4. **Decisions:** clone copies the blocklist and note; rotating an already-ended key is allowed (its end stays earlier); preference changes are not audited; simulated and replayed deliveries count as first attempts; deliveries created more than a day before the window are not scanned; alerts run for enabled webhooks only; a `webhook-failure` row stays readable after its webhook is archived. `api-exports` calls `blockExportColumns` and stores the key's blocklist with the export.

### api-exports

EX1. **Decisions:** `export_too_large` is decided by the job (`failed` with that code past 50,000 rows or 64 MiB, `EXPORT_BYTE_LIMIT`, provisional), never truncated; verifications have their own export filter (`exportVerificationsFilterSchema`); a saved view cannot name a verifications or lists export; the delivery search's `links.next` is the last delivery's id to post as `page.after`; saved views are unpaged, oldest first; the bulk CSV header is `REPORT_QUERY_FIELDS[type]` plus `account-id` and `inquiry-id`, list members `|`-separated, rows numbered from 1; whole-file codes `report_bulk_file_unreadable`, `report_bulk_file_refused`, `report_runs_unavailable`, and `report_bulk_expired` after 24 hours. The lists export header is exactly `importColumns(kind)`, every cell personal data; `EXPORT_CSV_COLUMNS.lists` is to say so. No backfill of `transaction_filter_values` (no released tree wrote a filterable field without it).

EX2. **Settled by `integration-9b`:** `listTransactions` passes `blindIndexKey` to `transactionListFilters`, so a string field filter answers rather than 501 (`6a7e42dd`/`471a6831`); the 501s dropped (`80ed770f`). **Carried (S4B-14):** a problem code for a refused list kind (proposed `export_list_kind_refused`, 422; today a plain 422 at `/data/attributes/filter/filter[list-id]`); `@openverify/node` groups for reports bulk and saved views (not needed by 9C, whose console goes through the BFF).

### api-cases-reports

CR1. **Borrowed edits recorded** (C2, C8, B37, B41): the case template service and resources carry and serve layout, checklists, preset comments and `requires-fields`; the two route guards removed; the action service validates, stores and applies placement and `visible-when` through `isCaseActionVisible`; the K8-27 throws in `search.ts` and `attachments/scanning.ts`.

CR2. **Decisions:** `redacted-at` is served on `GET /verifications/:id` only once the inquiry was redacted, left out before (K8-19: an included verification reads as its own route serves it); a `module:<key>` placement names a module of the closed list, not checked against a template's layout (an action spans templates; the console shows it in the header where a template does not lay it out); `visible-when` statuses are not checked, its role ids must be the organisation's; an API key never checks a checklist item (403) and holds no role; a required field holds a value unless null, blank text or an empty list; `latest-run-notes` reads the newest run with `completed_at`; the print audit is `<object>.print-initiated` with empty metadata; a checklist change is audited as `case.updated {attributes: ['checklist']}`.

CR3. **Settled by `integration-9b`:** `GET /cases/:id/checklist` (`cases:read`, a route-schema change, `35305e4a`/`b7272eb8`); the graph-query job runs an expansion one hop from its stored key and lists a retired key's kind not indexed (`c4915baa`/`fa11ec88`); a rerun of an expansion runs from its stored start instead of binding parameters it never had (`8dee5c97`/`1a4c3da5`), the helpers moved to `services/graph/expansion-start.ts`; the 501s dropped (`80ed770f`).

## Workflow 9B Stage 4 amendments (integration-9b)

S4B-1. **PARALLEL RULES (orchestrator decision during Workflow 9B, 2026-11-06, on the owner's request to run in parallel what can safely run in parallel), recorded as binding for 9B and the default for 9C and 9D unless their own decisions change it:**
- `foundation-9b` ran as six calls in a chain, `-A`, `-U`, `-W`, `-X`, `-C` and `-F`, each starting when the previous returned; beside the chain, after `contracts-9b`, `analytics-logic`, `api-themes`, `api-templates-carry` and `api-flow-carry` started at once.
- Each family's builder started the moment its call returned with its checkpoint: `api-analytics` after (A) and `analytics-logic`; `api-access` after (U); `api-keys-webhooks` after (W); `api-exports` after (X); `api-cases-reports` after (C); `api-flow-expiry` after (F), (X) and `api-flow-carry`; `api-templates-finish` after (F), `api-templates-carry` and `api-themes`. A path handed to a follow-on (`api-flow-carry` to `api-flow-expiry`, `api-templates-carry` to `api-templates-finish`) changed hands only when the first returned; two agents never edited one file.
- Heavy calls (every API unit shard and its merge, every integration call, every forced lint or typecheck including the API or the dashboard, the whole dashboard, db or watchlist test script) went through `/tmp/openverify-heavy.sh`, a four-slot lock: slot 0 for the foundation calls and Stage 4 (`OPENVERIFY_HEAVY_RESERVED=1`), slots 1 to 3 shared. Exit 75 means no slot came free within 150 s and is not a test result.
- While other agents ran suites, the API unit suite ran as **N = 6** blob shards at `--maxWorkers=3`; iterating runs took no slot at `--maxWorkers=2`. Stage 1b ran **no whole gate**: each foundation call ran 9A's scoped gate on what it changed (orchestrator decision 3 before 9B).
- A gate failure confined to another agent's own paths was waited on for up to about 30 minutes, never edited, and then reported as failed by those tests and their owner.
- **Stage 4 is three agents:** `integration-9b` (the 9B specs, the only regeneration of the API artefacts after Stage 1, the missing hooks, CI, the amendments), `docs-9b` beside it (`docs/analytics/`, `docs/settings/`), then `validation-9b` alone (the "### Handover to 9C", the PROGRESS draft, the whole Validation list, and defects fixed test-first in any path, regenerating artefacts where a fix needs it).

S4B-2. **The API unit gate's shard count.** At the 9B close the API suite holds 686 files and 9,372 tests. On this machine two shards no longer fit the 600 s call (shard 1 of 2 reached the cap at a load of 7.8) nor do four (shard 2 of 4 reached it): `integration-9b` ran eight (167 to 296 s each at a load of 5 to 9) and merged them. For an agent running alone the default N is now **8** (rule 8's "2 for an agent running alone" is replaced); with other suites running, start at 12.

S4B-3. **CI.** Job times read with `gh run view 42070468737 --json jobs` (9A's push, 15 of 15 green): unit-api shard 1 28.1 of 40 min, shard 2 24.6, e2e 24.6 of 70, node 13.6 of 25, integration 13.2 of 30, unit-dashboard 12.4 of 20, unit-db 11.7 of 25, android-instrumented 9.7 of 60, lint 9.1 of 30, android 9.0 of 45, vision 5.9 of 30, format 1.6, infra 1.6, unit-api-coverage 0.9, secrets 0.4. With 9B's 9% more API tests shard 1's estimate (30.6) passes the last 25% of 40, so **unit-api runs as three shards** (`--shard=${{ matrix.shard }}/3`, timeout 40, heaps and concurrencies unchanged; `infra/tests/ci-workflow.test.sh` pins it). No other job is within 25% of its timeout. The SDK host-test floor rises to **1,366**.

S4B-4. **Firefox and WebKit for the theme CSP spec (C160.9, F19).** `apps/flow/e2e/playwright.config.ts` has `firefox` and `webkit` projects with `testMatch: 'theme-csp.spec.ts'` (no camera permission, no Chromium flags), always included in CI and elsewhere only where installed (this machine has Chromium only, so they are skipped locally). The e2e job restores `~/.cache/ms-playwright` keyed by `pnpm-lock.yaml` and installs `firefox webkit --with-deps` through the flow package before the tests. **Estimated cost**, not measured (no check branch or dispatch run is allowed): about 1 to 2 minutes of install from a cold cache, less from a warm one (the system packages still install), and under 1 minute for the spec's 5 tests in each engine. **The orchestrator reads the first CI run's step time**: past 3 minutes, Workflow 9C's integration moves the two engines under a `schedule:` trigger.

S4B-5. **The Validation list's order is unchanged**, with two notes: the API unit suite runs as eight blob shards plus a merge for an agent alone (S4B-2), and the mocked flow suite reports 163 tests locally (theme-csp's 5 in Chromium only; CI adds 10 in Firefox and WebKit).

### Handover to 9C

Written by `validation-9b` from the code at `f7494687` (the routes from their plugins, the settings from `packages/contracts/src/dashboard-api.ts`, the CI jobs from `.github/workflows/ci.yml`). **Every 9C agent re-verifies the lines it uses against the code before its first commit** (9C has no Stage 1a). Server routes are under `/api/v1`, dashboard routes under `/api/v1/dashboard`, flow routes under `/api/v1/flow`; a user session reaches the server routes as a principal whose permissions are those of its current environment (C167.2). Every route answers RFC 9457 problems; the statuses listed are the route's declared `problemResponses`, and no built 9A or 9B route declares 501 any more (S4B-7).

- **Analytics (C164.8):** `GET /analytics/{inquiries,funnel,verifications,checks,cases,signals}`, `analytics:read`, the `analytics` policy (60 a minute per principal) counted first; 400 (the query schema; `filter[platform]` or `filter[device-kind]` on the funnel, verifications or checks; `filter[tag]` serves four counts with no rates or durations), 401, 403, 429, and 500 only for core's `AnalyticsInputError`. Answers carry `time-zone`, `time-zones`, `threshold`, `settle-days`, `suppressed-cells` and `freshness { rolled-up-through, outcomes-as-of }` (both `null` before the first pass); a live day bucket has `live: true` with `created` and `started` only; `cases.totals.assignees` is `null` without `users:write` (organisation-wide), and API keys never get it. Definitions and suppression: `docs/analytics/README.md`; a chart, caption or CSV is drawn only from the suppressed answer.
- **Exports (C166):** `POST /exports` (no static scope; the route requires `EXPORT_PERMISSIONS[object-type]`, then the `exports` policy, 10 an hour per environment): 202; 400 (a filter the list does not take, at `/data/attributes/filter/<key>`), 401, 403 (`include-personal-data`, `filter[next-step-name]`, a transaction string field or `filter[identifier]` without `pii:view`), 404 (a saved view not the caller's nor shared, an unknown list), 422 (a saved view of another list; a list kind with no importer, with no code yet: S4B-14), 429. `GET /exports` (the caller's own, of the types it still holds `:export` for; `filter[status]`, `filter[object-type]`); `GET /exports/:id` (404 another principal's, 403 a lost type permission); `GET /exports/:id/download` (`text/csv`, `export-<type>-<id>.csv`; 404 before the file, 410 after expiry, 403 personal data without `pii:view`). Over 50,000 rows the export fails with `export_too_large`, never truncated. Audit `export.created`, `export.downloaded`.
- **Saved views (C165):** `GET`, `POST` (201), `PATCH /:id`, `DELETE /:id` (204) on `/dashboard/saved-views`; a session and the list's read permission (`SAVED_VIEW_LIST_PERMISSIONS`); 400 at `/data/attributes/filters/<key>`, 403 (another owner's view), 404, 422 `saved_view_pii_filter` (any string field of any transaction type version counts). `GET` answers own and shared views of readable lists, oldest first, unpaged.
- **Bulk reports (C166.5):** `POST /reports/bulk` (`reports:write`, the `report-bulk` policy, 5 an hour per environment; multipart `report-template-id` and part `file`, at most 2 MiB and 1,000 rows): 202; 400, 401, 403 (the `account-id` link column needs `accounts:read`, `inquiry-id` needs `inquiries:write`), 404, 413, 415, 422 (`report_template_not_published`; a refused CSV with `readCsv`'s codes at `/file`, never a cell), 429. `GET /reports/bulk/:id` (`reports:read`): every row `created` with its `report-id` or `failed` with its `error-code`, in row order; reports carry `origin: bulk`.
- **Delivery search (C169):** `POST /webhook-deliveries/search` (`webhooks:read`; at most depth 2 and 20 clauses): newest first; `links.next` is an id to post as `page.after`; 400, 401, 403.
- **Theme sets (C160):** `GET /theme-sets` (`theme-sets:read`; `filter[status]`, `page[after]`, `page[size]`); `POST` (`theme-sets:write`; 201 revision 1; 422 `theme_contrast_insufficient` with `pairs[]` at their pointers and unrounded ratios, or a contradiction with `errors[]`); `GET`/`PATCH`/`DELETE /theme-sets/:id` (PATCH 422 on an archived set; DELETE archives, 409 `theme_set_default`); `GET /theme-sets/:id/revisions` (newest first, `page[before]`); `POST /theme-sets/:id/restore` (`meta.revision`, 422 at `/meta/revision`); `POST /theme-sets/assets` (`theme-sets:write`, the `theme-assets` policy, 30 an hour per environment, counted first; 201; 400, 403 a font from an API key, 413, 415 `asset_type_refused`, 422 over 2,048 px or a re-encoded logo over 256 KiB, 429, 503 with `Retry-After: 10` when vision is busy or absent); **the asset content route** `GET /theme-sets/assets/:id/content` (`theme-sets:read`; the stored bytes, for the preview's `preview-assets` message); the flow's `GET /flow/theme-assets/:id` (session token, `private, max-age=3600`). The console's live contrast uses `checkPaletteContrast` from `@openverify/core` (never rounded).
- **Translations (C162):** `GET` and `POST /inquiry-templates/:id/translations` and `.../translations.csv` (`inquiry-templates:read` / `:write`; imports count the `translations-import` policy, 30 an hour per environment; POST 400, 401, 403, 404, 409, 422 with row and column pointers and never a value, 429, and for CSV 413 and 415; at most 2 MiB and 20,000 rows). Imports write the draft, never publish.
- **Template preview and simulate (C156):** `POST /inquiry-template-versions/:id/preview` and `.../simulate` (`inquiry-templates:read`; the `template-preview` policy, 120 a minute per principal, shared with `validate`; 400, 401, 403, 404, 422, 429). Preview's 422 pointers: `/meta/locale`, `/data/attributes/theme-set-id`, `/data/attributes/styles/...`, `/data/attributes/flow`; simulate's: `/meta/scope/<pointer>`, `/meta/preset-id`, `/meta`. Presets: `GET`, `POST`, `DELETE /:id` on `/dashboard/simulation-presets` (`inquiry-templates:read`): 22 built-ins first (`builtin:<type>-passed|failed`, scope `null`), then the operator's own; a preset's scope is `null` without `pii:view`, so the console must not offer to edit it then.
- **Notifications (C169.7):** `GET` and `PUT /dashboard/notification-preferences` (a session, no permission; every `USER_NOTIFICATION_KINDS` kind `{ console, email }`, default `{ true, false }`; PUT changes the kinds named; 400, 401). Only the webhook-failure sweep reads them today (S4B-14). `GET /dashboard/notifications` serves a `webhook-failure` row (`subject-type: webhook`, a `webhook` relationship) only with `webhooks:read` in the session's environment.
- **Keys (C169):** `POST /api-keys/:id/rotate` (`api-keys:write`; `meta { previous-expires-in-seconds 0-2592000, name? }`; 201 with `Location` and `no-store`, the token once; 400, 401, 403 naming the scopes the caller lacks, 404). Keys serve `note`, `attribute-blocklist` and `age-warning` (`api-key-age-warning-days`); a rotated key gets no `expires-at` of its own (docs gap 7, carried). Webhooks serve `name`, `description`, `failure-alert { enabled, threshold, window-minutes }` and `previous-secret-expires-at`.
- **Access (C167, C168):** `GET /dashboard/roles/grantable` (`roles:read`; scopes are every environment oldest first, then `null`, with reasons `not-held`, `organization-level`, `admin-organization-wide`; 401, 403); `PUT /dashboard/users/:id/roles` (`users:write`; 422 `role_scope_invalid`, 422 at `/assignments/i/...`, 403, 404, 409 for the last admin); `POST /dashboard/users/:id/reactivate` and `.../reset-mfa` (`users:write`; 403 `user_more_privileged`, 403 `step_up_required`, 409; reactivate also 502, 503); `POST /dashboard/invitations/:id/resend` (`users:write`, the `invitations-resend` policy, 5 an hour per invitation; 201; 403, 404, 409, 429, 502, 503). **Sessions** (a session, no permission): `GET /dashboard/sessions` (device summaries, never the user agent; the BFF must forward the browser's `User-Agent` on `POST /dashboard/sessions` or the summary is `null`), `POST /dashboard/sessions/:id/revoke` (204; 404), `POST /dashboard/sessions/revoke-others`, `POST /dashboard/sessions/current/step-up` (401 `mfa_code_invalid` must not sign the console out; 429) and `.../step-up/webauthn/options`; switching to an environment the user has no role in is 403 `no_environment_role`. `GET /dashboard/users` serves `mfa-enabled`, `mfa-factors` and `last-sign-in-at` to `users:write` only. The users table's reactivate button should move from `PATCH status active` to `POST /reactivate` (owner decision AC2 open).
- **The audit filters (C168.5, C168.6):** `GET /dashboard/audit-logs` and the server API's `GET /user-audit-logs` (`audit-logs:read`) take `filter[action-prefix]` (kebab case), `filter[from]`, `filter[to]` and `filter[ip]` (one exact IPv4 or IPv6 address; 403 without `pii:view`, 503 without a blind-index key; never an unfiltered list); 400, 401, 403, 503; the next link keeps every filter; a saved view cannot hold `filter[ip]`.
- **Cases and reports (C172, C173):** `GET /cases/:id/checklist` (`cases:read`; 400, 401, 403, 404) and `POST /cases/:id/checklist` (`cases:write`; `meta { checklist-key, item-key, checked, lock-version? }`; 200 the whole state; 403 for an API key, 404, 409 `case_version_conflict`, 410 `case_redacted`, 422 at `/meta/checklist-key` or `/meta/item-key`); a status change refused by the guard is 422 `case_checklist_incomplete` or `case_fields_required` at `/meta/status`; a hidden action is 403 `case_action_not_available` on `run-action` and absent from `available-actions`; case template versions serve `layout`, `checklists`, `preset-comments` and each status's `requires-fields`, and publish refuses with `case_template_invalid`. **Print audits:** `POST /dashboard/print-audits` (a session; the object `inq_`, `rep_` or `ver_` in the session's environment and its read permission, else 404 writing nothing; 201 `{ id: aud_, recorded-at }`; 400, 401). Reports serve `latest-run-notes` once a run has finished; `GET /verifications/:id?include=files` and `redacted-at` (present only once redacted). **The trust-store flags** (C173.4, B40): no `meta.flags`; `GET /dashboard/trust-anchors` (`settings:read`) already serves `settings { refresh-enabled, icao-crls-allowed }`, shown read-only.
- **The property-node expansion (owner decision 20 after Workflow 7B, B42):** `POST /graph-queries/:id/nodes/:nodeId/expand` (`graph:write`, plus `pii:view` for a person node; the `graph-queries` policy; body `meta.run-sync`; 201 a new graph query; 400, 401, 403, 404, 409, 410 `graph_query_redacted`, 422 for an account node, 429 including `graph_sync_limited`). The explorer merges the new result onto the node with the same `gn_` id; a key made under a retired blind-index key answers only the start node, its kind in `kinds-not-indexed`.
- **Settings 9A and 9B serve** (`GET`/`PATCH /dashboard/settings`, `settings:read` / `settings:write`, each change audited `settings.updated`; defaults in `DASHBOARD_SETTINGS_DEFAULTS`; `SETTING_LEVEL` in `@openverify/core` is `{ 'require-mfa': 'organization' }`, so only `require-mfa` is organisation-level, needs `settings:write` held organisation-wide by a user, and is audited in every environment (S4B-12); every other key is environment-level):

  | Setting | Workflow | Default | Bounds |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | `allowed-embed-origins` | 9A | `[]` (framing refused) | at most 50 (`MAX_ORIGIN_ENTRIES`), each at most 300 characters in the origin grammar; production refuses `http:` and `localhost`, and a wildcard needs `meta.acknowledge-wildcards` (422 `embed_origin_invalid`, `embed_wildcard_unacknowledged`) |
  | `allowed-redirect-origins` | 9A | `null` (the embed origins) | as above, nullable |
  | `default-theme-set-id` | 9A | `null` (the built-in theme) | an active `theset_` of the environment (`checkThemeSetChoice`, 422) |
  | `analytics-time-zone` | 9B | `UTC` | an IANA name the runtime and `pg_timezone_names` know; offsets refused (`analyticsTimeZoneSchema`; 422 at `/analytics-time-zone`) |
  | `analytics-suppression-threshold` | 9B | 11 | 11 to 100 |
  | `analytics-retention-months` | 9B | 13 | 13 to 60 |
  | `analytics-settle-days` | 9B | 30 | 1 to 90 (raising it reopens closed days: S4B-14) |
  | `analytics-assignee-retention-months` | 9B | 13 | 1 to 60 |
  | `api-key-age-warning-days` | 9B | 90 | 1 to 3,650 |
  | `mail-sender-name` | 9B | `null` (the install's From) | 1 to 100, no control or format character, `<`, `>`, `@`, `"` or `=?` |
  | `audit-log-retention-days` | 9B | `null` (kept) | 30 to 3,650, nullable |
  | `require-mfa` | 9B default | `true` for an organisation created since 9B (column default); `seed:dev` and test tenants pass `false` and say so | boolean, organisation-level |

  The template version's own 9A members (`embed-origins`, `redirect-uri`, `redirect-uri-schemes`, `redirect-include-status`, `help-url`, `privacy-url`, `theme-set-id`, `enabled-locales`, `default-locale`, `copy`) are checked at publish (C158.2, C158.4, C162).

The session carries it only for a theme set: the built-in tokens give no member (the flow keeps `packages/ui/src/styles.css`, whose hex values are `BUILTIN_PALETTES`). `mode` is `auto` exactly when the set's `use-dark-theme` is true. The preview document's `theme` has the same shape, with `theme-set-id` and `revision` `null` for the built-in theme or unsaved styles, beside `contrast { status, findings[] }`.
- **visx: not present.** No `@visx/*` package is in `pnpm-workspace.yaml`, `apps/dashboard/package.json` or `pnpm-lock.yaml`: B9 (visx 4.0.0's closure holds `robust-predicates` under The Unlicense, not on rule 4's list) is still the owner's. `dashboard-analytics` draws no chart until the owner decides and `dashboard-flow-builder` adds the dependency on request with its licence rows derived from the lockfile; until then the tables and captions stand alone. **Settled after this Handover:** the owner chose B9 (b); see "Owner decision after Workflow 9B Stage 1a (visx, 2026-11-06)" below. `d3-scale` and `d3-shape` are in the workspace before 9C starts.

## Owner decision after Workflow 9B Stage 1a (visx, 2026-11-06)

Asked in the orchestrator's session when `contracts-9b` returned partial on B9; the owner chose **(b)**. Binding on 9C and 9D. It settles B9, amends decision 42 and replaces C176's and `contracts-9b`'s visx rows.

1. **CLAUDE.md rule 4 stays as it is.** The Unlicense is not added, and no exception is granted for `robust-predicates`. No `@visx/*` package is installed (option (c), the override, is not taken either).
2. **Decision 42, amended.** The charts' primitives are `d3-scale` 4.0.2 and `d3-shape` 3.2.0 (ISC), client-only. Axes, groups and text are drawn as plain React SVG in `components/analytics/`. Everything else in decision 42 and in C164.9 is unchanged: every chart is a `figure` with a caption generated from the suppressed answer, an SVG `role="img"` with a name, an adjacent table that is the default for keyboard and screen-reader users, direct labels or patterns as well as colour, 3:1 marks, nothing on hover only, and the suppression marks in both views. Recharts, chart.js and ECharts stay not adopted.
3. **Applied in the gap after 9B, before 9C, by the orchestrator** (test-first; `docs/tdd/phase9-gap-charts.tdd.md`):
   - catalog pins `d3-scale` 4.0.2, `d3-shape` 3.2.0, `@types/d3-scale` 4.0.9 and `@types/d3-shape` 3.2.0; `apps/dashboard` takes the first two as dependencies and the types as dev dependencies;
   - the install added 12 packages, each released more than 14 days earlier: the four above, `d3-array` 3.2.4, `d3-format` 3.1.2, `d3-time` 3.1.0, `d3-time-format` 4.1.0, `d3-path` 3.1.0, `internmap` 2.0.3, `@types/d3-time` 3.0.4 and `@types/d3-path` 3.1.1. `d3-interpolate` 3.0.1 and `d3-color` 3.1.0 were already locked through `@xyflow/system`. The d3 patch releases of 2026-11-03 are younger than 14 days and are not resolved. No `minimumReleaseAgeExclude`, no `allowBuilds` entry, no `@visx/*`, `d3-delaunay` or `robust-predicates` in the lockfile;
   - `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md` §2.1.16 has a row for each of the 12, with version, release date and licence read from the installed package;
   - `apps/dashboard/src/chart-dependencies.test.ts` holds all of it.
4. **What changes for 9C.** `dashboard-analytics` draws the charts with these primitives and adds no dependency. Where earlier text says visx (decision 42, C164.9's "`@visx` primitives", `contracts-9b`'s Common bullet, C176's licence row, C170.3's "`@xyflow/react`, visx and Cytoscape render client-only", the Handover's "visx: not present"), read `d3-scale` and `d3-shape` with React SVG. The CSP rule is unchanged: the charts' SVG attributes are not inline styles.
5. **Records.** `docs/PLAN.md` §3's Charts row is corrected here. `docs/research/phase9-engineering.md` stays as the research record of its date.

## Orchestrator decisions before Workflow 9C (2026-11-07)

The owner: "Launch 9C right now and make decisions yourself". Taken from 9B as built at `f0e29cc0` and CI 42331362147 on `079cdba0`. Binding on 9C; they win over earlier text they contradict.

1. **TH1, confirmed.** The built-in light `focus` token stays `#000000` (C160.3, C160.4), so focus on `primary` keeps its 3:1 and the console's focus rings stay black. C160.4's focus-on-primary pair is kept.
2. **AC2: reactivation goes through `POST /dashboard/users/:id/reactivate` only.** `PATCH /dashboard/users/:id` with `status: active` on a deactivated user would give back every role and skip C168.1's new invitation, the superset rule and the step-up, so it is refused: 422 with the code `user_reactivation_required` at `/data/attributes/status`, naming the reactivate route. `PATCH` keeps deactivation and role changes. `dashboard-settings` moves the users table's reactivate button to `POST /reactivate` (C168.1's words: a new invitation, no role back), and `integration-9c` makes the API refusal test-first (route, problem code, OpenAPI snapshot, `@openverify/node` if it exposes the call), recording it as an amendment.
3. **The WebKit CSP failure of CI 42331362147** (the owner, 2026-11-07: "Hand it to 9C"). The mocked flow suite failed only on `[webkit] theme-csp.spec.ts` (5 tests, all three attempts; Chromium and Firefox passed). Each WebKit page reports one `style-src-attr` violation from `apps/flow/src/theme/apply.ts`' CSSOM writes to `<html>`'s inline style, though every token applies. C160.9's three-engine line is not met until it is fixed. `integration-9c` fixes it test-first, with the tokens applied through a same-origin stylesheet's CSSOM, never a style attribute and never an `unsafe-*` source, and owns `apps/flow/src/theme/` and `apps/flow/e2e/theme-csp.spec.ts` for it.
4. **The Firefox and WebKit step stays in the `e2e` job.** Its install took 29 s in CI 42331362147, under S4B-4's 3 minutes.
5. **CI at the 9B push** (minutes of the timeout): unit-api shards 19.9, 16.0 and 19.3 of 40, the merge 0.6 of 20; node 11.1 of 25; unit-db 12.6 of 25; **unit-dashboard 16.2 of 20, within 25% of its timeout** (Phase 8 F08), so `integration-9c` splits it or raises its timeout with a recorded reason before 9C's dashboard tests grow it; integration 12.4 of 30; lint 8.2 of 30; android 9.3 of 45; android-instrumented 8.9 of 60; vision 9.0 of 30; e2e failed at 7.5 of 70 (no full-stack time). The job names "shard N of 2" are corrected to "of 3".

The API unit suite runs as eight blob shards and a merge, as `validation-9b` ran it.

## Workflow 9C Stage 1 amendments

Recorded by `integration-9c` from the seven Stage 1 reports and their TDD records (`docs/tdd/phase9-dashboard-*.tdd.md`). Each item binds 9D. Where this Workflow assigned a path the plan left unnamed ("(script)" in the launch rules), the assignment is recorded here as the owner of record. Every request below was answered by its owner with a `[9c-done:...]` commit; none was open when Stage 1 closed at `70f97bd7`.

### dashboard-settings (C170, C167.2, C167.4, C168, C169, C174.6)

Checkpoints: `[9c-checkpoint:csp]` `3d2209ab`, `[9c-checkpoint:dashboard-settings:1]` `eff02991`. A stray `[9c-done:dashboard-settings]` on `bfe7abf0` answers no request.

- **DS1, one policy header.** On every route `proxy.ts` handles, its policy replaces `next.config.ts`' static `frame-ancestors 'none'`, and carries `frame-ancestors 'none'` itself. The static header stays for the routes the proxy skips, and stays enforced in report-only mode.
- **DS2, the build-output rule.** Next.js 16 prerenders its own `/_global-error`; it is the one static route allowed (`FRAMEWORK_STATIC_ROUTES`). Under `'strict-dynamic'` its scripts do not run.
- **DS3, mode and flow origin.** `OPENVERIFY_DASHBOARD_CSP_MODE` reports only for exactly `report-only`; anything else enforces. A missing or malformed `OPENVERIFY_FLOW_PUBLIC_URL` (not http(s), credentials, query or fragment) gives `frame-src 'none'`. The readers live in `apps/dashboard/src/lib/csp/policy.ts`.
- **DS4, `/csp-reports`.** 204, 415, 413 (16 KiB, declared or streamed), 400, 429 with `Retry-After: 60`; 60 reports a minute per client address from `requestClientAddress()`, each report of a batch counted; unknown addresses share one bucket; at most 10,000 addresses held; one JSON warn line per violation with the directive and the blocked scheme and host only.
- **DS5, the real-header test.** The page list is derived from the e2e build's route manifest. Scripts the runtime adds later carry no nonce, as `'strict-dynamic'` intends. The error page and the dynamic pages were left to `integration-9c` (see its section).
- **DS6, AC2 in the console.** The users table reactivates only through `POST /dashboard/users/:id/reactivate`; `setUserStatus` refuses `active` with words pointing to Reactivate (an existing test changed).
- **DS7, step-up.** The reset and reactivate dialogs carry an optional TOTP field (`StepUpCodeField`) verified through `POST /dashboard/sessions/current/step-up` first; a refused code is said at the field and nothing else is called. There is no WebAuthn step-up in these dialogs (carried to 9D).
- **DS8, drift.** No route reads a user's per-environment assignments or effective permissions. The assignments dialog therefore asks for every checked role's scope with no default and says the current environments are not shown, so a save never widens or narrows silently. Carried to 9D (see the Stage 2 section).
- **DS9, the role editor.** An environment-level permission is offered only where the editor may grant it in every environment; organisation-level ones are judged by the organisation-wide scope; refusals come from `GET /dashboard/roles/grantable`. Sensitive marks: `pii:view`, `settings:privacy`, `environments:production`, every `:redact` and `:export`, and `secrets:*`. The removal confirmation counts holders from at most five pages of 100 users, else says "every user with this role".
- **DS10, assignments.** `updateUserRoles` sends `PUT /dashboard/users/:id/roles` with `assignments` (existing tests changed from the PATCH form); a scope of several but not all environments is not offered.
- **DS11, the audit explorer.** The IP filter is posted to a server action (`searchAuditLogsByIp`, `audit-logs:read` and `pii:view`), never put in a URL; `filter[action-prefix]` is a GET filter checked by the contract's schema; the date filters keep their existing names.
- **DS12, the User-Agent.** Sign-in, MFA, WebAuthn and invitation-accept calls forward the browser's User-Agent (at most 1,024 characters) through a local `signInRequest` over `sendApiRequest`; `http.ts` and `client.ts` are unchanged.
- **DS13, keys.** Rotation choices for the old token: now, 1 hour, 1 day, 7 days, 30 days (`ROTATION_CHOICES`); an expired key is offered no rotation; note and blocklist only from the edit dialog; the age warning names the environment's limit without repeating its number.
- **DS14, webhooks.** A name is required on create only; an unnamed existing webhook keeps its URL as title; the failure alert's bounds are said at their fields; the previous-secret sentence shows only while that secret still works.
- **DS15, delivery search** at `/webhooks/deliveries`: rows joined by all-of or any-of, "is not" wraps a row, at most 20 rows (two levels), times read as UTC, the next page posted with `page.after`.
- **DS16, the settings form** carries every 9A and 9B setting where the server reports it; numbers checked against the contracts' bounds; `meta.acknowledge-wildcards` only with a change of an origin list and its production box ticked; `require-mfa` described as an organisation setting. `PLATFORM_NUMBER_FIELDS` lives in `settings-fields.ts` (a value from a client module is a client reference on the server).
- **DS17, notification preferences** at `/notifications` say that only the webhook-failure alert follows them today and that emails hold a link only.
- **DS18, borrowed edits.** `app/layout.tsx` awaits `connection()` (with `app/page.test.tsx`'s layout tests made async); `console-layout.tsx` mounts `CspModeBanner` (two tests); `ui-graph.spec.ts` and `ui-reviewer.spec.ts` drop `injectNonceCsp` and run under the real header. `injectNonceCsp` and `nonceCsp` stay exported, unused.
- **DS19, RSC payload tests** for `/users` and `/audit-logs` are full-stack (an auditor without `pii:view` sees no address, factor kind or last sign-in).
- **Script ownership:** `components/operators/`, `components/csp/`, `lib/csp/`, and the files of `components/integrations/` only its pages import (`delivery-search.tsx`, `delivery-search-query.ts`, `expiry-choices.ts`, `action-data.ts`, `api-key-actions.tsx`, `webhook-form.tsx`).
- **Not built (status partial):** the sign-in's `no_environment_role` in words, and the 9A and 9B settings in the read-only settings view. Both carried to 9D.

### dashboard-flow-builder (C155, C150.12, C155.9, the shared files)

Markers: `[9c-shared]` `68a65629`, `[9c-checkpoint:graph-editor]` `34036fe9`, `[9c-checkpoint:dashboard-flow-builder:1]` `23559acc`. Requests answered: template-forms R1 to R4 (`68a65629`, `d0a0ee6c`, `51755671`, `537c1783`), analytics, preview and themes-copy (`fb7d253d`, `a6fe4ffb`, `537c1783`).

- **FB-1, navigation.** `NavItem` gains `anyOf`, because `GET /exports` serves the caller's exports of the types it can still export: the Exports entry shows for any of `EXPORT_PERMISSIONS`' six scopes. New entries: `/theme-sets` (Verifications), the group "Insights" with `/analytics` and `/exports`, and `/exports/bulk-reports` (Reports). 31 links in 8 groups; only the most specific item is marked current (`currentNavHref`). The navigation tests changed with it.
- **FB-2, dependency.** `apps/dashboard` depends on the workspace package `@openverify/flow-spec` (lockfile importer link only), for the v2 rules in the browser and C161.5's locale review status.
- **FB-3, clients.** `@openverify/node` neither sends `If-Match` nor reads `ETag`, and has no validate, diff, restore, copy, used-by or promote call; the console calls them through `apiRequest`, whose `ApiCall` gains `headers` (the BFF's own headers always win) and `apiRequestWithHeaders`. Whether the node client gains them: decided in the Stage 2 section.
- **FB-4, the inquiry list.** `listInquiries` reads `GET /inquiries` (masked for the session principal, as the dashboard route was), because `GET /dashboard/inquiries` ignored C165.3's filters; five tests changed their mocked path, one of them a borrowed edit of `app/reports/rsc-payload.test.tsx`. The dashboard route is fixed in Stage 2 (DA1).
- **FB-5, the editor move.** C155.1's refactor moved `workflow-canvas.css`, `history.ts` and `edit-menu.tsx` (with their tests) into `components/graph-editor/`, and the APG tree into `graph-editor/outline-tree.tsx`; `DefinitionOutline` wraps it. The old paths are gone.
- **FB-6, real header.** The two Phase 8 tests that injected their own CSP (`ui-workflow-editor.spec.ts`, `ui-workflows.spec.ts`) read the console's real header instead: an injected policy leaves Next.js pages unhydrated once the console sends its nonce.
- **FB-7.** The template page's viewer test expects one button, "Compare": comparing versions only reads.
- **FB-8, where things live.** The builder is at `/templates/[id]/flow` (tab "Flow") and edits the draft; notification preferences are a header link ("Notification settings", `NOTIFICATION_PREFERENCES_PATH`), since the route needs a session only; template tabs show with `inquiry-templates:read` and each page checks its own permissions.
- **FB-9, the builder's rules.** Step ids `s_` plus 8 base32 characters (edges `e_`), from `crypto.getRandomValues`; every v2 type in the palette, a second step of one verification type refused in words; an unwired `exhausted` port is not a gap; warnings never count as a step's errors; autosave 1,500 ms after the last edit (provisional: well under `template-preview`'s 120 a minute); publish saves first, then validates in publish mode, each acknowledgement a checked box beside its reason; the 412 dialog compares the server's latest draft with the last saved; route on-null choices are skip-route, route:<other>, else and review (`flow-route` position) with no default; paths with `availableInFlow: false` are never offered.
- **FB-10, shared types.** `ScopePath` is the graph editor's, with optional `tier` and `stage`; `pathMarks` words a flow fact's source, tier and beta stage; workflow paths read as before.
- **Slots (rule 19).** `check-panels/step-panel.tsx` (`StepPanel`, props `flow`, `step`, `verificationConfig`, `editable`, `onVerificationConfigChange`) and `workflows/overlay/run-overlay.tsx` (`RunOverlay`, props `definition`, `run`) were created at the graph-editor checkpoint and filled by their owners.
- **Not built (status partial):** C155.4 modules; C155.5's screen editor (a screen's components are edited through the generated step form and the step JSON); C155.7's copy and promote dialogs (clients built) and the canvas diff badges (`FLOW_ADAPTER.diffMarksOf` built, not passed); C150.9 to C150.11's builder warnings and `waitFor` editing outside the JSON; Phase 8's R005 loop and module authoring in the workflow editor. Carried to 9D's `fix-console` (Stage 2 section).

Checkpoint `3aabc27a`; requests `e68cfa48` (R1 to R3) and `f92c8a6a` (R4), all answered.

- **1, drift.** 7C's "list picker in VersionEditor" never existed (list-config was JSON). The generated form's `list-config.screen` uses a new widget, `components/schema-form/list-picker.tsx` (all, none, or chosen active lists); saved list problems use `SIGNAL_PROBLEM_WORDS`, imported.
