### dashboard-preview (C156.2, C156.4, C156.6, C156.7)

Checkpoint `736df957`; requests `736df957` (tab and link) and `0712fdfb` (the overridden canvas badge), answered by `68a65629`, `fb7d253d` and `537c1783` (`STATUS_BADGES.overridden`).

- **P1, origins.** The frame's src comes from `OPENVERIFY_FLOW_PUBLIC_URL` through `flowPublicOrigin` (`lib/csp/policy.ts`), so the frame and the console's `frame-src` name one origin. The same-origin refusal compares it with the browser's `window.location.origin`, so the iframe renders client-only; a missing or non-http(s) URL is said in an alert.
- **P2, theme assets** go through a console route, `GET /templates/:id/preview/assets/:assetId`: PNG, JPEG or WOFF2 of at most 1 MiB, `private, no-store`, `nosniff`, no filename; posted as transferred buffers in one `preview-assets` message of at most 1 MiB. Without `theme-sets:read` the page says the logo or font could not be read.
- **P3, cadence.** The preview resolves in the browser through a server action, at most once every 600 ms (`PREVIEW_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`, provisional: 100 a minute under the shared 120); a 429 is said and never retried automatically.
- **P4, pointers.** `/meta/locale` shows beside the language menu; every other pointer in a labelled problems list. The preview page has no theme or styles form; `PreviewWorkbench` takes `unsaved` members for an editor that embeds it.
- **P5, version.** `?version=itmplv_...` must belong to the template (else 404); without it, the newest draft of the first 25 versions, else the newest version.
- **P6, the simulated scope form** offers each verification step's status (or unknown), check overrides and extracted values, every flow-provisional signal, the lists the routes read, device kind and platform, each run_workflow step's result, tags and fields; never the scope's account, session or inquiry status (a preset holding them still simulates by id). Unset members are not sent.
- **P7, presets.** Built-in presets and presets served with a `null` scope are simulated by id and never shown; the operator's own load into the form and simulate as a scope; deleting one needs a confirmation; the RSC payload carries each scope as served (an RSC test).
- **P8, the overlay.** `RunOverlay` renders the run's path list and Phase 8's `RunPathCanvas`, with the `overridden` status ("✎ Overridden"); with no run it says so.
- **P9, the outcome control** is offered only for steps that create a verification.
- **P10, the dashboard unit gate** runs as blob shards and a merge (settled in CI by Stage 2).
- **Script ownership:** `lib/api/template-preview.ts` (preview, simulate and theme asset content).

### dashboard-themes-copy (C160, C162, C161.5)

Checkpoint `1c4a6ac2`; request `ef2bb979`, answered (`a6fe4ffb`, `68a65629`, `d0a0ee6c`). `BuilderThemePanel` and `BuilderCopyPanel` are mounted in the builder.

- **TS-1.** No route lists theme assets; the editor knows an asset from its upload answer or the styles naming it, and says so. A list route is optional (9D or later).
- **TS-2, fallback order.** The console states C162.5's order (the person's language's custom text, its built-in text, the default locale's custom text, its built-in text, never a raw key), which the flow follows; docs-9c corrects `docs/i18n/README.md`.
- **TS-3.** The shared problem reader keeps no `pairs[]` or per-error code, so `theme_contrast_insufficient`'s pairs and the translation refusals are read from `OpenVerifyApiError` in this area's own lib files.
- **TS-4.** The copy rules exist only in the API; the console shows each key's rules from `COPY_KEYS` and `COMPONENT_COPY_*` and says each refusal code in words, never re-implementing them.
- **TS-5.** The editor offers the keys the copy does not hold yet (every `COPY_KEYS` key and each v2 screen's component keys); an import never deletes, so an override is removed in the builder.
- **TS-6.** CSV goes through a console route (`GET` and `POST /templates/:id/translations/csv`), not a server action (1 MB body limit against the contract's 2 MiB); origin checked, over 2 MiB refused, answers `{imported}` or row and column problems, never a value.
- **TS-7.** The logo shows through `GET /theme-sets/assets/:id/content`, reusing `template-preview.ts`.
- **TS-8.** Unsaved styles show on real screens through `FlowPreviewFrame` (import only), with `inquiry-templates:read`, the chosen template's current version, the `en` locale and the first 25 templates (provisional); SVG swatches otherwise.
- **TS-9.** The default set is changed from the set page through `PATCH /dashboard/settings` (`settings:write`), on active sets only.
- **TS-10.** A decorative logo shows the organisation's display name only with `settings:read`; the session theme carries no name (TH5, carried).
- **TS-11.** Duplicate makes "<name> (copy)" (cut to 100 characters) from the current styles, revisions from 1.
- **TS-12.** Upload refusals are said from the Handover's statuses (403, 413, 415 naming SVG, 422, 429, 503); 409 `theme_set_default` on archive; 422 on an archived set's PATCH and on restoring a missing revision.
- **TS-13, process.** Short `flock -w 60` waits starved behind longer waiters; future Workflows give builders one lock-wait value.
- **TS-14.** The theme and translations tabs show the draft, else the current version read-only, saving through `updateTemplateVersion` (If-Match, allow-invalid).
- **TS-15.** A JSON import reports the cells sent; a CSV import the rows the draft holds; neither publishes.

### dashboard-analytics (C164.9, C165, C166)

Checkpoint `c807f38d`; request `2992188f`, answered by `fb7d253d`.

- **DA1, an API defect.** `GET /dashboard/inquiries` accepted C165.3's filters but never applied them, answering the unfiltered list. Fixed in Stage 2 (b639ed20); the console reads `GET /inquiries` (FB-4).
- **DA2, the saved-views slot** is one line per list page, `<SavedViewsSlot list="..." />` (`list="webhook-deliveries"` on `app/webhooks/[id]/page.tsx`): a client component reading the page's URL and fetching views from the console route `GET /exports/saved-views?list=`, which sends the list name only. A server-rendered slot would have broken the list pages' own tests.
- **DA3, URL to filters** per list (`components/saved-views/saved-view-lists.ts`): an unknown page parameter is named as not kept; a view the page cannot show is not applied, so a list is never wider than its view. Not kept: reports' `result`, audit logs' `from` and `to`, events' `simulated`. A transaction field filter is `field.<key>` in the URL.
- **DA4.** Every transaction field filter counts as personal data in a saved view (fail closed, as B20 allows); passing the type's string keys is later work.
- **DA5, the analytics query.** Each view offers only the filters its answer takes; platform or device filters on funnel, verifications and checks are refused in words before asking. Without a window, the last 30 local days in the reporting zone (at most two reads when the zone's day differs from UTC's).
- **DA6, the charts** (owner decision B9 (b)): `LineChart` and `BarChart` on d3-scale and d3-shape, client-only through `useSyncExternalStore`; small counts drawn as a hatched band to threshold-1, complementary cells as a short hatched mark; series solid against striped or dashed; captions state this window only, never a comparison.
- **DA7, words.** An undefined rate reads "not defined"; the entry step's missing step-share is explained; a duration reads "between N and M <unit> (approximate)".
- **DA8, exports.** Per type: verifications need a type, lists a list id, inquiries a status, every type but lists a UTC created range (end exclusive). A saved view is exported by id only, through `/exports?view=svw_&type=`. The download route answers 403 before asking a reader with no `:export` scope.
- **DA9, bulk upload** streams through `POST /exports/bulk-reports/upload` (origin, multipart and 2 MiB plus 64 KiB framing checked, session checked, its own Idempotency-Key), answering our words only; inside the proxy's 10 MB buffer, so `proxy.ts`' matcher is unchanged.
- **DA10.** `InquiryFilters` takes `canFilterByStep` and `TransactionFilters` `canFilterStrings`, both `pii:view`; a field filter needs a chosen type, at most ten, only filterable top-level fields.
- **DA11, borrowed edits** (`07ade37c`): the slot line on the nine list pages, and the two `pii:view` props at their call sites; no existing test changed.
- **DA12, copy.** `ANALYTICS_COPY` uses `{name}` placeholders filled by `fillCopy`; metric definitions are shown in `ANALYTICS_METRICS`' own words.
- **DA13, process.** RED `7625e718` did not typecheck (a missing import, added by GREEN `97375ee7`).
- **Script ownership:** `app/exports/bulk-reports/` with its upload route; `components/inquiry-filters.tsx`, `lib/inquiry-query.ts`, `components/transactions/transaction-filters.tsx` and `transaction-query.ts`.

### dashboard-cases-reports (C172, C173, owner decision 20 after 7B)

Checkpoint `8e68584d`. No request made or received.

- **Drift.** `GET /verifications/:id` takes one include, and `@openverify/node`'s `verifications.retrieve` types `selfies` only; the console reads `include=files` through `apiRequest` (`lib/api/verification-files.ts`).
- **Layout.** A module shows only where the version's layout places it; a null layout keeps Phase 8's arrangement. Case actions are read whenever the reader holds the `case-actions` module's permissions; header actions stay in "Case actions", which shows where the layout places that module, else at the top.
- **Placements (C172.5).** An action placed in a module the page does not show, or in the status switcher for a reader without one, shows with the header's actions.
- **Saves.** Layout, checklists and preset comments are each saved alone through new actions in `components/cases/layout/layout-actions.ts`; Phase 8's draft form and `app/case-templates/actions.ts` are unchanged; publish refusals on `/layout/...` show the API's words without a position.
- **Reports (C173.1).** The inquiry's reports section reads 25 from the masked list and the newest 10 completion reports in full, judging the rest by `latest-run-notes`, and says so.
- **K15, `FormDialogButton` options:** no record says what Phase 6 proposed; dropped as superseded (Stage 2 decision).
- **C173.4.** The trust-store page already showed both flags read-only since Phase 5; only the e2e check was added.
- **Print views (C173.3).** Each of the inquiry, report and verification pages imports its own `print.css` (every rule in `@media print`, scoped by `body:has([data-<kind>-print])`); the footer gives environment, object, operator and time, "Contains personal data." only with `pii:view`, and that only the button's prints are recorded. `recordObjectPrint` passes no static permission; the API checks the object's.
- **Ownership by importer:** `components/lifecycle/inquiry-view.tsx`, `components/verification-view.tsx` and `components/reports/*` (script: with `components/trust-store/`, `components/print/`).
- **Borrowed edits:** `components/graph/graph-explorer.tsx` and `graph-table.tsx` (the expansion, the link's visible name, 24 px targets).
- **Expansion.** A shared value expands through `graphQueries.expandNode(queryId, nodeId, { runSync: true })` from a server action, never on render or hover; person-identifying kinds only with `pii:view`; no Idempotency-Key.
- **K19 not built** (the OFAC Consolidated fixture entry is a `packages/watchlist` change); **comparison-source words not built** (shared evidence modules). Both carried (Stage 2 section).
- **RSC practice.** A bare `RSC: 1` request answers 307 on the stack; read the payload inlined in a normal navigation.

## Workflow 9C Stage 2 amendments (integration-9c)

Evidence: `docs/tdd/phase9-integration-9c.tdd.md`. Every gate below exited 0, read from its own exit code.

- **S9C-1, the WebKit CSP failure (orchestrator decision 3 before 9C).** Cause: `apps/flow/src/theme/apply.ts` wrote the tokens, the radius scale and `color-scheme` to `<html>`'s inline style through the CSSOM, which WebKit checks against `style-src-attr`. Fix: one rule, `:root:root:root` (specificity (0,3,0), unlayered), inserted at the end of the page's last enabled same-origin stylesheet (an adopted constructed sheet when the page has none; nothing applied when neither exists), every checked value set with `rule.style.setProperty`. It outranks `:root[data-openverify-variant='dark']` and Tailwind's layered tokens, as the inline style did. No `unsafe-*` source and no style attribute. `theme-csp.spec.ts` now asserts `<html>` has no style attribute and the token comes from a stylesheet rule; `e2e/tests/theme-sets.spec.ts` reads the token as computed. Engines run: Chromium and Firefox locally (10 passed); WebKit is installed here but cannot start (missing host libraries), so it is proven in CI only.
- **S9C-2, local engines.** `apps/flow/e2e/playwright.config.ts` keeps Firefox or WebKit locally only when it is installed and starts (`launches`, answer passed to workers in `OPENVERIFY_FLOW_E2E_ENGINES`); CI always runs both. Without this, a downloaded WebKit failed every local mocked suite run. The mocked suite is now 168 tests here (163 Chromium, 5 Firefox).
- **S9C-3, AC2.** `PATCH /dashboard/users/:id` with `status: active` on a deactivated user answers 422 `user_reactivation_required` at `/data/attributes/status`, its detail naming `POST /dashboard/users/:id/reactivate`, before any change (role ids sent with it are not applied). Deactivation and role changes are unchanged. The code joins `ACCESS_PROBLEM_STATUSES` with its golden problem `contracts/fixtures/problems/access/user-reactivation-required.json` (no Android fixture-list change: that folder is not in it). `@openverify/node` exposes no dashboard user call, so it is unchanged. Three existing route tests changed from a 200 reactivation to the refusal. The console reactivates only through `POST /reactivate` (DS6); no e2e spec uses the PATCH form.
- **S9C-4, DA1 fixed.** `GET /dashboard/inquiries` applies `inquiryListFilters` with the operator's PII visibility (`filter[next-step-name]` without `pii:view` is 403) and repeats the added filters in `links.next`. The console keeps reading `GET /inquiries` (FB-4); both answers are masked alike.
- **S9C-5, OpenAPI** regenerated by `openapi:write` twice with one sha256 (`0ccb2cf21ab1e2a6c588985a8e25e6c607667bf401c6c58b63dc55edbded0021`); only the two route descriptions changed.
- **S9C-6, the console sweep (C170.3, C171.2).** `e2e/tests/a11y-console.spec.ts` runs `expectAccessible` once per console page: every page route of the e2e build's route manifest (`e2e/src/console-routes.ts`, never a list by hand), one test each, 97 at this close. A route with a dynamic segment opens the first URL the console links to: the server HTML of every static page is read with an admin's session, up to eight pages of each dynamic route are read for nested links, and `makeSweepObjects` makes through the server API the objects no spec makes (an SLA policy, a case queue with an assignment policy, a bulk case action, whose page is reached without a link). A route no link reaches fails its test. The signed-out pages open without a session, `/login/mfa` as the seeded MFA admin between password and code. `ui-csp.spec.ts` gains a test over every dynamic page (real policy byte for byte, a fresh nonce, every server script carrying it, no violation). Both specs form the Playwright project `console-sweep`, which depends on `chromium`, so `pnpm e2e` runs it last; locally it is its own call with `--no-deps` after the shards. `e2e/src/playwright-config.test.ts` pins it.
- **S9C-7, the error page** stays out of `ui-csp.spec.ts`: no reliable full-stack trigger (malformed cursors and ids on the list and detail pages, an unknown inquiry, a malformed analytics window all answer with their own words or the 404 page). Carried to 9D.
- **S9C-8, defects the gate found, fixed test-first.** (a) `publishFlowDraft` revalidated `/templates`; a revalidating server action sends the current route again, and the flow page without a draft has no builder, so "Published" was unmounted before it could be read (`ui-flow-builder.spec.ts`, intermittently): the action revalidates nothing, since every console page renders per request. (b) Cytoscape.js inserts `<style id="__________cytoscape_stylesheet">` unless an element of that id exists, a `style-src-elem` violation under the console's real CSP (`ui-graph.spec.ts`, first run under the real header since DS18): the canvas puts an empty `<meta>` of that id in the head before the library starts and positions its container with `relative`. (c) The template version page's JSON blocks were scrollable without keyboard access (axe `scrollable-region-focusable`): each is a labelled region with `tabIndex=0` and a visible focus ring. (d) `ui-integrations.spec.ts` created an unnamed webhook, refused since C169.5: it names it.
- **S9C-9, e2e shards.** The full-stack suite is four shards of the `chromium` project plus the console sweep: 175 tests in shards of 47, 42, 45 and 41 (228, 265, 266 and 348 s), the sweep 102 tests in 409 s, the stack start 87 s, at a load of 1.0 to 3.7; the mocked suite 168 tests in 60 s. The 9C UI specs measured in the gate: ui-settings 25.8 s, ui-template-forms 22.1 s, ui-cases-reports 20.9 s, ui-analytics 16.6 s, ui-flow-builder 15.5 s, ui-themes-copy 14.5 s, ui-preview 14.2 s. No OTP send was added. `e2e/README.md` holds the commands; rule 7 (c)'s gate becomes the four `--project=chromium --shard=i/4` calls and one `--project=console-sweep --no-deps` call.
- **S9C-10, CI.** Times read from CI 42331362147 (9B's push), in minutes of the timeout: unit-api shards 20.0, 16.0 and 19.4 of 40, merge 0.7 of 20; node 11.1 of 25; unit-db 12.6 of 25; unit-dashboard 16.2 of 20; integration 12.5 of 30; lint 8.3 of 30; android 9.3 of 45; android-instrumented 8.9 of 60; vision 9.0 of 30; format 1.9; infra 1.7; secrets 0.3; e2e failed at 7.5 of 70 (the mocked suite's WebKit, S9C-1). Changes, test-first through `infra/tests/ci-workflow.test.sh`: unit-dashboard runs as two blob shards (`--shard=N/2`, each with its own thresholds off) and a new `unit-dashboard-coverage` job merges them and applies the 80% gate, as unit-api does (16.2 of 20 was inside the last 25%; 9C adds about 930 tests to 6,538; locally 166 and 184 s per shard at a load of 1 to 7); the unit-api job name says "of 3". Lint keeps its job, heap and concurrency (8.3 of 30; this Workflow's forced dashboard lint took about 450 s locally). The e2e job keeps 70 minutes: 9A's push took 24.6 minutes for a local total of about 17, so this Workflow's local 27.7 (mocked 1, stack 1.5, shards 18.5, sweep 6.8) scales to about 40 minutes, outside the last 25%. The Firefox and WebKit install (29 s at 9B's push) stays in the e2e job (orchestrator decision 4).
- **S9C-11, decisions on Stage 1 items.** (a) `@openverify/node` gains no ETag, If-Match, validate, diff, restore, copy, used-by, promote or `include: 'files'` in 9C: the console's BFF calls through `apiRequest` are tested, and widening the published client is an SDK change left to Phase 10's SDK work (FB-3; cases-reports' drift). (b) K15's `FormDialogButton` options are dropped as superseded: no record names them, and Phase 6's match dialogs compose `Dialog`. (c) `TYPE_OPTIONS` stays private in contracts; the derived reading is tested against the contract.
- **S9C-12, carried to Workflow 9D (`fix-console` unless named).** The sign-in's and the environment switch's 403 `no_environment_role` in words (shared login files); `GET /dashboard/users/:id/roles` and a read of effective permissions per environment, then preselected scopes in the assignments dialog (DS8; `fix-api` then `fix-console`); the 9A and 9B settings in the read-only settings view; a WebAuthn step-up in the reset and reactivate dialogs; C155.4 modules, C155.5's screen editor, C155.7's copy and promote dialogs and canvas diff badges, C150.9 to C150.11's builder warnings and `waitFor` editing, and Phase 8's R005 loop and module authoring; comparison-source words in the database and government-ID evidence; K19's synthetic OFAC Consolidated entry through `packages/watchlist`'s fixture generator with rule 16's benchmark checks (`fix-api`), then the "Other lists" step; a layout position in `app/case-templates/actions.ts`' publish problem places; the webhooks list showing names; a theme asset list route (optional); an error-page trigger for the real-header test; the unused `injectNonceCsp` helpers.
- **S9C-13, Validation.** The list is unchanged except the full-stack lines: four `--project=chromium --shard=i/4` calls and one `--project=console-sweep --no-deps` call on the same stack, the sweep last.
- **S9C-14, `validation-9c`'s defect fixes** (evidence: `docs/tdd/phase9-validation-9c.tdd.md`).
  - **Docs** (`639b6d58`; docs-9c's followups, checked against the code): `docs/i18n/README.md` states C162.5's fallback order as `copyText` runs it (the person's language's custom text, its built-in text, the default locale's custom text, its built-in text; no English step unless English is the default; never a raw key); `docs/flows/preview-and-simulate.md` documents the resolve route, simulate (each step kind as the walk treats it, the answer, the two known differences from a live inquiry) and the presets, closing S4B-14's "9C's docs or 9D" item; `docs/flows/README.md` and `engine.md` replace "not built in 9A" with what 9B built and document the session's `links`, `theme` (inquiry's set, else the version's, else the environment default; absent for the built-in theme) and the handed-off phone's consent step; `docs/flows/steps.md` documents prefill by input name (`-` read as `_`); `docs/themes/README.md` describes S9C-1's stylesheet rule instead of the root's inline style; `docs/analytics/README.md` says only the analytics CSV download is missing; `apps/dashboard/next.config.ts`' comment points at `proxy.ts` and DS1. No test reads these.
  - **The analytics benchmark's refresh step** (`8059a4b2` refactor, `9994adad` RED, `95059082` GREEN). The `inquiries` trigger `openverify_set_updated_at` keeps a value an update sets only when it differs from the stored one, so every `--measure` after the first on one seed wrote the wall clock instead of `NOW + 60 s` and the "5-minute refresh" timed an empty pass (`days: 0`, 15 ms). `touchRefreshWindow` (exported from `apps/api/scripts/analytics-benchmark.ts` with `ANALYTICS_BENCHMARK_TOUCHED_AT`) moves the 48 hours to `2026-10-28` first, then to the touched time; `apps/api/src/analytics-benchmark-refresh.test.ts` (PGlite) touches twice. The 9B figures came from first runs and stand. The rule for later Workflows: a benchmark step that writes a fixed time through a table with this trigger writes a different value first.
- **S9C-15, the Validation list as run on the final tree** (`639b6d58`; the API re-gated on `95059082` after S9C-14's code commits), every step in the foreground with its exit code read from the command, `validation-9c` alone. The order is S4B-16's with S9C-13's full-stack lines. Splits: the forced typecheck in one call (122 s); the forced lint as CI runs it, `--concurrency=1` under a 4 GB heap, in three calls (the API 179 s; the dashboard and contracts 196 s; the other 14 packages 293 s); the unit suites outside the API in three calls (watchlist, e2e and db 341 s; the dashboard alone 349 s at a load of 1.5 to 7.8, which fits one call only on a quiet machine; the other 12 packages 145 s); the API as eight blob shards (122 to 155 s, load 1.4 to 11) plus a merge (31 to 32 s); the full stack as four chromium shards (233, 264, 247 and 364 s) and the console sweep (407 s) on one stack started in 85 s, load 1.6 to 4.5. Every step exited 0; the numbers are in the Handover below.
- **S9C-16, carried by `validation-9c`** (beside S9C-12; each with its 9D owner in the Handover): `consolePublicOrigin` and `DASHBOARD_PUBLIC_URL_ENV` in `apps/dashboard/src/lib/csp/policy.ts` are exported and read by no production code (the preview's same-origin refusal reads `window.location.origin`, P1); `/exports/bulk-reports` offers no upload to a `reports:write` holder without `report-templates:read`, though the API needs only `reports:write`; `components/analytics/bulk-report-job.tsx` shows a failed row's `error-code` raw where the rest of the console words its codes; no analytics view offers its answer as a CSV, though C164's "every view and download" line names downloads; the simulate panel labels a path simulated but does not list simulate's two known differences (`docs/flows/preview-and-simulate.md`); `docs/settings/README.md` does not link the delivery search section. Three idle `opencode` processes (started 5 to 6 days before) ran beside the Validation and touched nothing (as S4B-18).

### Handover to 9D

Written by `validation-9c` from the code at `95059082` (the Validation run in `docs/tdd/phase9-validation-9c.tdd.md`). **Every 9D agent re-verifies the lines it uses against the code before its first commit**; `a11y-audit` reads this first.

- **Accessibility tooling (C171.2).**
  - `expectAccessible(page, { include?, exclude?, where? })` lives twice, with one rule set: `apps/flow/e2e/support/accessible.ts` (the mocked flow suite) and `e2e/tests/support/accessible.ts` (the full stack). Both take their tags from `AXE_WCAG_TAGS` in `packages/config/src/axe.ts` (`wcag2a`, `wcag2aa`, `wcag21a`, `wcag21aa`, `wcag22aa`), fail on any violation, and attach axe's "incomplete" results for the manual pass. `@axe-core/playwright` and `axe-core` 4.14.0 are dev dependencies only (MPL-2.0, `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md` §5).
  - **Exceptions:** only `e2e/a11y-exceptions.json` (rule, optional target and page, reason with its tracking item, expiry day; an expired one fails every run). It is **empty** at the 9C close.
  - **C171.2's CI matrix** runs in the mocked flow suite (`apps/flow/e2e`, no stack), in CI's `e2e` job before the full stack: `step-matrix.spec.ts` (every step type, `en` at 320 px light, `ar` at 320 px dark, one run at 768 px per type), `screens.spec.ts` (the screen step's states), `server-position.spec.ts` (waiting, halted and end screens), `pseudo-locale.spec.ts` (`en-XA` and `ar-XB` at 320 px with the text-spacing override), `rtl.spec.ts`, `embed.spec.ts` (the embedded runs: 10 step types; `selfie`, `completion`, `failure`, `screen`, `device_handoff` and `nfc` not run embedded, 9A amendment 70), `accessible.spec.ts` and `selfie-extension.spec.ts`. The full cross product (C171.2: every state in the five locales, both pseudo-locales, light and dark, hosted and embedded, three widths) has **never run**: it is `a11y-audit`'s, once, in shards that each fit a 10-minute call, with the timing recorded.
  - **The console axe spec:** `e2e/tests/a11y-console.spec.ts`, one test per page route of the e2e build's route manifest (`e2e/src/console-routes.ts`; 97 pages at the close), each dynamic route opened at the first URL the console's own links reach (`e2e/tests/support/console-pages.ts`: `discoverConsolePages`, fan-out 8 pages a route; `makeSweepObjects` makes the SLA policy, assignment policy and bulk case action no spec makes). It is the Playwright project `console-sweep` with `ui-csp.spec.ts`, depending on `chromium`, so `pnpm e2e` runs it last; locally it is its own call with `--no-deps` after the four chromium shards, on the same used stack (run on a fresh stack before the shards, it fails with "no console link reaches" on routes whose objects the shards make). 102 tests in 407 s at a load of 2.0 to 2.6.
  - The 9C UI specs also call `expectAccessible` at each state of their pages and `toMatchAriaSnapshot` on their main pages (`ui-flow-builder`, `ui-preview`, `ui-template-forms`, `ui-themes-copy`).
- **The real-header CSP spec (C170.3):** `e2e/tests/ui-csp.spec.ts`, 5 tests: the policy byte for byte against `expectedConsoleCsp`, a fresh nonce per response, every server script carrying it, no `securitypolicyviolation`, over every static page of the route manifest and (S9C-6) every dynamic page; helpers in `e2e/tests/support/ui-settings.ts`. **The error page is not covered** (no reliable full-stack trigger, S9C-7). Every 9C UI spec also fails on any violation on the pages it opens (`recordPolicyViolations`). The policy builder is `consoleCsp` in `apps/dashboard/src/lib/csp/policy.ts`; `proxy.ts` sends it; `OPENVERIFY_DASHBOARD_CSP_MODE=report-only` reports instead.

## Orchestrator decisions before Workflow 9D (2026-11-07)

The owner: "Launch 9C right now and make decisions yourself" and, for 9D, "Make the open decisions yourself and record them in the plan" (2026-11-07); the orchestrator decides the open items in the owner's place and records them here. Taken from 9C as built (`017d8e36`, the "### Handover to 9D" above) and CI 42382549247 on `658c8678`. Binding on 9D; last and binding over every earlier section it contradicts.

1. **Eleven agents (P-A, P-B).** Decision 65's nine agents become eleven:
   - **`fix-builder`**, a third Stage 4a fixer beside `fix-flow` and `fix-console`. It owns the flow builder and the workflow editor: `apps/dashboard/src/components/flow-builder/`, `components/graph-editor/`, `components/workflows/editor/`, `components/workflows/draft-editor*` and `definition-outline*`, `app/templates/[id]/flow/`, `lib/copy/flow-builder.ts` and `graph-editor.ts`, new copy and promote dialog files under `components/flow-builder/`, `e2e/tests/ui-flow-builder.spec.ts` and `ui-workflow-editor.spec.ts`. Its known items are the editor features 9C did not build (K9-3 to K9-7: C155.4 modules; C155.5's screen editor with C155.10's two full-stack items; C155.7's copy and promote dialogs and canvas diff badges; C150.9 to C150.11's warnings and waitFor editing; Phase 8's R005). Reason: 8C's and 9C's single editor builders both ended partial on exactly these items, and `fix-console` already holds 16 other known items.
   - **`validation-9d`**, alone and last, after `fix-api`: the whole Validation list, the Acceptance and the PROGRESS draft, with any defect it finds fixed test-first in any path. `fix-api` keeps its fixes, the console halves of DS8 and K19, the e2e and CI re-measurement and the review amendments, and runs the gates of what it touched. Reason: 9B and 9C split integration from validation the same way (S4B-1; decision 6 before 9C).
2. **Known items:** 35, as the launch's `KNOWN` (console 16, builder 5, api 11, flow 3). One is HIGH and blocks: **K9-35** (decision 3, `fix-flow`); the rest are MEDIUM or LOW. DS8 (K9-22) and K19 (K9-23) are `fix-api`'s in both halves: their API half must land first, and `fix-api` runs after the console fixers. The Handover's "contest-route words in five locales", "`json-logic.ts`" and "the embed entry's seam" are `fix-flow`'s (their paths are `apps/flow`; 9A amendments 55, 61, 66), not `fix-api`'s.
3. **Engines (C160.9): CI is red on WebKit again, and the cause is WebKit's own report for a native `<select>`, not the flow's code.** CI 42382549247: 17 of 18 jobs green; `e2e` failed after 7.5 minutes because the mocked flow suite had 168 passed and 5 failed, all `[webkit] theme-csp.spec.ts` (three attempts each), each with exactly one `style-src-attr inline`. The full-stack suite therefore did not run in CI, for the second push in a row. S9C-1's change (the theme written to a stylesheet rule) left the failure as it was. The orchestrator's diagnosis:
   - In the CI trace, the console error comes 13 ms after `load`, before the session reply. `applySessionTheme` runs only with a session, so the theme code cannot be the source.
   - The violation event's target is the footer's language `<select>`. The flow's own scripts wrote no style attribute, property, rule or `<style>` element before it (instrumented in WebKit).
   - A script-free page that holds only a CSP (`default-src 'self'`, as a `<meta>` or as a header) and one native `<select>` gets the same single `style-src-attr` report in WebKit. Without the `<select>` there is none, and Chromium reports none.

   **Decided:**
   - **Local WebKit without sudo.** WebKit runs here through a Playwright browser server in Docker, with the image tag equal to `@playwright/test`'s version: `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.64.1-noble` and `run-server`. The suite connects with `PW_TEST_CONNECT_WS_ENDPOINT`, `PW_TEST_CONNECT_EXPOSE_NETWORK='<loopback>'` and `OPENVERIFY_FLOW_E2E_ENGINES=webkit`. It reproduces CI exactly: the same five failures. This is test tooling only (Apache-2.0 Playwright, used unmodified, never shipped, not in CI), run under the slot and e2e locks, and the container is removed after each run.
   - **`fix-flow` fixes it as K9-35 (HIGH), test-first, in `theme-csp.spec.ts`.** The spec measures an engine baseline: a same-origin, script-free control page under the flow's production policy, holding one native `<select>`. Each test then allows only reports that match that baseline (same directive, same target element, empty sample). A new test pins the baseline itself: empty in Chromium and Firefox, and at most the select's `style-src-attr` in WebKit. The allowance therefore never widens silently and is never keyed on an engine's name.
   - **What `fix-flow` must not do:** add `unsafe-inline`, `unsafe-hashes` or a `style-src-attr` source; skip WebKit; or replace the native `<select>`, which would be an accessibility regression.
   - **Who records what.** `a11y-audit` records the red CI line and leaves K9-35 to `fix-flow`. `validation-9d` runs the Docker WebKit command once and records it. The 9D push's `e2e` job is the final proof of C160.9's three-engine line.
   - **Firefox/WebKit install step:** 47 s at this push (29 s at 9B's). It stays in the job, under the three-minute line.
   - **Extra engines:** the launch still passes no `extraEngines`, because that switch means host libraries installed by the owner.
4. **Manual accessibility results:** none exist; the launch passes no `manualAudit`. The statement says "not yet evaluated for conformance" and Phase 9 records "WCAG 2.2 AA audit: automated part done, manual part not run, no conformance claimed" (decisions 55, 56, 71).
5. **CI at the 9C push** (minutes of the timeout): unit-api shards 14.2, 21.3 and 15.3 of 40, merge 0.7 of 20; unit-dashboard shards 6.0 and 10.3 of 20 (the S9C-10 split works: 16.2 before), merge 0.6 of 20; node 11.3 of 25; unit-db 9.1 of 25; integration 15.7 of 30; lint 9.6 of 30; android 9.6 of 45; android-instrumented 10.1 of 60; vision 9.0 of 30; format 2.1 of 10; infra 1.8 of 10; secrets 0.4 of 10; e2e failed at 7.5 of 70 (decision 3). No job is inside the last 25% of its timeout (Phase 8 F08), so the CI file needs no timeout or shard change. The e2e job's full-stack time against its `globalTimeout` (45 minutes) and the job's 70 minutes is still unmeasured in CI since 9A's push (24.6 minutes then): `fix-api` keeps the local estimate (S9C-10) and the 9D push measures it.
6. **The heavy-call slot lock** (S4B-1, 9C rule 23) holds for every 9D agent; the three Stage 4a fixers share slots 1 to 3, the agents that run alone also take slot 0. The API unit suite runs as eight blob shards and a merge.
7. **Owner items, decided:**
   - **P1, `logicalPropertiesConfig()` in the root `eslint.config.js`:** stays the owner's, carried to the release gate, no 9D action. The config-protection hook refuses an agent's or the orchestrator's edit of the root ESLint config, and `packages/config/src/logical-properties.test.ts` already holds every file of `apps/flow/src` and `packages/ui/src` to the rule, so nothing regresses meanwhile.
   - **P2, C148.3's nested quantifiers:** the refusal (`nested_quantifier`) stays as built and the item is closed. Relaxing it in the last Workflow would change the grammar, the golden vectors and both matchers just before Phase 10's Kotlin port must match them (C175.1); every refused pattern has an unnested equivalent (`\d{5}|\d{5}-\d{4}` for `\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?`). Revisit with the Kotlin port.
   - **P3, the CLAUDE.md wording of 9A Stage 1a amendment 15:** stays the owner's (agents and hooks cannot edit CLAUDE.md); carried. Not a blocker: `workspace-conventions.test.ts` and `test_dependencies.py` enforce the rule.
   - **P4, K15:** dropped, as S9C-11 recorded: no record names Phase 6's `FormDialogButton` options. The Acceptance's "Carried (C172, C173)" box records it as dropped with that reason.
   - **P5:** promote passing a theme set's `assets` is built in 9D (`fix-api`, K9-32). The session's `organization-display-name`, the font's `unicode-range` (TH5) and `ProblemMembers` lists are deferred to Phase 10 (a new session member must decode in today's SDK; Phase 10 renders v2 themes natively).
   - **P6:** `apps/flow/src/flow/json-logic.ts` and its test are removed (`fix-flow`, K9-34); the embed entry's seam (9A amendment 66) stays optional.
   - **P7:** the theme asset list route (TS-1) is deferred; the editor already says how it knows an asset.
   - **P8:** device dimensions on the step, verification and check rollups are not built; DA5 refuses those filters in words.
   - **P9:** editing case-action `placement` and `visible-when` in the console is deferred; C172.5 requires the API fields, their enforcement and their display, all built.
   - **P10:** WebKit's host libraries stay an optional owner step (`pnpm --filter @openverify/flow exec playwright install-deps webkit`, needs sudo). Since decision 3, local WebKit runs no longer need them: the Docker browser server covers the theme CSP spec.
   - **Still the owner's:** the manual accessibility pass (a person; decisions 55, 56 and 71) and the fluent review of the four machine-drafted catalogs.
8. **Corrections to the Handover:** `ci.yml` has 15 job keys and 18 runs, not "16 jobs, 19 runs".
9. **Correction to S9C-1.** S9C-1 named `<html>`'s inline style as the cause of CI 42331362147's WebKit failure. Decision 3 shows that the report comes from WebKit's native `<select>`, independent of the theme code. S9C-1's stylesheet rule stays, because no inline style is still the right design, but its stated cause is withdrawn. `docs/themes/README.md` stays as written. `fix-flow` corrects the code comments that repeat the cause (K9-35).

## Workflow 9D Stage 1 amendments (a11y-audit)

Recorded by the Stage 3 verifier from `a11y-audit`'s report (commits `bd131a32`..`3fe4cece`; evidence in `docs/tdd/phase9-a11y-audit.tdd.md`, `docs/accessibility/audit-2026-11-07.md` and `docs/accessibility/statement.md`). Binding on the rest of 9D and on Phases 10 and 11; where an item and an earlier section disagree, the item wins.

1. **The CI matrix of C171.2, as built.** `apps/flow/e2e/step-matrix.spec.ts` is kept, rewritten to run `matrixRuns(process.env)` from `apps/flow/e2e/support/a11y-matrix.ts` over the 39-state catalog `A11Y_STATES` (16 client step types, `CLIENT_STEP_TYPES`), with each state's recipe in `apps/flow/e2e/support/a11y-states.ts` (`STATE_RECIPES`, `openState`, `closeEmbedding`). By default it is the CI matrix: 188 axe runs, every state at 320 px in `en` light, `ar` dark, `en-XA` light and `ar-XB` dark, hosted; every step type's arrival at 768 px in `en`; every step type embedded in `en` at 375 px light. These three choices close what C171.2 left open. `embed.spec.ts`'s 10 per-type axe runs are removed as superseded (9A amendment 70 is closed: all 16 types run embedded). The generator's unit test is `apps/flow/src/a11y-matrix.test.ts`.
2. **The cross product's switch.** `OPENVERIFY_FLOW_A11Y_CROSS_PRODUCT` (`CROSS_PRODUCT_ENV`) set to exactly `1` turns the same spec into the full cross product: 39 states by 84 configurations, 3,276 runs. Any other value, or none, gives the CI matrix. CI never sets it. It runs in four `--shard=i/4` calls, one shard per call, under the e2e lock (two shards in one call came close to the 10-minute cap). Its single 9D run took 1,193 s (19.9 min) with 0 violations and no exception. Rerunning it is a release-gate task, not a CI job. `e2e/README.md` documents the command (`fix-api`).
3. **Dark runs are dark (D1).** Every dark run of the mocked flow suite (`step-matrix`, `screens`, `server-position`, `rtl`) opens with `style-variant=dark` and fails unless `data-openverify-variant="dark"` is set. Emulating `prefers-color-scheme` alone does not count as a dark run, because with no theme set the built-in palette stays light. The first cross-product pass is void for this reason. Only the rerun counts.
4. **The page's variant applies at start (D2, `apps/flow`).** `applyPageVariant` (exported from `src/theme/apply.ts`) applies `style-variant` before any session, so a screen with no session (session ended) keeps dark. Accepted behaviour change: a themed session with only a light palette, opened with `style-variant=dark`, shows the built-in dark loading card until its theme paints.
5. **The shared Checkbox is 24 by 24 CSS px (D4, `packages/ui`; 2.5.8).** It applies to every console checkbox and the flow's consent step. `packages/ui/src/checkbox.test.tsx` gains the size test.
6. **The flow builder's single-pointer path (D5; 2.5.7).** The builder toolbar has "Actions for the selected step" (`aria-haspopup="menu"`, which opens the outline item's menu) and "Tidy up" (`tidyFlowLayout` in `components/flow-builder/flow-model.ts` writes every step's layout). The copy keys are `FLOW_BUILDER_COPY.builder.selectedActions`, `.tidy` and `.edited.tidied`. Both are `fix-builder`'s from now on. A known gap goes to `fix-builder`, test-first: a step selected on the canvas does not move the outline's `aria-selected`.
7. **Toasts never cover focus (D6; 2.4.11).** `ToastProvider` moves its stack between the bottom end and the top end, keeping it clear of the focused control (`placementClearOf`, exported; the section carries `data-placement`). It is `fix-console`'s.
8. **Fallback copy names its language (D7; 3.1.2).** `copyLanguage` (exported from `apps/flow/src/session/session-copy.ts`) reports which locale C162.5's fallback took an operator text from. `ScreenContextValue` carries it, and `ScreenStep` and `LiveScreenStep` take it as an optional prop. Each element that shows such a text (labels, hints, options, texts, steppers, buttons, image alternatives) carries that locale as `lang`. The preview's `StaticStep` passes none, so the preview does not mark fallback text.
9. **Where C171.3's own tests live.**
   - **Flow:** `apps/flow/e2e/wcag-steps.spec.ts` covers 2.4.11, 2.5.8 and 3.2.6 on each of the 16 step types, plus `toMatchAriaSnapshot` of each step screen. Its 16 reviewed `.aria.yml` files are in `wcag-steps.spec.ts-snapshots/`, and any update to them is a reviewed diff, never a blind `--update-snapshots`. `apps/flow/e2e/wcag-own.spec.ts` now uses the shared checks in `apps/flow/e2e/support/wcag-checks.ts` and gains 3.1.2.
   - **Console:** the console's own tests live in the full-stack specs `e2e/tests/ui-a11y-focus.spec.ts` (2.4.11 on 12 pages and with a toast; 3.3.8 password paste), `ui-a11y-analytics.spec.ts` (charts at 320 px and under a dark preference), `ui-a11y-preview.spec.ts` (preview and simulate by keyboard) and `ui-a11y-builder.spec.ts` (2.5.7 on the flow builder canvas), with the helpers `e2e/tests/support/ui-a11y.ts`. They run in the `chromium` project's four shards. `e2e/` normally takes new files only from Stage 1. These five are allowed as new files.
   - **Ownership:** `ui-a11y-builder.spec.ts` is `fix-builder`'s, beside `ui-flow-builder.spec.ts`. The other three specs and `support/ui-a11y.ts` are `fix-console`'s under Stage 4's "the `ui-*` specs with their helpers".
   - **Records test:** `apps/dashboard/src/accessibility-statement-records.test.ts` holds the statement to "not yet evaluated for conformance" until manual results exist.
10. **Existing tests changed (rule 11), all accepted as supersets or as moves:**
    - `step-matrix.spec.ts`: rewritten as above.
    - `embed.spec.ts`: the per-type loop is removed.
    - `screens.spec.ts`, `server-position.spec.ts` and `rtl.spec.ts`: dark runs are now dark.
    - `apps/flow/e2e/support/screens.ts`: `openAt` takes `styleVariant` and the five languages.
    - `wcag-own.spec.ts`: refactored as in item 9.
    - `support/mock-embed.ts`: `startEmbedPage` takes an optional script. The matrix uses it to serve its own embedding page, `MATRIX_EMBED_SCRIPT`, with the `styleVariant` option.
    - `packages/ui/src/checkbox.test.tsx`, `components/toasts.test.tsx` and `components/flow-builder/flow-builder.test.tsx`: tests added.
11. **The e2e cost, accepted.**
    - **Mocked flow suite:** 168 tests in 59 s became 324 tests in about 2.5 min locally, so CI's `e2e` job grows by about 1 to 2 minutes. The 9D push measures it (decision 5 before 9D), and no timeout changes before that measurement.
    - **Full-stack chromium project:** 181 tests in four shards of 47, 45, 45 and 44. `fix-api` rebalances the shards and updates the counts in `e2e/README.md`.
    - **Chromium updates:** the aria snapshots' accessible names come from the browser's ICU, so a Chromium update may need reviewed snapshot updates.
12. **Routed, not built by `a11y-audit` (file owners, test-first):**
    - **`/graph/[id]` sweep at its limit:** the console sweep's `/graph/[id]` test sits at its 180 s limit. axe takes 138 to 147 s on an unpaged result of 718 accounts and 1,282 shared values. `fix-console` pages the table in `components/graph/graph-table.tsx`, keeping "select every account". `fix-api` gives the sweep a smaller query in `e2e/tests/support/console-pages.ts` only if the table is not paged. The limit is never raised, and the test is never retried into a pass. Until one of these lands, the Stage 1 gate stays partial (101 of 102).
    - **`style-variant` in the embed fixture:** `fix-api` makes `e2e/fixtures/embed-page/embed-page.js` forward `style-variant`, so full-stack embed specs can run dark.
13. **What stays as it was.**
    - `e2e/a11y-exceptions.json` stays empty.
    - The statement's claim is exactly "OpenVerify's web verification flow and operator console are not yet evaluated for conformance with WCAG 2.2 level AA."
    - The manual pass (14 rows, results table empty) is the owner's, carried to the release gate. VoiceOver needs an Apple device.
    - WebKit was not run locally and nothing is claimed for it. K9-35 stays `fix-flow`'s, and the Docker WebKit run belongs to `validation-9d` (decision 3 before 9D).
    - The WCAG-EM sample's draw script is recorded in the audit file only. No script is committed. The method was committed before the seed `036ce1fc58bb0c78e914004930fc56e1` was drawn (a mechanical SHA-256 draw: 5 console pages, 5 flow states).
    - The PROGRESS line is the orchestrator's: "WCAG 2.2 AA audit: automated part done, manual part not run, no conformance claimed".

## Workflow 9D review amendments

Recorded by `fix-api`, which ran alone after `fix-flow`, `fix-builder` and `fix-console`, from the four fixers' reports and its own work (evidence in `docs/tdd/phase9-fix-{flow,builder,console,api}.tdd.md`). Each item is binding on `validation-9d` and on Phases 10 and 11; where one disagrees with an earlier section, it wins. No dependency, environment variable or production Kotlin was added by any fixer.

### fix-api

1. **DS8, the API half (K9-22, C167.2, a contract change).** Two dashboard reads, each `users:read`, 404 for another organisation's user:
   - `GET /dashboard/users/:id/roles` answers `userRoleAssignmentsDocumentSchema` as `PUT` stored the assignments.
   - `GET /dashboard/users/:id/permissions` answers the new `userEffectivePermissionsDocumentSchema` (`type: user-effective-permissions`): `organization`, the organisation-level permissions (`users:*`, `roles:*`) once, since only an organisation-wide assignment gives them; `environments`, one entry per environment of the organisation in creation order, each with `environment-id`, `may-enter` (the session's own rule, `mayEnter`: a role there and, in production, `environments:production` held there) and its environment-level `permissions`. Each list is in `PERMISSIONS` order, each permission once, each of its own level (the schema refuses the others). The rules are the session's (`permissionsForEnvironment`, `organizationWidePermissions`), so the built-in admin in a narrower assignment gives nothing.
   - The contract is whole: the schema and `packages/contracts/src/phase9d-contracts.test.ts`, the golden `contracts/fixtures/api/user-effective-permissions.json` in `fixtures.test.ts` and in `ContractFixturesTest.kt`'s `serverOnly`, OpenAPI regenerated (sha256 `e229bd12ee96e903a9a7f38aa429b9f52712288af77d4f5c20933a19175a852f`, stable on two writes). `@openverify/node` exposes no dashboard user call, so it is unchanged. The SDK host tests stay 1,366, CI's floor.
2. **DS8, the console half (K9-22, `fix-api` by the launch's grant).** The roles dialog (`UserRolesButton`) takes `readAssignments` (`readUserAssignments` in `app/users/actions.ts`, `users:read`, a malformed id refused before any call): when the dialog opens it reads the stored assignments and starts each role's choice at its scope, `all` for organisation-wide or the one environment, when that option is offered and grantable; a role assigned in more than one environment starts unchosen and says so in words; a failed read says the current environments could not be read. Without `readAssignments` the old note stays. `/users` links each user's roles cell to `?permissions=<usr_>` (the list's status and cursor kept); a well-formed id reads `GET /dashboard/users/:id/permissions` once and shows `EffectivePermissions` (`components/operators/effective-permissions.tsx`): organisation-wide, then each environment named by kind with "can enter" or "cannot enter" and its permissions; a malformed id reads nothing. Only permission names are shown, so no RSC payload test was needed. Borrowed edits of `fix-console`'s files: `components/operators/users-table.tsx` (`permissionsHref`, `UserManagement.readAssignments`), `app/users/page.tsx`, `lib/api/users.ts` (`getUserRoles`, `getUserPermissions`), `lib/copy/settings.ts` (new keys); existing test changed: `app/users/page.test.tsx`'s mock of `./actions` gains `readUserAssignments`.
3. **K19 (K9-23, C173.1).** The sandbox list gains `SBX-2003` "Corvanel Sectoral Ironworks" (`SANDBOX_WATCHLIST_MAGIC.otherListsEntity`): an entity listed only on OFAC's Sectoral Sanctions Identifications List (`OFAC_LIST_NAMES.consolidated`), programme `SANDBOX-SSI-1`, in every version, its tokens shared with no filler. The four committed versions were regenerated by `fixture-files.test.ts`, never edited by hand: 69 entries and 102 names in version 1. Existing tests changed for the count only (68 to 69 entries, 101 to 102 names): `watchlists/{sync,cli,sandbox,rekey}.test.ts`, `routes/dashboard/watchlist-sources.test.ts`, `seed/dev-seed.test.ts`. No C78.4 constant changed; `benchmark:typecheck` and `benchmark:test` pass. `docs/checks/sandbox-providers.md` lists it. `ui-cases-reports.spec.ts` gains "a listing on a list other than the SDN List reads as what it is", a business screening of the entity showing "Sectoral Sanctions Identifications List (programme SANDBOX-SSI-1)" under Other lists, with axe (3.4 s, inside the spec's 90 s).
4. **Migration 0032 (K9-24, S4B-14), the one migration of the Workflow.** `packages/db/drizzle/0032_phase9d.sql`, generated by drizzle-kit and hand-edited where marked:
   - `analytics_rollup_state.closed_through` (date): the last local day closed. `refreshEnvironment` and `rebuildEnvironment` never recompute it or an earlier day (`openFrom`) and write the later of the stored day and the day before the first open one (`closedThrough`), so a raised `analytics-settle-days` never reopens a closed day (AA6) and a lowered one closes more days. The migration clears every state's `outcomes_as_of`, so the next pass recomputes every open day with the two changes below.
   - **The reserved start (AL6).** `ANALYTICS_FLOW_START_STEP_ID = '$start'` (`@openverify/core`, outside the step id form): the rollup writes a transition from `$start` into each inquiry's first person-visible step, and `analytics_transition_daily_from_step_id_format` admits it. `funnelFigures` counts these into the entry step (`AnalyticsFunnelFigures.entries`), so the entry step has a `step-share`; the start is never a served transition, so the funnel contract is unchanged. Days closed before 0032 hold no start, which leaves the entry step's share out on a window reaching them.
   - **Reasons per use (AL7).** `analytics_check_reason_daily` gains `verification_type` and `requirement` (NOT NULL, in its key); the rollup writes them, `AnalyticsCheckReasonDailyRow` carries them, `checkFigures` refuses a reason of another check, type or requirement, and the verifications answer passes each use its own reasons. An earlier row took them from its check's results of the same day, zone, source and version when those named exactly one; a row they could not attribute was deleted, never guessed.
   - **W3 (KW2).** The webhooks' trigger is `openverify_webhooks_set_updated_at`: an update that changes nothing but `failure_alert_notified_at` keeps `updated_at`; any other change moves it as before.
   - Device dimensions are not added (P8). Existing tests changed: `packages/core/src/analytics/metrics.test.ts` (the entry step now has a step share, the transitions into a step count the start), `apps/api/src/services/analytics/rollup-sql.test.ts` (transitions include the start; reasons keyed by type and requirement), `packages/db/src/phase9b-analytics-tables.test.ts` (the reason table's columns); the core goldens `funnel.json` and `checks.json` were regenerated by their own writer (the start rows, `entries`, the entry step's share; the reasons' two members), the other goldens unchanged.
   - **PGlite migration time**, under the e2e lock (cold: an empty cache directory; warm: the cached template): before (0031 last) cold 5,234, 5,277 and 5,112 ms, warm 1,201, 1,206 and 1,404 ms, a second copy 934 to 1,087 ms, load 0.5 to 1.6; after (0032) cold 5,349, 5,166 and 5,775 ms, warm 1,209, 1,181 and 1,183 ms, a second copy 932 to 1,053 ms, load 0.8 to 1.2. No hook budget moves.
   - **Benchmarks.** An analytics benchmark seed taken before 0032 is re-seeded (S4B-17); 0032 changes no table the benchmarks write a fixed time through (S9C-14).
5. **The sender name everywhere (K9-25, C169.8).** `fromEnvironment(message, settings)` (`apps/api/src/mail/sender-name.ts`) adds the environment's `mail-sender-name`, leaving the member out when none is set; the case notification emails, the workflow email step (real deliveries, not the sandbox record), the email one-time codes and the handoff emails pass through it, as the webhook alert already did. Invitations and account notices belong to no environment and keep the installation's From. One test per sender.
6. **Preferences for case notifications (K9-26, C169.7).** Every case notification goes through `notifyByPreferencesInTx` (`services/cases/notification-channels.ts`; `notify` in `writes.ts` and `notifyUser` in `queues/work.ts` call it): for each recipient and kind, `notificationPreferencesOf` (the alert's reader) decides the console notification (`insertUserNotifications`, new in `@openverify/db`) and the link-only email (`queueCaseNotificationEmails`). **Behaviour change, accepted:** with the contract's default (console on, email off), Phase 8's assigned, mentioned and SLA-breached emails are no longer sent until a user turns email on for that kind; a template version's `notify-on-create` list has no preference kind and is emailed as before. Borrowed edit of `fix-console`'s files (DS17's sentence would otherwise be false): `lib/copy/settings.ts`' `appliedNote` and its test in `app/notifications/page.test.tsx`.
7. **The unsettled remainder (K9-27): documented, not built.** `docs/analytics/README.md`, "What suppression does not cover", states that the remainder is added by the API (`settledSeries`, `settledGroups`), so another caller of `suppressAnalyticsTable` must add it itself, and that a relation broken the other way on a day is not declared; suppression is not weakened.
8. **FE2 (K9-28, C151.10).** `startInquiry` applies the after-start window in its transaction (`applyStartWindowInTx`, `flow-engine/expiry.ts`, the engine's rule), so `openInquiry` and `getFlowSession`'s fallback move the expiry as the engine's start does, never reviving an expired inquiry; `redeemHandoff` extends the expiry with `extendExpiryOnResumeInTx`, as a one-time link's redemption does. One test per path under the injected clock.
9. **The list export (K9-29, C166.3, a contract change).** `EXPORT_PROBLEM_STATUSES.export_list_kind_refused` (422), its golden problem `contracts/fixtures/problems/exports/export-list-kind-refused.json`, answered at `/data/attributes/filter/filter[list-id]` for a list kind with no importer. `EXPORT_CSV_COLUMNS` holds no `lists` entry: a list is written under its kind's import columns, and a test holds every `IMPORTER_LIST_KINDS` kind to `importColumns(kind)`. Existing tests changed: `phase9b-problems.test.ts` (the code joins the frozen list) and `phase9b-exports.test.ts` (the documented types). No dashboard `Record` is typed by `ExportProblemCode`, so the console shows the problem's detail as before. OpenAPI unchanged by it; no Android fixture list change (problems folders are not in it).
10. **K9-30, decided: documented.** The rotated (new) key's `expires-at` is `null` until set with `POST /api-keys/:id/expire`; the old key's own resource serves its end as `expires-at`, equal to `meta.previous-expires-at` (`docs/settings/api-keys.md`). No contract change.
11. **K9-31.** `docs/settings/README.md` links the delivery search (`webhooks.md#searching-deliveries`).
12. **The promote and theme assets (K9-32, P5).** `promoteTemplate` passes `assets` (storage, keyring, ids) to `promoteThemeReferenceInTx`, so a set with a logo or font is copied with its assets as stored (re-sealed under a new id, never re-uploaded); still only for a caller with `theme-sets:write` in the target, recorded in the promote's `copied` and audit entry. No contract change.
13. **R003.** One rule, `isCalendarDate` (`flow-engine/calendar-date.ts`), for a screen's date and a `date` field: the day must round-trip, so `2023-02-29` and `2024-04-31` are refused.
14. **The embed page (9D Stage 1 amendment 12).** `e2e/fixtures/embed-page/embed-page.js` forwards `style-variant`; `flow-embed.spec.ts` embeds the flow dark and reads `data-openverify-variant="dark"`.
15. **Docs.** The pages the console fixers named are updated (`docs/flows/preview-and-simulate.md`, `docs/settings/mfa.md`, `docs/reports/bulk.md`, `docs/analytics/README.md`, `docs/analytics/exports.md`), with `docs/settings/{roles,sender-name,webhooks}.md` for 9D's API behaviour, `e2e/README.md` (below) and `apps/dashboard/README.md` (borrowed: what `fix-api` built of the console halves).
16. **Routed, not fixed (`fix-api`'s report has the diagnosis):** `e2e/tests/flow-bundle.spec.ts` failed once under load in `fix-flow`'s run (passed alone, and in `fix-api`'s gate in 19 s). Read from the code, not yet proven by a test, the likely cause is the flow's auto-capture: the guidance judges a sampled frame, then `captureFrame` draws the video's current frame after the detector's latency, so under load the uploaded photo can come from the head-turn clip's next turn (its holds are 1.6 s, its turns 0.3 s), which the server refuses for its pose. The fix is in `apps/flow` (outside `fix-api`'s paths): keep a full-resolution copy of the frame each guidance tick samples and upload that copy on an auto-capture. `validation-9d` may make it test-first; it is never retried into a pass.

### fix-flow

1. **K9-35 (C160.9), WebKit's own report.** `theme-csp.spec.ts` measures an engine baseline on a script-free control page of the flow's origin (`/__csp-control`, served under `buildContentSecurityPolicy({ apiOrigin })`, one native `<select>`), and each of its tests allows only flow-page reports that match a baseline entry: the same directive, blocked value and target element, an empty sample. A test pins the baseline per engine: empty in Chromium and Firefox, at most WebKit's `{style-src-attr, inline, SELECT, ''}`; another proves a report on another element, with a sample or under another directive is never allowed. The CSP, the native select and the WebKit project are unchanged. "Follows prefers-color-scheme" settles two frames after its media change, because WebKit fires the change in its next rendering update. S9C-1's stated cause is corrected in `apply.ts`' comments; the Docker WebKit recipe is in the spec's header, `playwright.config.ts` and `apps/flow/e2e/README.md`.
2. **R005 (C157.1).** The flow server opens a dist file before its head, so an unopenable file is a 500; it streams through `stream.pipeline`; a dropped client closes the file; a read error after the head cuts the response and logs `{entry: 'error', status: 500}` once; a client that went away is not logged as an error.
3. **K9-33 (C128.7, C161).** The contest route's words are catalog messages `contest.heading`, `.intro`, `.host`, `.link` and `.contact` in the five catalogs, machine-drafted outside English, with the host and the contact each in its own `<bdi>`; `contest-route-words.ts` is removed. Borrowed: `contracts/fixtures/i18n/{en,es,fr,de,ar}.json` regenerated by the flow's `i18n:export` (the catalog version and `consent-texts.json` unchanged).
4. **K9-34.** `apps/flow/src/flow/json-logic.ts` and its test are removed; `packages/flow-spec/src/v2/test-v1-walk.ts` is the only copy of the v1 rules C151.3's equivalence test uses.
5. **R007 (C150.1, ADR 0008).** `apps/flow/src/client-bundles.test.ts` proves, against a positive control build, that neither the hosted flow (with its preview entry) nor `@openverify/js` carries the workflow-engine evaluator.
6. **Not built:** the embed entry's seam (9A amendment 66) stays optional (P6).

### fix-builder

1. **C155.4 modules (K9-3).** Editor macros in `components/flow-builder/flow-modules.ts`: each verification module inserts an optional explanation screen, the step and an exhausted fallback screen, both leading where the place led (C150.6's default `onFallback: review`); success and failed insert a terminal step only on a port leading nowhere; country select inserts a screen with a `country_select` bound to `address_country_code` and a branch with one route (`US`, `onNull: else`). A refusal is `checkFlowGraph`'s own placement error on the inserted steps. Each says what it inserts before inserting (C155.11), offered from the outline by keyboard and from "Actions for the selected step" by a single pointer.
2. **C150.9 to C150.11 and `waitFor` (K9-6).** `BranchRoutes` shows `client_signal_before_screen` and `tipping_off_route` in words (never as errors); `waitFor` is edited outside the JSON, one checkbox per fact that can be unknown, the longest wait 1 to `FLOW_V2_CAPS.waitMaxSeconds` (30), refused in words outside it; a new wait starts at 5 seconds (provisional, ours: two session polls and a margin), and removing the last fact removes it.
3. **C155.7 (K9-5).** The flow page reads the draft's diff against the current version and the builder shows it as canvas badges, outline labels (", added", ", changed") and the list "Compared with version N", as saved when the page opened. Copy and promote are dialogs on the template page for its current version, with `inquiry-templates:write` (borrowed: `app/templates/[id]/page.tsx` and its test); promote targets the session's other environments and its refusals are in words.
4. **The screen editor, partial (K9-4, C155.5).** `screen-model.ts` and `screen-editor.tsx` edit a screen's components by buttons (move up, down, into, out, add from the library, remove), every result checked by `screenStepSchema`; Data, Logic (a visibility rule in the condition builder's new `visibility` position, over `screen.<field>`, an undescribed field counted as PII) and Design (where the look comes from); `FlowBuilder` takes the version's `fieldSchemas`, and a new route's empty condition reads "no comparison yet". C155.10's two full-stack items are in `ui-flow-builder.spec.ts`, keyboard only. **Not built:** the drag-and-drop screen canvas, and per-component style overrides with live contrast, which need a style member in flow-spec 2's component schema (a C148.2 contract change). Both are carried to Phase 10 with the owner's decision on that member; the Acceptance's builder box records them as partial.
5. **Phase 8's R005 (K9-7).** The workflow palette gains "Loop over a list", "Loop while a condition holds", "Map a list" and modules (one per workflow given in `DraftEditor`'s `modules`, else one entry refused with its reason); new loops carry placeholders the author replaces (items `trigger.items`, condition `false`, `max-iterations` 10, provisional, within the contract); "Lead next back to <loop>" adds the back edge into `continue`; the condition builder gains the `while` position and the list predicates `some`, `all` and `none` with their on-null. Existing tests changed minimally: `palette-model.test.ts`, `palette.test.tsx`, `condition-model.test.ts`. **Not wired:** `app/workflows/[id]/draft/page.tsx` (`fix-console`'s) passes no `modules`, because no read says which workflows start with `module-input`; until a read exposes it, a module step is written in the definition JSON. Carried to Phase 10.
6. **R010.** A step selected on the canvas moves the outline's `aria-selected` and tab stop (`OutlineTree.revealItem`).

### fix-console

1. **The error page in the real-header spec (K9-15, closes S9C-7).** `/error-probe` throws inside `renderPage` for a signed-in operator only when `OPENVERIFY_MODE` is exactly `development` (the e2e stack's mode; the compose dashboard sets none, so it is the 404 page there and calls nothing). `ui-csp.spec.ts` checks the error page's policy byte for byte, the nonce on every server script and no violation; the console sweep checks `/error-probe` as the error page it is (500, the boundary's alert, then axe; borrowed edit of `fix-api`'s `a11y-console.spec.ts`).
2. **Refusals in words.** A 403 `no_environment_role` at sign-in, after the second factor (`/login?reason=no-environment-role`) and at the environment switch, never naming a role or an environment (K9-9: `LoginFailure` and `MfaLoginFailure` gain `no_environment_role`, recognised for that code only); a bulk job's codes as "words (code)", an unknown one "<code>: not described" (K9-21); a case template publish problem at a layout module read as "Layout module N (its name)", from one read of the draft (K9-17).
3. **Settings and access.** The 9A and 9B settings in the read-only view (K9-10); a security key or passkey step-up in the reset and reactivate dialogs, verified at `POST /dashboard/sessions/current/step-up` before acting (K9-11, `startStepUpWebauthn`); each webhook's name in the list's first column, "Webhook" (K9-12); `consolePublicOrigin` and `DASHBOARD_PUBLIC_URL_ENV` removed, unused (K9-13); `injectNonceCsp` and `nonceCsp` removed (K9-14).
4. **Data and reports.** `comparison-source` in words beside each compared field (`InquiryComparisonEvidence.sources`; a missing or unknown source reads "not recorded"; K9-16); "Download this view as CSV" on every analytics view, built in the browser from the view's own suppressed tables (a hidden cell as its mark, the per-reviewer table left out with a line saying so, cells neutralised against formulas; K9-18); saved views hold a transaction field filter only on a key some type declares and none declares as a string (`SlotData.nonPersonalTransactionFields`, `null` when not every type was read; K9-19, closes DA4); a `reports:write` holder without `report-templates:read` gets a "Report template ID" field (K9-20); a relation's target kind in words (K9-1); a redacted transaction's relations stay, by id (K9-2); simulate's two known differences listed beside the path (K9-8).
5. **R001 and R009.** The public `/csp-reports` limiter refuses a new address past 10,000 held in one minute (429, `Retry-After: 60`) while held ones keep their counts (DS4's numbers, provisional); the graph result table pages 25 rows at a time with "select every account" still taking the whole result and 24 px checkboxes, so the sweep's smaller query is not needed (9D Stage 1 amendment 12 settled).
6. **Existing tests changed:** `lib/csp/policy.test.ts` (the removed origin), `lib/database-evidence.test.ts` (the `sources` member), `app/exports/bulk-reports/page.test.tsx` (the template ID field replaces the defect's "No active report template"); `toasts.test.tsx` and the others gained tests only.
7. **Optional, not done:** exporting `REPORT_BULK_FILE_CODES` from the contracts (the console types the job's own codes by hand, `components/analytics/bulk-code-words.ts`).

### The e2e suite and CI, measured (fix-api, rule 7 (c))

- **Locally, one fresh stack, every step under the e2e lock** (load 1.3 to 10.6 for the mocked suite, 1.9 to 3.1 after): the mocked flow suite 328 tests in 1.8 minutes (111 s); the build 28 s and the stack 97 s; the four `--project=chromium` shards 47, 46, 46 and 44 tests (183) in 229, 293, 271 and 370 s; the console sweep 104 tests (98 pages under axe, the real-header spec's 6) in 294 s, its `/graph/[id]` 10.9 s against 138 to 147 s before R009. Every call exited 0. The four shards stay: the longest is 6.2 minutes, under the 8-minute line, so nothing is rebalanced; `e2e/README.md` holds the counts, the cross-product switch and the Docker WebKit recipe.
- **CI 42382549247 (the 9C push)**, minutes of the timeout: unit-api 14.2, 21.3 and 15.3 of 40; unit-dashboard 6.0 and 10.3 of 20; node 11.3 of 25; unit-db 9.1 of 25; integration 15.7 of 30; lint 9.6 of 30; android 9.6 of 45; android-instrumented 10.1 of 60; vision 9.0 of 30; format 2.1, infra 1.8, secrets 0.4 of 10; the merges 0.6 and 0.7 of 20. None is inside the last 25% of its timeout, so those jobs are unchanged. The e2e job failed after 7.5 minutes (WebKit, K9-35), so its full-stack time is still unmeasured in CI.
- **R006, decided:** the local full-stack run is about 26 minutes (the stack and the five calls); at 9A's CI ratio of 1.45 that projects to about 38 minutes, inside the last 25% of the old 45-minute `globalTimeout`. The `globalTimeout` of `e2e/playwright.config.ts` is 60 minutes and the e2e job's `timeout-minutes` 80 (at least 20 above it, for the installs, the fixtures and the mocked suite), held by `infra/tests/ci-workflow.test.sh`. The run is not split: the console sweep needs the objects every chromium spec makes on its stack. The 9D push measures it; past 75% of 60, Phase 10's first integration agent splits or raises it with the figure.

### The Validation order

Unchanged from S9C-13 and S9C-15; `validation-9d` also runs the Docker WebKit command of K9-35 once (decision 3 before 9D) and records it.

### Validation as run (validation-9d)

Recorded by `validation-9d`, which ran alone and last on `cf34a038`. Each call, with its exit code, seconds and load, is in `docs/tdd/phase9-validation-9d.tdd.md`. Binding on Phases 10 and 11.

1. **The tree.** `e56e4e9c..cf34a038` holds 1,118 Phase 9 commits, 177 of them after 9C's `017d8e36`. No defect in the repository was found, so `validation-9d` made no code commit. `git status` shows only the owner's untracked note.
2. **The list.** Every step ran in the order of S9C-13 and S9C-15, each call in the foreground, and every call exited 0 except one (item 3).
   - Unit tests: 34,412 in 17 packages, each at or above 80%. The API has 692 files and 9,428 tests at 94.39%, run as 8 shards and a merge. The dashboard has 579 files and 6,715 tests at 95.59%, in one call of 362 s at a load of 1.4 to 7.2.
   - Integration: db 78, graph 13 (template built in 14.8 s), and the api shards 89, 99 and 79.
   - Vision: 1,471 and 1,130 tests, at 97.48% coverage.
   - Android: 1,366 SDK host tests, equal to CI's floor.
   - OpenAPI: sha256 `e229bd12…a175a852f`, stable, with no diff in `docs/workflows`.
   - Images: apps, vision and code-runner.
   - gitleaks: 6,003 commits, no leaks.
3. **`infra/tests/install-gitleaks.test.sh` and GitHub's release host.**
   - The test downloads gitleaks' pinned release. Its six download checks failed because `github.com/gitleaks/gitleaks/releases/download/v8.30.1/` answered HTTP 504 from 08:59Z, while `github.com` itself answered 200.
   - The host flapped for about an hour, and the test ran nine times with its code unchanged. Eight runs failed, each failed check on that 504. The ninth (09:57Z) passed 9 of 9 and exited 0.
   - The test is unchanged and is not an exception. The 9D push's `infra` job runs it again.
4. **The e2e gate.**
   - The mocked flow suite: 328 tests in 111 s, in Chromium plus Firefox's theme spec.
   - **The Docker WebKit run of K9-35** (decision 3): `theme-csp.spec.ts` had 7 of 7 tests passing in WebKit, with its baseline pin included. The container was removed in the same call. This is local evidence only; C160.9's three-engine line in CI is proven by the 9D push's `e2e` job.
   - On one fresh stack (up in 90 s): the four chromium shards had 47, 46, 46 and 44 tests (183) in 229, 309, 270 and 377 s. The console sweep then had 104 tests in 307 s, with `/graph/[id]` taking 11.1 s (R009) and `/error-probe` 0.9 s (K9-15).
   - That is 26.4 minutes at a load of 1.5 to 3.7, matching `fix-api`'s figure, so R006's 60-minute `globalTimeout` stands. No shard is rebalanced.
5. **The routed `flow-bundle.spec.ts` item** ("### fix-api" item 16) is not built. The spec passed in shard 4 in 15.2 s, so the list found no defect. The suspected cause (the auto-capture uploads the live frame, not the judged one) is carried to Phase 10's flow work, test-first, with a unit test that an auto-capture uploads the frame it judged.
6. **Benchmarks** (reported only, under the e2e lock):
   - **Workflow engine.** Effectful steps ran at 19, 47 and 49 steps a second at 1, 8 and 32 clients; control flow at 95, 205 and 208. Load 1.4 to 2.4.
   - **Analytics.** The seed was taken again after 0032: `--teardown`, then three `--seed` calls of 455, 425 and 473 s. On 1 M inquiries the build took 369 s with no failed environment. The 5-minute refresh of 2 days took 55 ms, and the pass after midnight over 30 days took 30.1 s. Reads at 30, 90 and 400 days took at most 33, 51 and 396 ms. Load 1.4 to 2.2.
   - **The container** `openverify-analytics-benchmark` is stopped with this seed.
